*Tarandeep Singh Mandhiratta,ANK Zaman,Abdul-Rahman Mawlood-Yunis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个基于Mask RCNN的神经网络模型,用于从2D平面图中自动提取照明信息,包括检测照明符号、识别灯具类型及提取关联文本,实现了bbox_mAP=0.7596和segm_mAP=0.7111的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在实现高效楼层设计,自动确定每层所需的灯具数量与类型,并估算平面图的电力需求,从而帮助建筑和施工等行业缩短设计时间、创建高效工作流。

Method: 以Mask RCNN作为基础模型;对平面图图像进行标注并转换为COCO数据格式用于模型训练,以完成对照明符号的检测、灯具类型分类及关联文本的提取。

Result: 模型在bbox_mAP和segm_mAP上分别达到0.7596和0.7111;在不同IoU阈值下表现良好,bbox_mAP 50达到0.9850,segm_mAP 75达到0.9219。

Conclusion: 该模型能够自动检测平面图中的MEP(机械、电气、管道)对象,是构建高效建筑设计和电力估算工具的第一步,有望为相关行业带来显著的设计效率提升。

Abstract: This research developed a neural network-based model to extract various information from 2D floor plans. We detect lighting symbols, identify the appropriate type of light, and extract the associated texts with lights. The study aims to enable efficient floor designing and determining the number and type of lights needed per floor, i.e., allow efficient design and estimate the power requirement of the floor plan. The model was developed using Mask RCNN as the base. The images were annotated and converted into a Coco data format for training the model. The model achieved bbox\_mAP and segm\_mAP values of 0.7596 and 0.7111, respectively. It also performed well at different IoU thresholds, i.e., with bbox\_mAP 50 and segm\_mAP 75 values of 0.9850 and 0.9219, respectively. The developed model will help various industries, such as architecture and construction, to improve design time and create efficient workflows by automatically detecting Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) objects from floor plans, and it is the first step towards building tools that will help energy-efficient building design.

</details>


### [55] [TRIAGE: Risk-Controlled Pseudo-Label Admission for Annotation-Efficient Semi-Supervised Retinal OCT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14321)
*Md Ashraful Hossen Akash,Shyla Afroge,Abdullah Al Mamun,Md. Kishor Morol,Tze Hui Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRIAGE，一种面向 OCT 扫描分类的风险受控半监督框架。它引入患者级共形风险控制器与不对称代价矩阵，联合层次化分类器、患者分组共形风险控制器及上下文感知 Transformer 教师三个模块，在有限标注下显著提升分类性能并降低低分级（under-grading）错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT 作为先进视网膜疾病诊断成像模态，因专家标注成本高昂而缺乏自动化。现有半监督学习方法虽可利用未标注 B-scan 缓解标注不足问题，但大多数伪标签生成技术仅基于预测置信度，未考虑不同类型错误之间的不对称性（例如视网膜疾病中漏诊/低分级与过度分级的临床代价不同）。

Method: TRIAGE 由三个核心模块组成：(1) 层次化分类器，支持疾病亚型的部分异常监督；(2) 患者分组共形风险控制器，采用原始-对偶（primal-dual）覆盖控制，并结合不对称代价矩阵进行风险控制；(3) 上下文感知 Transformer 教师，用于跨切片（cross-slice）验证。

Result: 在 Noor Eye Hospital 数据集（16,822 张 B-scans、161 名患者、554 个 volume，测试集为未见患者）上，仅用 20% 标注数据达到 89.66% 扫描级准确率、0.8805 macro-F1、0.9641 macro-AUC，低分级率 8.34%；仅用 5% 标注数据时保持 76.88% 准确率和 0.1656 低分级率。相比六种最先进的半监督方法显著更优；消融实验证明各模块的贡献，低分级率相较于固定阈值方法提升 42.7%。在 OCT-C8 数据集上，以 1% 标注数据实现 3 类分类 98.00% 准确率，以 10% 标注数据实现 8 类分类 95.94% 准确率。

Conclusion: TRIAGE 通过将患者级共形风险控制与不对称代价矩阵引入半监督学习，有效解决了伪标签置信度阈值忽略误差不对称性的问题，在极低标注比例（1%–20%）下均取得优异性能，为临床 OCT 自动化诊断提供了可靠且风险可控的解决方案。

Abstract: The advanced retinal disease diagnosing imaging modality, optical coherence tomography (OCT), encounters a lack of automation because of the high expenses for annotations performed by specialists. The use of SSL solves the problem of insufficient annotations using unlabeled B-scans; however, most of the current techniques for generating pseudo-labels are based on prediction confidence without considering the asymmetry between different types of errors. This paper proposes TRIAGE, a risk-controlled semi-supervised framework for OCT scans classification, which uses the concept of a patient-level conformal risk controller with an asymmetric cost matrix. TRIAGE unites three crucial modules: a hierarchical classifier that is capable of working with partially abnormal supervision of the disease subtypes, a patient-grouped conformal risk controller with primal-dual coverage control, and a context-aware Transformer teacher for cross-slice verification. On the dataset from Noor Eye Hospital (16,822 B-scans, 161 patients, and 554 volumes) with a test set of unseen patients, TRIAGE demonstrates 89.66% scan-level accuracy, 0.8805 macro-F1, 0.9641 macro-AUC, and an 8.34% under-grading rate when using only 20% of the labeled data. With only 5% of the labeled data, TRIAGE keeps 76.88% accuracy and a 0.1656 under-grading rate. Compared with the other six state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, TRIAGE significantly outperforms them with ablation study demonstrating the contribution of each module in the overall framework performance (by 42.7% in terms of under-grading rate comparing to fixed threshold methods). TRIAGE demonstrates 98.00% accuracy for 3-class classification with 1% labeled data and 95.94% accuracy for 8-class classification with 10% labeled data on the OCT-C8 dataset.

</details>


### [56] [Weakly Supervised Polar Low Segmentation in Sentinel-1 SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14366)
*Andrea Federici,Jakob Grahn,Giacomo Boracchi,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CREST，一种仅使用图像级标签的弱监督语义分割（WSSS）框架，用于在 Sentinel-1 SAR 影像中分割极地低压。基于 Adversarial Erasing（AER）思想，通过 CORE 模块（约束序贯区域扩张）编码极地低压的空间连通性先验，并用动态引导（DB）损失将挖掘顺序视为标签可靠性代理，从而抑制 AER 采集的无关背景特征，生成多类、可靠性分级的掩码，在 SAR、乳腺超声和 PASCAL VOC 人像数据上均优于标准 AER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极地低压是高纬度快速形成的强海上气旋，深度学习可在 SAR 图像中检测它们，但像素级分割仍是开放难题：缺乏像素级掩码用于训练，且极地低压边界本身主观、弥散，专家标注也不一致。因此需要在仅有图像级标签的条件下生成分割掩码。

Method: CREST 基于对抗擦除（AER）管线，迭代挖掘判别区域、擦除并重训练分类器以揭示互补线索，将其作为分割伪标签。为克服标准 AER 会累积无关背景特征、降低伪标签质量的问题，提出两个组件：（i）Constrained Ordinal Region Expansion（CORE）模块，编码极地低压的空间连通性先验，从高置信种子出发约束区域扩张；（ii）Dynamic Bootstrapping（DB）损失，把挖掘顺序视为标签可靠性代理，衰减来自较晚挖掘、噪声较大区域的监督。最终输出多类（而非二元）掩码，类别表示各区域被赋予的可靠性等级。

Result: 在 Sentinel-1 SAR 数据上，CREST 比标准 AER 更贴近气旋结构，并返回多类而非二元掩码，类别指示各区域的可靠性。在满足相同连通性先验且具备稠密掩码的 BUS-UCLM 乳腺超声与 PASCAL VOC 人像两个数据集上，相同设置下 CREST 均优于等价 AER 管线。

Conclusion: CREST 通过空间连通性先验约束与基于挖掘顺序的动态监督加权，显著缓解了 WSSS 中伪标签的背景噪声问题，使仅凭图像级标签即可生成结构更贴合且带可靠性分级的极地低压分割掩码，并可在其他同质先验目标上泛化。

Abstract: Polar lows are intense maritime cyclones that form rapidly at high latitudes. Deep learning can detect them in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery, but pixel-level segmentation remains an open challenge. No pixel-level masks are available for training, and a polar low's extent is inherently subjective, with diffuse boundaries that even experts delineate inconsistently. We propose Constrained Region Erasing with Soft Targets (CREST), a Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation (WSSS) framework that generates masks solely from image-level labels. Our approach builds on Adversarial Erasing (AER), which iteratively mines discriminative regions, erases them, and retrains a classifier to reveal complementary cues that become pseudo-labels for segmentation. However, standard AER also collects irrelevant background features, degrading pseudo-label quality. CREST addresses this with (i) a Constrained Ordinal Region Expansion (CORE) module that encodes the spatial-connectedness prior of polar lows, constraining region expansion from a high-confidence seed, and (ii) a Dynamic Bootstrapping (DB) loss that treats the mining order as a proxy for label reliability, attenuating supervision from noisier, later-mined regions. On Sentinel-1 SAR data, CREST follows the cyclone structure more closely than standard AER, and returns a multi-class rather than binary mask whose classes indicate the reliability assigned to each region. We further evaluate on BUS-UCLM breast ultrasound and PASCAL VOC person data, whose targets satisfy the same connectedness prior but come with the dense masks the SAR data lacks. On both datasets, CREST performs better than the equivalent AER pipeline under identical settings.

</details>


### [57] [GBU-Palm: A Multimodal Video Dataset and Benchmark for Palm Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14389)
*Yingjie Ma,Zitong Yu,Wei Jia,Ajay Kumar,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GBU-Palm，一个大规模多模态视频掌纹呈现攻击检测（PAD）数据集与基准，包含 105 名受试者、210 只手掌、21,326 段视频、六种采集环境，以及 6,310 个同步 RGB-NIR 样本，涵盖真实、打印与回放三类呈现。通过泄漏受控协议与四种代表性视频架构的评测，揭示了跨环境迁移下显著的架构依赖性性能退化，以及 RGB-NIR 融合并非始终优于纯 RGB 输入的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掌纹 PAD 数据集多受限于静态图像、苛刻的采集条件或缺乏多模态视频数据，导致在不同环境、模态与攻击类型下难以进行系统性评测。为填补这一空白，作者构建了统一且具挑战性的跨环境、多模态视频基准，以支撑鲁棒掌纹 PAD 方法的发展与评估。

Method: 作者采集并标定了 GBU-Palm 数据集（含 6 种环境、真实/打印/回放三类呈现、同步 RGB-NIR 视频）；设计了将掌纹身份与攻击血统分离的泄漏受控协议；在环境匹配与留出环境（held-out-environment）两种设置下评测了四种代表性视频架构，并通过真接受/真拒绝/假接受/假拒绝分解、频谱掩蔽、时间顺序干预、冻结骨干 NIR 探针等手段分析模型行为。

Result: 评测结果显示：(1) 环境迁移下存在显著的架构依赖性性能下降；(2) RGB-NIR 融合并不总是优于仅用 RGB 输入；(3) 行为分析揭示了不同架构的失败模式差异与证据利用方式的差异。

Conclusion: GBU-Palm 为跨环境场景下鲁棒多模态掌纹 PAD 提供统一且具挑战性的评测基准，并提示研究者在融合多模态信息与设计跨域鲁棒架构时需谨慎权衡，其公开的数据集与协议可推动该领域进一步发展。

Abstract: Existing palm presentation attack detection (PAD) datasets are often limited by static imagery, restricted acquisition conditions, or insufficient multimodal video data, hindering systematic evaluation across environments, modalities, and attack types. We present GBU-Palm, a large-scale multimodal video dataset and benchmark containing 21,326 videos from 105 subjects and 210 palms across six acquisition environments, including bona fide, Print, and Replay presentations, with 6,310 synchronized RGB-NIR samples. We construct leakage-controlled protocols that separate palm identity and attack lineage and benchmark four representative video architectures under environment-matched and held-out-environment settings. Results reveal substantial architecture-dependent degradation under environmental shift and show that RGB-NIR fusion does not consistently outperform RGB-only input. We further analyze model behavior through true accept (TA), true reject (TR), false accept (FA), and false reject (FR) decomposition, spectral masking, temporal-order intervention, and frozen-backbone NIR probing, revealing distinct failure patterns and evidence utilization across architectures. GBU-Palm provides a unified and challenging benchmark for developing and evaluating robust multimodal palm PAD methods under cross-environment conditions.

</details>


### [58] [Can We Defend Against AI-Generated Video Attacks on Real-World Crisis Events? A Systematic Evaluation of Detectors, Generators and Social Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14391)
*Shuo Liang,Yixing Ma,Pengfei Zhou,Xingyan Chen,Zihan Mei,Manting Li,Feihan Chen,Zhiwen Wang,Bin Xu,Haotian Zhang,Jiajun Song,Shiya Su,Run Liu,Zhenghang Ni,Yifa Yu,Jintao Hong,Bolong Feng,Yifei Liu,Zirui Zhang,Jingxuan Zhang,Songlin Zhao,Yifan Bai,Kang Tan,Yizhe Liu,Junhao Du,Yongtao Ge,Zhaopan Xv,Xinyuan Zhang,Mengru Ma,Chunhua Shen,Wei Wang,Yang You,Zheng Zhu,Kaipeng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-Bench，一个以真实视频为锚点的AI生成视频检测基准，包含17,886个视频（1,830个真实锚点视频，覆盖10个社会风险类别，以及来自4个开源和5个闭源生成器的16,056个生成片段）。基于该基准的评估发现：三类检测器（传统检测器、零样本多模态模型、微调的MLLM）都无法稳定泛化；生成属性对不同检测器家族影响不同，而源级检测模式在采样种子间保持稳定；能够欺骗人类的视频同样难以被检测器识别，且社交传播会使检测更加困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器可以制造关于战争、灾难、公共紧急事件等现实危机的逼真视频，构成重大错误信息风险。然而现有基准缺乏对检测器与生成器在此类情境下行为的充分证据，包括可检测性如何随生成条件变化、人们如何感知生成视频、以及检测器在社交传播过程中是否仍然可靠。为填补这一空白，作者引入RA-Bench。

Method: 构建RA-Bench基准，以真实视频为锚点，包含17,886个视频：1,830个真实视频锚点（覆盖10个社会风险类别）和16,056个由4个开源与5个闭源生成器生成的片段。评估沿三个维度展开：（1）检测器泛化能力，测试7个传统检测器、3种审查设置下的10个零样本多模态模型、以及2个专门在AI生成视频检测上微调的MLLM；（2）可检测性随生成质量、条件信息和采样种子的变化；（3）人类真实性判断与社交传播期间检测器的可靠性。

Result: 三类检测器家族均无法在RA-Bench实例上稳定泛化。生成属性（生成质量、条件信息）对检测器家族的影响各不相同，而源级检测模式在采样种子之间保持稳定。研究进一步发现：误导人类的视频对当前检测器同样困难，且社交传播会使检测难度增加。

Conclusion: 现有方法在检测逼真的AI生成视频方面存在困难，凸显了开发对现实场景鲁棒的检测器的必要性——这些检测器需要能够应对社交传播等实际条件，并弥合人类感知与检测器性能之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent video generators can fabricate realistic depictions of wars, disasters, public emergencies, and other real-world crises, creating substantial risks of misinformation. Existing benchmarks, however, provide limited evidence on detector and generator behavior in such settings, including how detectability varies with generation conditions, how people perceive generated videos, and whether detectors remain reliable during social dissemination. To address this gap, we introduce RA-Bench, a benchmark for AI-generated video detection that uses Real videos as Anchors. RA-Bench contains 17,886 videos, comprising 1,830 real-video anchors across 10 social-risk categories and 16,056 generated clips from four open-source and five closed-source generators. Based on RA-Bench, we organize our evaluation along three dimensions. We first assess detector generalization across seven traditional detectors, ten zero-shot multimodal models under three review settings, and two MLLMs specifically fine-tuned on AI-generated video detection. Across these methods, none of the three detector families generalizes consistently across RA-Bench instances. We then examine how detectability varies with generation quality, conditioning information, and sampling seeds. These analyses show that generation properties affect detector families differently, while source-level detection patterns remain stable across seeds. Finally, we study human authenticity judgments and detector reliability during social dissemination. We find that videos that mislead people are also difficult for current detectors, and that social dissemination makes detection harder. Together, these findings show that current methods struggle to detect realistic AI-generated videos, highlighting the need for detectors robust to evolving video generators.

</details>


### [59] [IRGNN: Efficient Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for Radar Point Cloud Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14394)
*Xiao Guo,Wanke Xia,Lili Yang,Caicong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 IRGNN（不变雷达图神经网络），用于自动驾驶中的雷达点云三维目标检测。其将稀疏、无序的雷达点云重构为具有平移与旋转不变特征设计的图表示，并通过带残差连接和虚拟节点层的改进消息传递神经网络（MPNN）增强局部特征传播与全局上下文建模，最后通过任务专属头完成目标分类与包围框预测。在 RadarScenes 数据集上，IRGNN 超越现有基于雷达的目标检测方法，且显著降低了推理时的计算成本与内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 方法在目标检测上进展显著，但在恶劣天气下可靠性下降；雷达点云因对恶劣天气和低光照具有鲁棒性而成为可靠替代。然而雷达点云稀疏、无序且信息量低于 LiDAR，导致现有基于 LiDAR 的感知方法难以直接迁移，需要专门为雷达点云设计的检测模型。

Method: IRGNN 分三步：(1) 使用平移与旋转不变的特征设计将雷达点云重构为图表示，以鲁棒建模稀疏雷达量测；(2) 采用改进的消息传递神经网络（MPNN），引入残差连接与虚拟节点层，增强局部特征传播和全局上下文建模；(3) 在学到的图表示上施加任务专属头，分别进行目标分类与包围框预测。

Result: 在 RadarScenes 数据集上的实验表明，IRGNN 优于现有基于雷达的目标检测方法，取得具有竞争力的性能；同时显著降低了推理时的计算成本与内存占用，表明其在实际部署中高效可行。

Conclusion: IRGNN 通过图神经网络结合不变特征设计，有效解决了雷达点云稀疏无序导致的感知难题，在精度、鲁棒性与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，展示了其在自动驾驶中高效雷达感知的实用潜力。

Abstract: Perception is a fundamental component of autonomous driving systems. While LiDAR-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in object detection, their reliability can degrade under adverse weather conditions. Radar point clouds provide a robust alternative due to their resilience to bad weather and low-illumination scenarios. However, radar point clouds are typically sparse, unordered, and less informative than LiDAR data, making it challenging to directly apply existing LiDAR-based perception methods. To address these challenges, we propose IRGNN, an Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for radar point cloud object detection. IRGNN first reconstructs radar point clouds into graph representations using translation- and rotation-invariant feature designs, enabling robust modeling of sparse radar measurements. It then employs an improved message passing neural network (MPNN) with residual connections and a virtual node layer to enhance local feature propagation and global context modeling. Finally, task-specific heads are applied to the learned graph representations for object classification and bounding box prediction. Experimental results on the RadarScenes dataset show that IRGNN outperforms existing radar-based object detection methods and achieves competitive performance. In addition, IRGNN significantly reduces computational cost and memory usage during inference, demonstrating its effectiveness and practical potential for efficient radar-based perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [60] [CRAFT: Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning for Subject Personalization without Composed Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14403)
*Jihun Park,Kyoungmin Lee,Jongmin Gim,Hyeonseo Jo,Jaeyeul Kim,Han Zou,Zhenpeng Zhan,Yan Zhang,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAFT 提出一种仅需 10K 参考图像与主体掩码（无需任何合成目标监督）的单步 ReFL 框架，通过 LoRA 微调预训练的 reference-aware MMDiT，以“关注何处”原则用注意力级奖励对齐注意力路由、并用注意力掩码约束像素级身份奖励，在 FLUX.2-klein-9B 上取得 XVerseBench 的 SOTA，且可迁移至其他骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动图像个性化方案依赖海量（参考图、合成目标图）配对数据训练，而合成目标需经过 LLM 提示生成、T2I 合成、主体提取、VLM 质量过滤、对应标注等多阶段昂贵管线，且方法被特定合成器与数据筛选策略所束缚，成本高、可迁移性差。

Method: 提出 CRAFT（Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning）：1）采用单步 ReFL 框架，用 LoRA 适配器微调预训练的 reference-aware MMDiT；2）数据仅需 10K 参考图像及主体掩码，无需 composed-target 监督；3）实现“Where to look”原则——注意力级奖励使噪声 token 与短语 token 的注意力对齐到正确的参考主体，得到的逐主体注意力掩码再门控像素级身份奖励，保证图像空间监督与学到的注意力路由一致。

Result: 应用于 FLUX.2-klein-9B 时，CRAFT 在 XVerseBench 上达到 SOTA，且仅用 10K 参考样本，而此前广义方法需要 15 万到 200 万以上的 composed-target 配对；同一配方迁移到其他 reference-aware 骨干仍持续提升性能。

Conclusion: CRAFT 证明仅靠紧凑的参考-only 数据即可实现 SOTA 的主体驱动个性化，彻底摆脱了对昂贵多阶段目标合成管线的依赖，为个性化模型训练提供了更高效、可迁移的新范式。

Abstract: Subject-driven image personalization---generating new images that preserve the identity of one or several reference subjects in novel scenes---is a foundational capability for modern visual content creation. It is currently dominated by generalized methods that fine-tune a pretrained multimodal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) on hundreds of thousands to millions of paired \emph{(reference, composed-target)} examples, where each composed target is a synthesized image of the subject in a novel scene. Producing such targets demands a costly multi-stage curation pipeline---LLM-based prompt generation, T2I-based composed-target synthesis, reference-subject extraction, VLM-based quality filtering, and correspondence labeling---and tightly couples each method to a particular target synthesizer and curation choice. We introduce \emph{CRAFT} (Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning), a single-step ReFL framework that fine-tunes a pre-trained \emph{reference-aware} MMDiT via LoRA adapters using a compact reference-only data construction---$10$K reference images and subject masks, with no composed-target supervision. CRAFT realizes a \emph{Where to look} principle: attention-level rewards align noise- and phrase-token attention with the correct reference subject, and the resulting per-subject attention masks gate a pixel-level identity reward to keep image-space supervision consistent with the learned attention routing. Applied to FLUX.2-klein-9B, CRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on XVerseBench \rev{while using no composed-target supervision---only $10$K reference-only samples, whereas prior generalized methods require $150$K to over $2$M composed-target pairs}. The same recipe transfers to other reference-aware backbones, consistently improving performance. Project page: https://jihun999.github.io/projects/CRAFT/.

</details>


### [61] [GhostPoint: Self-Supervised Representation Learning by Hallucinating Occluded LiDAR Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14428)
*Mohamed Abdelsamad,Bin Yang,Michael Ulrich,Miao Zhang,Yakov Miron,Alexandru Paul Condurache,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GhostPoint，一种针对 LiDAR 3D 物体检测的自监督学习（SSL）框架。它指出现有 SSL 方法仅对可见表面上的 LiDAR 回波定义目标函数，存在"可见表面偏差"，从而忽视遮挡与未观测区域；由于 3D 检测对缺失结构敏感，这种偏差限制了迁移效果。GhostPoint 通过新颖的"实例体素扩张"在检测到的实例周围生成邻域，并让其幻化潜在特征：编码器处理观测回波，额外的预测器从观测上下文推断邻域表示；同时引入预测器级监督（可见体素匹配教师编码器目标，未观测体素匹配教师预测器幻觉），从而显式建模超出观测回波的结构。在 nuScenes 和 Waymo 上的大量实验表明，该方法达到 SOTA，持续提升下游 3D 检测性能，尤其在稀疏扫描场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SSL 方法（如面向语义/全景分割的预训练）的损失函数几乎只定义在可见表面返回的 LiDAR 点上，遮挡和未观测区域完全不受约束。这种"可见表面偏差"对逐点预测任务足够，但 3D 检测需要对缺失结构鲁棒，因而 SSL 预训练迁移到 3D 检测的效果仍然较弱，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: GhostPoint 包括：(1) 实例体素扩张（instance voxel dilation）：在发现的目标实例周围生成局部邻域体素；(2) 架构上，编码器处理观测回波，额外的预测器从观测上下文推断邻域表示（幻觉潜在特征）；(3) 两层级监督：标准编码器级监督 + 新提出的预测器级监督，对生成邻域中采样的体素施加约束——可见体素匹配教师编码器目标，未观测体素匹配教师预测器幻觉。由此鼓励学习到的表示显式建模超出观测返回值的结构。

Result: 在 nuScenes 与 Waymo 两个大规模数据集上的广泛评估表明，GhostPoint 达到 state-of-the-art 性能，能够持续改进下游 3D 检测任务，尤其在扫描稀疏、观测不完整的场景下增益显著。

Conclusion: 通过显式约束未观测/遮挡区域并让模型学习"超越可见表面"的结构建模，GhostPoint 有效弥合了 SSL 预训练与 3D 检测之间的迁移鸿沟，验证了针对缺失结构建模范式对 3D 检测类任务的价值。

Abstract: 3D object detection from LiDAR point clouds is a core problem in autonomous driving. Recent advances in self-supervised learning (SSL) enable scalable pretraining and transfers well to per-point tasks such as semantic and panoptic segmentation, but transfer to 3D detection remains weaker. We analyze recent SSL methods and find that most objectives are defined only on measured LiDAR returns from visible surfaces, leaving occluded and unobserved regions unconstrained. This visible-surface bias can be sufficient for point-wise prediction, but 3D detection requires robustness to missing structure. To address this gap, we propose GhostPoint, an SSL framework that hallucinates latent features in local neighborhoods around discovered instances, generated via a novel instance voxel dilation. In GhostPoint, an encoder processes observed returns, and an additional predictor infers neighborhood representations from observed context. In addition to standard encoder-level supervision, we introduce a predictor-level supervision scheme on sampled voxels from generated neighborhoods. Specifically, observed (visible/masked) voxels match teacher-encoder targets, while unobserved voxels match teacher-predictor hallucinations. This design encourages the learned representation to explicitly model structure beyond observed returns. Extensive evaluations on nuScenes and Waymo demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently improving downstream 3D detection, especially under sparse scans and limited labels.

</details>


### [62] [Style or Signature? Artist-Disjoint Evaluation of Style Classification in Frozen Vision Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14435)
*Rory Ashton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验了使用CLIP等冻结图像嵌入进行绘画艺术史风格分类的准确性是否真正反映了对风格的理解，而非对个体艺术家的识别。通过采用艺术家留出（artist-disjoint）评估协议，准确率从0.87降至0.77，且不同流派降幅极不均衡（超现实主义下降20个百分点），作者由此论证艺术家留出式评估是衡量风格理解能力的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献报道冻结图像嵌入（如CLIP）在艺术风格分类上具有很高的准确率，但作者质疑这种准确率可能源于对画家本人的识别，而非对艺术运动/风格的真正理解。标准评估使用随机划分时，同一艺术家的作品可能同时出现在训练集和测试集中，导致分类器通过识别画家身份获得虚高成绩，从而掩盖了模型对风格本质的理解程度。

Method: 作者提出艺术家留出协议：逐个留出每一位艺术家，确保没有任何作品在分类时依赖同一画家的其他作品。在包含320幅画作、涵盖四个二十世纪艺术运动的平衡数据集上，采用5近邻（5-NN）分类器进行重新评估，并使用了四种图像编码器（包括纯视觉自监督模型）以排除语言模型的干扰。

Result: 在艺术家留出协议下，5-NN风格分类准确率从0.87降至0.77，且下降幅度极不均衡：印象派和立体主义几乎不受影响，而超现实主义下降了20个百分点。该现象在四种图像编码器（包括仅视觉的自监督模型）中一致出现，说明影响源于视觉结构而非语言信息。当编码器捕捉到真正的共同形式时，个体艺术家几乎不可识别但风格分类依然稳健；超现实主义则呈现相反情况。

Conclusion: 作者认为，艺术家留出式评估是衡量风格理解所必需的，否则分类器很可能识别艺术家而非风格。实验表明不同艺术运动对"识别画家"这一捷径的依赖程度不同，风格分类的高准确率不能直接等同于对风格的真正理解。

Abstract: Frozen image embeddings from models such as CLIP are increasingly used to classify paintings by art-historical style, with high reported accuracy. We ask whether this accuracy reflects an understanding of style or the recognition of individual artists. Standard evaluation uses random splits in which works by the same artist appear on both sides, so a classifier can succeed by recognising the painter rather than the movement. We re-evaluate style classification under an artist-disjoint protocol, holding out every artist in turn so that no work is ever classified using other works by its own painter. On a balanced dataset of 320 paintings across four twentieth-century movements, 5-NN style accuracy falls from 0.87 to 0.77 under this protocol, and the drop is sharply uneven. Impressionism and Cubism barely move, while Surrealism falls twenty points. The pattern holds across four image encoders, including a vision-only self-supervised model, which places the effect in visual structure rather than language. Where an encoder captures genuine shared form, individual artists are barely recognisable yet style is robust, while Surrealism shows the opposite. We argue that artist-disjoint evaluation is necessary to measure stylistic understanding in frozen embeddings.

</details>


### [63] [Marionette: Predicting World States, Rendering Geometry, Painting Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14530)
*Zian Meng,Zhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Qiang Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Marionette，一种用于带关节角色交互式游戏的世界模型。它显式地建模并预测可解释的 276 维 3D 世界状态（多实体骨架、度量根轨迹和旋转），通过零参数渲染器闭式计算几何与遮挡，再由视频扩散模型在控制条件下合成照片级 RGB 外观。实验表明该状态可被直接控制、可修复，且外观保真度无明显损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式游戏世界模型通常直接在像素或潜空间自回归视觉观测，导致姿态、几何、遮挡等结构化属性被迫由同一生成序列隐式维持。随着时间步增长，这些潜在世界属性的误差会累积，使一致性与可控性变差。作者旨在将精确的几何计算从神经网络中分离出去，交给固定、零参数的渲染器，从而提升长时程一致性与可控性。

Method: Marionette 由三部分组成：1) 两阶段自回归动力学模型预测显式可解释的 276 维 3D 世界状态，包含多实体关节骨架、度量级根轨迹与旋转；2) 零参数图形桥将预测状态转换为姿态控制视频，以闭式方式计算世界空间几何与遮挡；3) 控制条件视频扩散观测模型根据结构化控制合成照片级 RGB 观测。

Result: 实验验证两个特性：1) 预测的世界状态可直接控制——强制注入不匹配的动作流使根对齐关节误差在 48 个保留片段上改变 31%；2) 长时程行为由状态决定且可在状态层面修复——默认生成下两个角色漂移至相距 21.2 米（真实记录会话约为 5 米），且三分之一帧出现地面穿透；在显式状态上施加地形碰撞器和分离上限两条规则后，穿透减少 66% 并保持角色交互，观测模型无需改动。外观保真度无可检测损失：FVD 831 对比基线 799。

Conclusion: 将结构化世界状态显式建模、让零参数渲染器承担几何计算、而神经网络仅负责外观合成，是一种可行且有效的范式：既赋予世界模型直接可控性与可修复性，又不牺牲观测质量的保真度。该设计为长时程交互式游戏世界模型提供了更稳健、更可解释的架构方向。

Abstract: Interactive game world models typically autoregress visual observations directly in pixel or latent space, forcing structured properties such as pose, geometry, and occlusion to be implicitly maintained by the same generative sequence. Over long horizons, errors in these latent world properties accumulate, making consistency and controllability fragile. We explicitly model the evolving world state, delegate exact geometric computation to a fixed, zero-parameter renderer, and leave the neural model to synthesize appearance. We instantiate this idea as Marionette, a world model for interactive games with articulated characters. First, a two-stage autoregressive dynamics model predicts an explicit and interpretable 276-dimensional 3D world state comprising multi-entity articulated skeletons, metric root trajectories, and rotations. Second, a zero-parameter graphics bridge converts the predicted state into pose-control videos, computing world-space geometry and occlusion in closed form. Third, a control-conditioned video-diffusion observation model synthesizes photorealistic RGB observations from the resulting structured controls. Our experiments establish two properties of Marionette. First, the predicted world state is directly controllable. Forcing a mismatched action stream changes root-aligned joint error by 31% across 48 held-out segments. Second, long-horizon behaviour is determined in the state, and can be repaired there. Left free, the two generated characters drift to 21.2 m apart (recorded sessions stay near 5 m) and a third of frames show ground penetration. Two rules imposed on the explicit state, a terrain collider and a separation cap, cut penetration by 66% and keep the pair engaged, with no change to the observation model. Routing appearance through the predicted state costs no fidelity we can detect, at an FVD of 831 against 799 for recorded pose.

</details>


### [64] [Decoding the Past: An Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning Framework for Sex Attribution in Prehistoric Hand Stencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539)
*Karel Becerra,Boris Mederos,Dean Snow,Ramón A. Mollineda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个不确定性感知的深度学习框架，用于旧石器时代手印模板（hand stencils）的性别归属判定，该框架显式建模、传播并聚合整个分析流程中的不确定性，在当代数据上准确率超过88%，并能为史前手印同时输出性别预测与内部一致性置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断旧石器时代手印模板创作者生物学性别极具挑战，原因包括：缺乏真值（ground truth）、当代群体与史前群体存在种群差异、图像退化引入不确定性。传统形态测量方法存在跨性别结构高度重叠、跨种群泛化能力差以及特征工程主观性强等问题，亟需一种更稳健、可复现且能量化不确定性的方法。

Method: 构建不确定性感知深度学习方法，包含：(1) 双图像处理与双轮廓提取；(2) 结构化剪影数据增强；(3) 模型架构多样性；(4) 集成式决策聚合。每个手印生成12种可行的剪影实现以捕捉边界不确定性，交由两个各含10个深度神经网络的集成模型（EfficientNet-B3与MobileViT-S）处理，模型在14,036个当代手部样本上训练。此外采用三角化验证方案，将集成预测与无监督2D潜空间流形映射（UMAP + k-NN）以及可解释AI空间归因（LayerCAM）相结合，以确保解剖学上的一致性。

Result: 在当代数据上，集成模型在较年长年龄组的分类准确率超过88%。应用于史前手印时，框架同时输出性别预测与内部一致性置信度，能够区分形态学上稳定与模糊的案例；集成预测、潜空间结构与可解释性分析三者收敛，验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 不确定性可以成为考古学推断中一个可量化、可度量的组成部分，使古代岩石艺术的解码更加稳健、可复现。

Abstract: Determining the biological sex of the individuals who created Upper Paleolithic hand stencils remains a challenging problem due to the absence of ground truth, population differences between contemporary and prehistoric groups, and the uncertainty introduced by image degradation. Traditional morphometric methods suffer from high structural overlap across sexes, poor cross-population generalizability, and subjective feature engineering. This study presents an uncertainty-aware deep learning framework for sex attribution in prehistoric hand stencils that explicitly models, propagates, and aggregates uncertainty throughout the analytical pipeline. The methodology combines dual image processing, dual contour extraction, structured silhouette augmentation, model architectural diversity, and ensemble-based decision aggregation. The pipeline generates twelve plausible silhouette realizations per stencil to capture boundary uncertainties, which are processed by two ensembles of ten deep neural networks each (EfficientNet-B3 and MobileViT-S) trained on 14,036 contemporary hand samples. Furthermore, a triangulated validation scheme integrates ensemble predictions with unsupervised 2D latent-space manifold mapping (UMAP + k-NN) and explainable AI spatial attributions (LayerCAM) to ensure anatomical consistency. On contemporary data, ensemble models achieve strong classification performance, with accuracies exceeding 88% in older age groups. When applied to prehistoric stencils, the framework produces both sex predictions and confidence measures of internal agreement, enabling the distinction between morphologically stable and ambiguous cases. Convergence across ensemble predictions, latent-space structure, and interpretability analyses shows that uncertainty can become a measurable component of archaeological inference, enabling robust and reproducible decoding of ancient rock art.

</details>


### [65] [MagnifiQ: Patch-aware Text Guided Progressive Upscaling for High-Resolution Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14543)
*Mahesh Reddy,Yashesh Savani,Antoine Mercier,Hong Cai,Fatih Porikli,Guillaume Berger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagnifiQ 是一种渐进式高分辨率图像复原框架，通过将预训练文生图扩散模型（如 SDXL）的自注意力层替换为卷积操作并将复杂度和分辨率呈线性关系，配合多阶段渐进升采样与分块文本提示，实现了从 1024×1024 到 4096×4096 的全局连贯、细节锐利且计算可行的 4K 复原，在感知质量和人类偏好上超越既有扩散复原方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分分辨率（尤其 4K）图像复原既需保持全局结构一致性，又需恢复精细局部细节；直接在 4K 上做扩散复原计算开销巨大，且容易产生重复或不一致的纹理，因此需要一种更为可扩展且质量可控的复原方案。

Method: (1) 复用预训练文生图扩散模型 SDXL，将其自注意力层替换为卷积运算，使计算成本随图像分辨率线性增长，支撑 4K 推理；(2) 提出渐进式升采样策略，在多个分辨率阶段（如 1024→4096）逐步迭代复原并细化中间输出，而非一次性直接生成 4K 图像，以提升全局一致性并减少伪影；(3) 引入分块专属文本提示（patch-specific text prompts），在复原过程中提供空间局部的语义引导，以增强局部细节并控制内容漂移。

Result: 在合成与真实退化图像上的大量实验表明，MagnifiQ 在感知质量与人类偏好上优于先前的扩散式复原方法，能生成更锐利的纹理和更连贯的 4K 结果，并通过可扩展的骨干网络提供实用的速度—质量权衡。

Conclusion: MagnifiQ 验证了“线性复杂度骨干 + 渐进升采样 + 局部语义提示”这一组合能有效缓解高分辨率扩散复原的计算瓶颈与纹理不一致问题，为可扩展的 4K 图像复原提供了实用且高质量的方案。

Abstract: High-resolution image restoration from degraded inputs is challenging because it must preserve global structural consistency while recovering fine-grained local details, especially at 4K resolution where direct diffusion-based restoration is computationally expensive and prone to repeated or inconsistent textures. In this work, we introduce MagnifiQ, an image restoration framework that progressively upscales and restores images across resolutions, e.g., from 1024x1024 to 4096x4096. Our approach leverages a pre-trained text-to-image diffusion model such as SDXL and adapts it for more scalable high-resolution inference by replacing its original self-attention layers with convolutional operations whose computational cost grows linearly with image resolution. We further propose a progressive upscaling strategy that iteratively restores images over multiple resolution stages, refining each intermediate output rather than directly hallucinating the final 4K image, thereby improving global coherence and reducing high-resolution artifacts. To enhance local details while controlling content drift, MagnifiQ uses patch-specific text prompts that provide spatially localized semantic guidance during restoration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world degraded images show that MagnifiQ outperforms prior diffusion-based restoration methods in perceptual quality and human preference, producing sharper textures and more coherent 4K results while offering practical speed--quality trade-offs through its scalable backbone and progressive design.

</details>


### [66] [CPI-Bench: A Comprehensive,Practical and Intelligent Benchmark for Real-World Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14546)
*Qinye Zhou,Jun Zheng,Yongchao Du,Yuan Wang,Zhengrui Chen,Zuan Gao,Taihang Hu,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Zhengtao Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Denghui Yang,Yuhang Yu,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPI-Bench，一个针对真实世界图像编辑的综合、实用且智能的评测基准，包含通用、实用、智能三个子集，覆盖多图像编辑、高频真实应用场景及高要求推理式编辑，并能更好地区分模型性能、与人类偏好高度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑评测基准局限于简单的单图任务，覆盖维度有限，难以有效区分不同模型的性能，无法可靠评估模型在多图编辑、复杂推理指令及实际部署等真实场景下的表现。

Method: 构建CPI-Bench基准，包含三个核心子集：CPI-General-Bench（全面覆盖多种编辑任务并首创多图像编辑评测）、CPI-Practical-Bench（聚焦高频真实用户应用场景）、CPI-Intelligent-Bench（针对高难度推理型编辑能力评测），并基于主流编辑模型开展系统评估与排名分析。

Result: 实验表明CPI-Bench增强了模型间的性能区分度，能够全面可靠地量化通用编辑能力、实际部署效能与高级推理编辑之间的差距；排名分析显示其与Arena Image Edit排行榜对齐度最高，能忠实反映人类评估者的偏好。

Conclusion: CPI-Bench可作为人类评估偏好的可靠代理，为图像编辑模型的后续优化提供有价值的指引。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of image editing models and their widespread application across various domains, there is an increasingly urgent need to deploy these model capabilities directly into real-world scenarios. However, existing benchmarks remain confined to simple single-image tasks, suffering from limited coverage dimensions and an inability to effectively differentiate performance among diverse models. Consequently, they fail to reliably evaluate model performance in complex multi-image editing, highly demanding reasoning instructions, and practical deployment settings. To address these limitations, we propose CPI-Bench, a Comprehensive, Practical andIntelligent benchmark for real-world image editing. CPI-Bench comprises three core subsets: CPI-General-Bench, which comprehensively covers diverse editing tasks and pioneers the inclusion of multi-image editing evaluation; CPI-Practical-Bench, which focuses on high-frequency real-user application scenarios; and CPI-Intelligent-Bench, which is dedicated to evaluating capabilities in highly demanding reasoning-based editing. Evaluation results of mainstream image editing models based on CPI-Bench demonstrate that CPI-Bench enhances performance differentiation among models. It provides a comprehensive and reliable quantification of gaps in general editing capabilities, practical deployment efficacy, and advanced reasoning-based editing, offering invaluable guidance for the future optimization of image editing models. Crucially, our ranking analysis reveals that CPI-Bench achieves the highest alignment with the Arena Image Edit Leaderboard, indicating it faithfully captures the preferences and perceptual judgments of human evaluators, serving as a robust proxy for real-world user experience.

</details>

### [67] [Does a Language Server Save Tokens for Coding Agents? A Measurement Methodology and Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13568)
*Pengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对“语义检索（LSP）比词法检索（grep）更省 token”这一长期被断言却几乎未被测量的说法，本文用一个指标（tokens-to-success）将其形式化，设计了五臂消融实验，并在 Python/TypeScript 仓库及多个 Claude 模型上做了初步研究。结论是有条件的且通常是负面的：在符号定位任务上 LSP 反而多花 token（+6% 至 +118%）；在引用完整性任务上 LSP 提升精度但不省 token，也无法突破由 agent 彻底性决定的召回上限，仅对最弱模型省 token。工具选择依赖任务类型，且完整 LSP 也无法弥补语义引用天然遗漏注释与字符串这一问题。最终建议不是“始终用 LSP”，而是按任务类别、模型能力与词法噪声设计自适应路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码 agent 的上下文预算大部分消耗在检索上。词法检索（grep）通用、即时、零配置但噪声大，无法区分定义、调用与注释；语义检索（LSP）精确、带类型，但需运行并索引的服务器，且每个符号都要付出往返开销。“语义检索更省 token”的说法被到处断言却几乎无人测量，公开来源中没有在任务成功率相等的条件下单独隔离 LSP 与词法之间的 token 差值。本文旨在填补这一测量空白。

Method: 用单一指标 tokens-to-success 形式化该问题；设计五臂消融实验，从混杂因素中隔离出语义检索的作用；将三个预先声明的失败模式映射为可测变量；在 Python 与 TypeScript 仓库上，用 Claude Opus 4.8、Sonnet 4.6、Haiku 4.5 开展初步研究。任务涵盖符号定位、引用完整性以及用真实测试执行打分的编辑任务，并对比了 grep、仅位置的 LSP、以及带内联文本来增强的完整 LSP。

Result: 答案是有条件的且通常是否定的。符号定位任务上 LSP 增加 token 消耗（+6% 至 +118%），agent 在免费获得 LSP 时也会忽略它；引用完整性任务上 LSP 提升精度但不省 token，且无法提高由 agent 彻底性设定的召回上限，仅在最强模型……最弱模型（Haiku）上省 token。工具选择依赖任务：定位任务上模型默认用 grep（语义检索使用率 0-6%），引用任务上约半数情况会自发使用 LSP。编辑任务差距最显著：grep 能完美解决多文件重命名，仅位置的 LSP 因漏掉一个调用点失败四分之三；即使完整、索引预热的文本增强型 LSP 也只能弥合大部分差距而无法完全闭合，因为重命名必须改动注释与字符串，而这些被语义引用排除在外。

Conclusion: 启示并非“始终使用 LSP”，而是需要一个按任务类别、模型能力与词法噪声进行自适应的检索路由器：对不同任务与模型选择 grep 或 LSP 各有最优解，且 LSP 的语义精确性在某些场景（如引用补齐）有用，在另一些场景（如需要触及注释/字符串的重命名）存在结构性盲区，无法替代词法检索。

Abstract: Coding agents spend most of their context budget on retrieval. Lexical retrieval (grep) is universal, instant, and zero-setup, but noisy: it cannot tell a definition from a call from a comment. Semantic retrieval via the Language Server Protocol (LSP) is precise and typed, but needs a running, indexed server and pays a per-symbol round-trip. The claim that semantic retrieval is more token-efficient is, we find, asserted almost everywhere and measured almost nowhere: no public source isolates the LSP-vs-lexical token delta for an agent at equal task-success. This paper formalizes the question with one metric (tokens-to-success), specifies a five-arm ablation isolating semantic retrieval from confounds, maps three pre-stated failure modes onto measurable variables, and reports a preliminary study (Python and TypeScript repos; Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 4.6, Haiku 4.5). The answer is conditional and usually negative. On symbol-named localization the LSP costs tokens (+6% to +118%) and the agent ignores it when free. On reference-completeness it buys precision but not token savings and cannot raise the recall ceiling set by agent thoroughness; it saves tokens only for the weakest model. Tool choice is task-dependent: models default to grep on localization (0-6% semantic use) but reach for the LSP about half the time on reference tasks, unprompted. On edits scored by real test execution the gap is starkest: grep solves multi-file renames perfectly, a location-only LSP fails three-quarters of them by missing a call site, and even a complete, index-warmed, text-enriched LSP (each reference's line inline, as production LSP-MCP servers do) recovers most of the gap but cannot close it, since a rename must touch comments and strings that semantic references exclude. The implication is not LSP-always but an adaptive router keyed on task class, model capability, and lexical noise.

</details>


### [68] [Think in Latent, Explain in Language: Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13570)
*Dayuan Zhao,Shengcao Cao,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SELR（Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning），一个统一的潜在推理框架，通过多任务训练目标（Answer Loss + CoT Loss）让单一模型既能进行高效的潜在推理，又能将自身潜在表征解码为人类可理解的思维链步骤，从而同时实现token效率与内在可解释性，无需外部解码器。作者在LLM和VLM上验证了该方法在效率和准确性上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理虽然通过将冗长推理压缩为紧凑嵌入而带来计算效率上的显著提升，但也使推理过程变得不透明、难以解释。现有方法存在尖锐的权衡：要么是完全不可读的"黑盒"（如Coconut），要么依赖独立的事后解码器（如Heima），后者引入额外的架构开销，并使解释与实际推理过程脱节。因此，需要一种既高效又天然可解释、且无需辅助模型的潜在推理方案。

Method: 核心方法是SELR框架，训练单个模型完成高效且内在可解释的潜在推理。其关键贡献是一个新颖的多任务训练目标，同时优化两个目标：(1) Answer Loss，优化潜在推理轨迹以产生准确的最终答案；(2) CoT Loss，显式训练同一模型将自身的潜在表征解码回人类可理解的思维链步骤。该设计确保生成的潜在表征既对任务有效又在语义上可解释，从而无需外部解码器。

Result: 在大型语言模型（LLM）和视觉-语言模型（VLM）上的实验验证表明，与基线方法相比，SELR在token效率和准确率上均表现更优，并且独特地提供了无需辅助模型的自包含可解释性。

Conclusion: SELR成功弥合了潜在推理的效率优势与可解释性需求之间的鸿沟，证明了通过多任务联合训练，模型可以在保持高效潜在推理的同时，实现自身潜在状态向人类可读推理步骤的内在可解码性，为可解释的高效推理提供了统一且无需额外架构的方案。

Abstract: Latent reasoning has emerged as a powerful alternative to text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT), offering significant gains in computational efficiency by compressing verbose reasoning into compact embeddings. However, compressing reasoning into the latent space renders the thinking opaque, hindering its interpretability. Current methods present a stark trade-off: they either function as unexplainable ''black boxes'' (e.g., Coconut), where the latent reasoning is not human-readable, or rely on separate post-hoc decoders for explainability (e.g., Heima), introducing architectural overhead and decoupling the explanation from the actual reasoning process. In this work, we present a unified framework for Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning (SELR) that trains a single model to perform efficient and inherently explainable latent reasoning. Our core contribution is a novel multi-task training objective that optimizes for two goals simultaneously: (1) an Answer Loss that optimizes the latent reasoning trajectory to produce accurate final answers, and (2) a CoT Loss that explicitly trains the same model to decode its own latent representations back into human-understandable reasoning steps. This design ensures that generated latent representations are both task-effective and semantically interpretable, eliminating the need for external decoders. We validate the effectiveness of SELR on both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), demonstrating that SELR achieves superior token efficiency and accuracy compared to baselines, while uniquely providing self-contained explainability without auxiliary models. Project page is available at https://jasondayuan.github.io/SELR/.

</details>


### [69] [Not All Tokens Are Equal: Inflation-Aware Routing for Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13571)
*Heming Fu,Shan Lin,Qianqian Xie,Guojun Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"令牌通胀"(token inflation)概念，指智能体系统因失败重试导致的真实工作流成本与单次调用成本之间的差距。作者开发了InflationAgent四阶段路由器：系统化测量各模型层与任务类型的通胀率（最高可达4.25×）、提出基于局部推理的预执行难度信号CoT Branching Entropy（AUROC 0.887）、以语义汇率(SER)最大化预期准确率与预测真实成本之比来选择模型，并采用全新升级策略丢弃失败链后再路由到更强模型。在GSM8K固定预算下，InflationAgent以94.7%准确率超过FrugalGPT的91.0%，同时节省31%的令牌；实验还表明将失败推理链直接转发给GPT-4o会使准确率下降最多34.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统在模型首次回答失败后会重试，产生额外令牌消耗，造成真实工作流成本与模型单次调用价格之间的巨大差距（即令牌通胀）。像FrugalGPT这样的路由系统仅基于单次调用成本做决策，在困难任务上会低估真实成本达2倍以上，导致成本估算与资源分配不准确。

Method: 提出InflationAgent四阶段路由器：(1) 跨模型层级与任务类型系统化测量令牌通胀率；(2) 提出CoT Branching Entropy (CBE)，一种完全由局部推理计算的预执行难度信号，用于预测高通胀；(3) 通过语义汇率(SER)（预期准确率除以预测真实成本）选择模型；(4) 采用fresh-escalation策略，在路由到更强模型前丢弃失败推理链，避免污染上游模型的上下文。

Result: 在GSM8K固定预算下，InflationAgent达到94.7%准确率，优于FrugalGPT的91.0%，同时令牌用量减少31%；7B模型在多跳问答上测得通胀率高达4.25×；CBE预测高通胀的AUROC达0.887；将失败推理链转发给GPT-4o会导致其准确率下降最多34.8个百分点，验证了fresh-escalation策略的必要性。

Conclusion: 令牌通胀是智能体工作流中不可忽视的真实成本因素，基于单次调用成本的路由会系统性低估开销。通过预执行难度信号与真实成本感知的模型选择，可以在固定预算下同时提升准确率并减少令牌消耗；丢弃失败链（而非转发）是升级到更强模型时的关键设计选择。

Abstract: When a language model fails to answer a query on the first attempt, an agentic system retries, consuming additional tokens each time. This retry overhead creates a gap between what a model's per-token price implies and what a full workflow actually costs. We call this gap \emph{token inflation} and define it as the ratio of true workflow cost to single-call cost. Systems like FrugalGPT route based on the latter, which can underestimate real cost by more than $2\times$ on difficult tasks. We address this with InflationAgent, a four-stage router that (1) measures token inflation systematically across model tiers and task types, finding inflation as high as $4.25\times$ for a 7B model on multi-hop question answering; (2) introduces CoT Branching Entropy (CBE), a pre-execution difficulty signal computed entirely from local inference, which predicts high inflation with AUROC 0.887; and (3) selects models by maximizing a Semantic Exchange Rate (SER) that divides expected accuracy by predicted true cost, with a fresh-escalation policy that discards failed chains before routing to a stronger model. On GSM8K under a fixed budget, InflationAgent achieves 94.7\% accuracy versus 91.0\% for FrugalGPT while using 31\% fewer tokens, and we show that forwarding a failed reasoning chain to GPT-4o reduces its accuracy by up to 34.8 percentage points, validating the fresh-escalation design.

</details>


### [70] [BCMT: Blockwise Causal Memory Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13578)
*Rachid Arezki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BCMT（分块因果记忆 Transformer），一种面向长上下文语言建模的架构。它在局部块内使用稠密因果自注意力，并通过指数因果记忆聚合每块的适应性摘要，再注入回 token 表示以传播长程信息，完全不需要显式全局注意力或可学习记忆状态。在长达 1024 token 的上下文上，BCMT 在达到与稠密 Transformer 相当的验证性能的同时，显著提升了训练吞吐量并降低了内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 的稠密自注意力虽能建模长距离依赖，但其复杂度随序列长度呈二次增长，成为长上下文语言建模的主要瓶颈；而现有的循环记忆架构又依赖不可并行化的可学习记忆状态。作者希望找到一种既能高效传播全局上下文、又完全可并行化、且与标准稠密自注意力实现兼容的方案。

Method: BCMT 将序列划分为局部块：块内使用独立的稠密因果自注意力处理局部 token 交互；每个块生成一个适应性摘要，通过指数因果记忆进行聚合；该记忆随后被注入回 token 表示，从而在不依赖显式全局注意力的前提下传播长程上下文。其记忆机制完全可并行化，且无需维护稠密的跨 token 交互或可学习记忆状态。

Result: 在最长 1024 token 的语言建模实验中，BCMT 取得了与稠密 Transformer 相当的验证性能，同时显著提升训练吞吐量并降低内存消耗。消融实验证实这些改进确实来自所提出的记忆机制。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，由块摘要构建的指数因果记忆可以作为长上下文语言建模中稠密全局注意力机制的有效替代方案，在保持性能的同时获得计算效率上的显著收益。

Abstract: Transformer architectures rely on dense self-attention to model long-range dependencies, but this mechanism exhibits quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length. We introduce BCMT (Blockwise Causal Memory Transformer), an architecture for long-context language modeling that decouples local token interactions from global context propagation. Dense causal self-attention is applied independently within local blocks, while each block produces an adaptive summary aggregated through an exponential causal memory. This memory is subsequently injected back into the token representations, enabling efficient propagation of long-range contextual information without relying on explicit global attention. Unlike standard Transformers and recurrent memory architectures, BCMT maintains neither dense interactions between distant tokens nor learned memory states. Its memory mechanism is fully parallelizable and remains compatible with standard implementations of dense self-attention. Experiments on language modeling with context lengths of up to 1024 tokens show that BCMT achieves validation performance comparable to that of Dense Transformers while significantly improving training throughput and reducing memory consumption. An ablation study further confirms that these improvements arise from the proposed memory mechanism. These results demonstrate that an exponential causal memory constructed from block summaries provides an effective alternative to dense global attention mechanisms for long-context language modeling.

</details>


### [71] [Jais 2: A Family of Arabic-Centric Open Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13580)
*Mohamed Anwar,Abed Alhakim Freihat,George Ibrahim,Mostafa Awad,Abdelrahman Sadallah,Gurpreet Gosal,Gokulakrishnan Ramakrishnan,Sarath Chandran,Biswajit Mishra,Rituraj Joshi,Ahmed Frikha,Etienne Goffinet,Abhishek Maiti,Ali El Filali,Sarah AlBarri,Samujjwal Ghosh,Rahul Pal,Parvez Mullah,Awantika Shukla,Sajid siddiki,Samta Kamboj,Onkar Pandit,Sunil Kumar Sahu,AbdelRahman Elbadawy,Amr Mohamed,Ahmad Chamma,Evan Dufraisse,Abdelaziz Bounhar,Dani Bouch,Hadi Abdine,Guokan Shang,Fajri Koto,Yuxia Wang,Zhuohan Xie,Ali Mekky,Rania Elbadry,Sarfraz Ahmad,Momina Ahsan,Omar El Herraoui,Daniil Orel,Hasan Iqbal,Kareem Elzeky,Mervat Abassy,Kareem Elozeiri,Saadeldine Eletter,Farah Atif,Nurdaulet Mukhituly,Haonan Li,Xudong Han,Aaryamonvikram Singh,Zainul Abedien Ahmed Quraishi,Neha Sengupta,Larry Murray,Avraham Sheinin,Joel Hestness,Natalia Vassilieva,Hector Xuguang Ren,Zhengzhong Liu,Michalis Vazirgiannis,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Jais 2 是由 MBZUAI、Cerebras 和 Inception 联合开发的阿拉伯语中心大语言模型家族，包含迄今最大（70B 参数）的开源从零训练阿拉伯语中心模型，以及一个具有竞争力的 8B 变体。通过定制阿拉伯语词表、优化架构与训练配方，在更小的 token 预算下实现了高效的训练与推理，并在 OALL2、AraGen 及诗歌、宗教、饮食、解梦等文化基准上取得了领先或强劲表现，同时保持有竞争力的英语结果。模型以商业许可开源发布，70B 版本还以聊天应用形式部署于 Cerebras 硬件上，推理速度高达每秒 2000 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动阿拉伯语中心的语言建模发展，解决现有开源模型在阿拉伯语数据覆盖、文化保真度与训练/推理效率上的不足。作者希望通过规模、语言多样性、文化保真度、开放性与速度的统一，为阿拉伯语 LLM 的研究与开发提供开放权重的基础模型。

Method: 开发了包含 70B 与 8B 两个规模的模型家族；采用定制阿拉伯语中心词表以提升训练与推断效率；使用优化的架构与训练配方，以显著小于同类模型的 token 预算完成高效训练。模型覆盖翻译、摘要等通用任务以及诗歌、宗教、饮食、解梦等文化相关的阿拉伯语基准。70B 版本还部署为聊天应用，运行于 Cerebras 硬件之上。

Result: 在报告的基准中，Jais 2 在所评估的开源模型中于 OALL2 和 AraGen 上取得领先结果；在诗歌、宗教、饮食、解梦等多个文化基准以及翻译、摘要等通用任务上表现强劲；阿拉伯语成绩强，英语成绩具有竞争力。70B 版本部署后可提供每秒高达 2000 token 的高吞吐服务。

Conclusion: Jais 2 以更小的 token 预算实现了强阿拉伯语性能和有竞争力的英语性能，展示了阿拉伯语中心 LLM 在文化保真与计算效率上的可行性；模型以商业许可开放发布，旨在作为支持阿拉伯语 LLM 进一步研究发展的开放权重基础。

Abstract: Jais 2 is a family of Arabic-centric large language models developed jointly by MBZUAI, Cerebras, and Inception, designed to advance Arabic-centric language modeling, with strong performance across the Arabic and culturally grounded benchmarks evaluated in this report. The family includes, to our knowledge, the largest open Arabic-centric LLM trained from scratch at 70B parameters, and a competitive 8B-parameter variant among the evaluated open models. A custom Arabic-centric vocabulary enables efficient training and inference. In addition, an optimized architecture and training recipe yield highly compute-efficient training. With a substantially smaller token budget than comparable models, Jais 2 achieves strong Arabic performance on the benchmarks considered in this report and competitive English results. The models obtain leading results among the evaluated open models on OALL2 and AraGen. They also perform strongly on several culturally grounded Arabic benchmarks, including poetry, religion, cuisine, and dream interpretation, as well as in general tasks such as translation and summarization. We release the models in HuggingFace under a commercially permissive license. Jais 2 70B is also released as a chat app on the Web, iOS, and Android; it runs on Cerebras hardware, delivering up to 2,000 tokens per second, and enabling high-throughput Arabic-centric chat serving in our deployment setting. By uniting scale, linguistic diversity, cultural fidelity, openness, and speed, Jais 2 provides an open-weight foundation intended to support further research and development in Arabic-centric LLMs.

</details>


### [72] [IterCOMP: Reasoning-aware Adaptive Prompt Compression for Multi-hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13588)
*JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IterCOMP 是一个无需训练的统一提示压缩框架，通过迭代压缩循环在多跳问答中融入多跳推理，将文档分解为证据片段、评估问题可回答性并生成后续问题以整合关键证据，最终在 MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 HotpotQA 上实现更高的精确匹配与 F1 分数且减少 token 预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要跨多个证据片段进行复杂推理，而检索增强生成系统常因冗长、嘈杂的上下文而效率与准确性下降。现有提示压缩方法多面向单轮查询，无法捕捉相互依赖的推理步骤。

Method: IterCOMP 是一种统一且无需训练的提示压缩框架，包含三个核心环节：将文档分解为证据片段、评估问题可回答性、生成针对性的后续问题；通过迭代压缩循环逐步整合必要证据，最终生成紧凑且面向推理的提示。

Result: 在 MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 HotpotQA 三个数据集上，IterCOMP 相比现有基线在 Exact Match 和 F1 指标上均实现显著提升，同时降低 token 预算，并展现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: IterCOMP 验证了将多跳推理融入迭代压缩流程的有效性，能够在压缩上下文的同时保持甚至提升多跳问答的推理性能，为长上下文检索增强生成场景提供了有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires complex reasoning across multiple evidence segments, which often overwhelms retrieval-augmented generation systems with lengthy and noisy contexts, thereby undermining both efficiency and accuracy. While existing prompt compression methods attempt to address this issue, they are typically designed for single-turn queries and fail to capture interdependent reasoning steps. We propose IterCOMP, a unified, training-free prompt compression framework that incorporates multi-hop reasoning within an iterative compression loop. IterCOMP decomposes documents into evidence segments, evaluates question answerability, and generates targeted follow-up questions to iteratively integrate essential evidence, producing a compact, reasoning-oriented prompt. Experiments on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA demonstrate that IterCOMP achieves substantial improvements in Exact Match and F1 scores while reducing the token budget, outperforming existing baselines and exhibiting robustness as reasoning complexity increases.

</details>


### [73] [Measuring Fairness in Large Audio Language Models via Semantic-Aware Bias Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13624)
*Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义感知的混合效应回归框架，用于评估大型音频语言模型（LALM）在不同人口学亚组上的公平性，通过在模型中引入句子级语义嵌入作为协变量、并将说话人身份建模为随机效应，显式控制语音内容语义变化和说话人特征等混杂因素，减少虚假公平性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LALM被广泛用于语音识别、音频问答等任务，引发了对不同人口学亚组公平性的担忧。然而在语音输入场景下，公平性评估因语义变化和说话人特征等混杂因素而困难重重，忽视这些因素会导致对模型偏见的误导性结论。

Method: 该方法基于语义感知的混合效应回归框架：将参考文本的句子级语义嵌入作为固定效应协变量，将说话人身份建模为随机效应。关键创新在于语义表示直接从被测LALM自身提取，从而以模型自身的感知视角控制语义变化。

Result: 在模拟数据与真实世界基准上的实验表明，所提方法显著减少了虚假的公平性发现，并得到更稳健、更具可解释性的亚组性能差异估计。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效消除语音公平性评估中的混杂偏差，为LALM的亚组公平性分析提供更可信、可解释的评估手段。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) have seen increasing use for audio understanding tasks such as speech recognition and audio question answering, raising concerns about fairness across demographic subgroups. Fairness evaluation in spoken-input settings is challenging due to confounding factors, including semantic variation in spoken content and speaker-specific characteristics. Ignoring these factors can result in misleading conclusions about model bias. We propose a semantic-aware mixed-effects regression framework for fairness evaluation in LALMs that explicitly accounts for these confounders. Our approach incorporates sentence-level semantic embeddings of reference text as covariates and models speaker identity as a random effect. Notably, semantic representations are extracted from the same LALM under evaluation, enabling semantic control over variation as perceived by the model itself. Experiments on simulated data and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach substantially reduces spurious fairness findings and yields more robust and interpretable estimates of subgroup performance differences.

</details>


### [74] [GRPO Beyond English: A Large-Scale Study of GRPO in Non-English and Multilingual Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13698)
*Konstantin Dobler,Federico Scozzafava,Jonathan Janke,Mohamed Ali,Simon Lehnerer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对当前强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）研究以英语为中心的问题，开展了一项大规模多语言/非英语GRPO实证研究，覆盖多种基础模型、训练语言与推理语言奖励设置。结果显示用母语训练推理与用英语训练差距很小，且存在强跨语言迁移，但具体趋势高度依赖模型与语言，某些语言训练会对其他语言产生严重退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（常与GRPO配合）已成为提升预训练语言模型推理能力的关键方法，然而现有研究严重偏向英语。为填补这一空白，作者希望系统探究非英语/多语言场景下GRPO训练的效果、迁移规律及其潜在风险。

Method: 在大规模实证框架下，系统性地改变三个变量进行对照实验：基础模型类型、训练语言（涵盖多种语言及英语）、推理语言奖励（即奖励信号所针对的推理语言），以评估不同配置下的推理能力表现，并分析跨语言迁移与退化现象。

Result: 实验发现：1）用母语训练推理与用英语训练推理之间的性能差距通常很小；2）存在显著的跨语言迁移，一种语言上的训练往往能提升多种其他语言的表现；3）但具体趋势高度依赖模型与语言组合，某些情况下特定语言训练会导致其他语言域外能力严重退化。

Conclusion: 多语言RLVR能为跨语言能力带来广泛收益，但也暴露出语言相关的退化风险，因此必须在广泛的多语言评测下开展，以检测并规避特定语言上的性能回退。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), often optimized with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has become a central recipe for improving the reasoning capabilities of pretrained language models but current studies remain heavily English-centric. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of multilingual and non-English GRPO across a wide range of base models, training languages, and different reasoning language rewards. We find that training to reason in the native language often leaves only a small gap to training for English reasoning. We further observe strong crosslingual transfer: training in one language often improves performance in many others. However, specific trends are highly model- and language-dependent. In some cases, training in a particular language induces severe regressions on out-of-domain capabilities in other languages. Our analysis shows that RLVR beyond English can provide broad crosslingual gains, but also requires broad evaluation to detect language-specific regressions.

</details>


### [75] [CLAIR-Fin: An Adversarial Multi-Agent Framework for Claim-Level Verification and Adaptive Debate in Cross-Modal Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13706)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,Jubayer Al Mahmud,M. F. Mridha,Md. Alam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLAIR-Fin，一个九智能体框架，通过将问题分解为原子声明并维护在类型化金融声明分类账中，结合非对称证据权威、证据链验证、自适应反驳循环与终结蕴含审计，在金融跨模态问答中显著提升忠实度（0.780→0.889）并允许在证据不足时拒绝作答（5.4%），优于 HyDE 与 Graph-RAG 等强检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 与多智能体流水线的幻觉防御是局部的：证据在模态不一致时仍被信任；辩论仅验证汇总报告而非逐条声明；验证只发生在草稿完成之后，导致智能体间的错误直到最终文本才被发现。作者旨在弥合这一差距，实现证据信任的细粒度、逐声明、全流程验证。

Method: CLAIR-Fin 包含四个核心机制：(1) Asymmetric Evidence Authority——按声明类型而非将所有模态同等对待来决定证据信任；(2) Chain-of-Custody Verification——在草拟与对抗性审查的交接点而非流水线出口检查依据；(3) Adaptive Rebuttal Cycle——将争议声明路由至对抗性辩论，辩论深度随辩论结果自适应扩展；(4) 终结蕴含审计配合持续 Hallucination Risk Index，区分通过审查的声明与从未被争议的声明。整体由九智能体协作，将每个问题分解为原子声明并维护在类型化 Financial Claim Ledger 中。

Result: 在基于孟加拉国央行年报构建的 500 题跨模态金融评测集 BB-FinQA-X（按查询类型、格式与难度分层）上，相对单遍 RAG 基线，忠实度从 0.780 提升至 0.889，同时在对 5.4% 的问题在证据不足时选择弃答而非强行生成；并超越了 HyDE、Graph-RAG 等更强检索策略基线（忠实度≤0.874）。

Conclusion: CLAIR-Fin 表明：通过声明级粒度、类型感知的证据信任、交接点验证与自适应对抗辩论，可以在不牺牲覆盖率的前提下显著降低金融领域跨模态 RAG/多智能体系统的幻觉，并为无法支持的查询提供受控弃答机制。

Abstract: Existing defenses against hallucination in retrieval-augmented and multi-agent pipelines remain partial: evidence is trusted despite modality disagreement, debate verifies an aggregate report rather than individual claims, and such verification occurs only after drafting, leaving inter-agent errors undetected until the final text. To close this gap, we present CLAIR-Fin, a nine-agent framework that decomposes each question into atomic claims maintained in a typed Financial Claim Ledger. Each claim is resolved through Asymmetric Evidence Authority, which conditions evidence trust on claim type rather than treating all modalities as equally reliable; Chain-of-Custody Verification, which checks grounding at the hand-off between drafting and adversarial review rather than only at the pipeline's exit; an Adaptive Rebuttal Cycle, which routes contested claims through adversarial debate whose depth scales with what that debate finds; and a terminal entailment audit paired with a continuous Hallucination Risk Index that distinguishes claims that passed scrutiny from claims never contested. We evaluate CLAIR-Fin on BB-FinQA-X, a 500-question cross-modal financial evaluation set built from Bangladesh Bank Annual Report material, stratified by query type, format, and difficulty. Relative to a single-pass retrieval-augmented generation baseline, it raises faithfulness ($0.780 \rightarrow 0.889$) while abstaining on 5.4% of questions when evidence is insufficient rather than forcing an unsupported response, and it exceeds stronger retrieval-strategy baselines such as HyDE and Graph-RAG on faithfulness ($\leq 0.874$).

</details>


### [76] [TeachMateGPT: A Multi-Agent Knowledge-Grounded Framework for Pedagogical Assessment Generation from Science Curriculum Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13708)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,M. F. Mridha,Jubayer Al Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TeachMateGPT，一个面向课程锚定科学测评题自动生成的多智能体系统，通过四项创新（层级知识库 COPE、分阶段 fail-closed 智能体流水线、来源归因验证协议 SAVER 及课程锚定数据集 NCTB-SciGen8）解决了现有 RAG 系统在扁平检索、仅单题生成、缺乏证据防护及低资源考试大纲课程适配性上的不足，将忠实度从 0.68 提升至 0.96、答案相关性从 0.60 提升至 0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成教材锚定的测评题可减轻科学教师负担，但现有检索增强生成（RAG）系统存在明显局限：仅采用扁平化检索、只支持单题生成、缺乏对薄弱证据的防护机制，且难以适配低资源、以考试大纲为结构的课程体系。作者旨在克服这些缺点，构建能按教学层级匹配证据、在证据不足时拒绝生成、并通过教师参与验证的测评题生成系统。

Method: TeachMateGPT 是一个多智能体系统，包含四项核心贡献：(i) COPE——层级知识库，用多分辨率索引取代 token-window 分块，沿教学大纲结构切分文档，并通过可遍历的图式谱系在三种粒度上建立链接，使证据匹配各主题的教学层级；(ii) 分阶段、fail-closed 智能体流水线，取代一次性 retrieve-then-generate：路由门控搜索、检索融合稠密与词汇证据并在覆盖率门控下于证据不足时拒绝生成，由专家智能体分别起草客观题与构答题；(iii) SAVER——来源归因验证协议，依据检索证据评估忠实度、相关性与幻觉风险，对每道创造性题的四个子部分施加更严格的接地检查，并采用教师参与式评估而非自动过滤；(iv) NCTB-SciGen8——覆盖 NCTB 八年级科学教材全部 14 章的课程锚定数据集，含 198 个题目（143 道多选题、55 道创造性题），由流水线生成并经三位在职教师评分。

Result: TeachMateGPT 相对于 vanilla RAG 系统，将忠实度从 0.68 提高到 0.96，答案相关性从 0.60 提高到 0.89。同时构建了 NCTB-SciGen8 数据集，覆盖全部 14 章、包含 198 个测评题，并由三位实践教师参与评估，验证了系统在低资源、以考试大纲为结构的课程（孟加拉 NCTB 教材）上的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过层级知识库、fail-closed 智能体流水线与来源归因验证协议的组合，TeachMateGPT 在测评题生成的忠实度和相关性上取得显著提升，并为低资源、考试大纲结构课程提供了可复用的课程锚定数据集；教师参与的评估机制取代全自动过滤，更符合教学实践的需求，为课程锚定测评题自动化生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Automatically generating textbook-grounded assessment items can reduce science teachers' workload, but existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems rely on flat retrieval, support only single-question generation, lack safeguards against weak evidence, and are ill-suited to low-resource, board-exam-structured curricula. We address these limitations with TeachMateGPT, a multi-agent system contributing four advances to curriculum-grounded science-assessment authoring. (i) COPE, a hierarchical knowledge base replacing token-window chunking with a multi-resolution index that segments documents along syllabus structure and links them at three granularities via a traversable graph-based lineage, matching evidence to each topic's instructional level. (ii) A staged, fail-closed agent pipeline replacing one-shot retrieve-then-generate: routing gates search, retrieval fuses dense and lexical evidence under a coverage gate that withholds generation on insufficient evidence, and specialist agents draft objective and constructed-response items. (iii) SAVER, a source-attributed verification protocol scoring faithfulness, relevance, and hallucination risk against retrieved evidence, applying stricter grounding checks across each creative question's four sub-parts, paired with teacher-in-the-loop evaluation rather than automatic filtering. (iv) NCTB-SciGen8, a curriculum-grounded dataset of 198 items (143 multiple-choice, 55 creative questions) spanning all 14 chapters of the NCTB Class 8 science textbook, produced by the pipeline and rated by three practicing teachers. TeachMateGPT raises faithfulness (0.68 $\rightarrow$ 0.96) and answer relevancy (0.60 $\rightarrow$ 0.89) over a vanilla RAG baseline.

</details>


### [77] [StreamHear: Domain-Adapted Pseudo-Labeling for Semi-Supervised Streaming Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13717)
*Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Sangwoo Cho,Xujun Peng,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StreamHear 是一种半监督流水线，用于将预训练的流式 ASR 学生模型适配到域偏移的目标音频上：先在带标签数据上微调离线 transducer 教师模型，再对无标签数据生成伪标签，最后在混合数据上微调学生模型；同时引入基于先验正则化的动态规划重对齐步骤，利用 ASR 假设锚点修正块级词位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式自动语音识别（ASR）在域偏移的目标音频上表现不佳；带域内标签的数据获取成本高昂，而无标签音频却十分充足，因此需要充分利用无标签数据来适配流式模型。

Method: 提出半监督训练流水线 StreamHear：（1）在带标签训练集上微调离线 transducer 教师模型；（2）用教师模型对无标签数据生成伪标签；（3）在标签与伪标签的混合数据上微调预训练的流式学生模型。此外引入一个先验正则化的动态规划重对齐步骤，借助 ASR 假设锚点修正块级（chunk-level）词对齐位置，以改善流式解码中的词放置。

Result: 在金融电话会议、准备性朗读语音和电话质量对话四个数据集上，StreamHear 始终优于仅用带标签数据的监督式学生微调，并缩小了流式学生与离线教师之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 半监督伪标签配合重对齐机制可有效适配流式 ASR 到新领域，且能在无需昂贵标注的前提下显著提升流式模型的域外性能，逼近离线教师水平。

Abstract: Streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) underperforms on domain-shifted target audio, where labeled in-domain data is costly to prepare while unlabeled audio is abundant. We present StreamHear, a semi-supervised pipeline that adapts a pretrained streaming student by fine-tuning an offline transducer teacher on the labeled training set, generating pseudo-labels on the unlabeled portion, and fine-tuning the student on the mixture. We further introduce a prior-regularized dynamic-programming realignment step that fixes chunk-level word placement using an ASR-hypothesis anchor. Across four datasets spanning financial calls, prepared read speech, and phone-quality dialogue, StreamHear consistently outperforms supervised student fine-tuning and narrows the gap to the offline teacher.

</details>


### [78] [BM25-Augmented Many-Shot Translation for Low-Resource North-Eastern Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13722)
*Aashish Dhawan,Christopher Driggers-Ellis,Dzmitry Kasinets,Christan Grant,Daisy Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了佛罗里达大学Gators团队参加WMT26低资源印度语言翻译共享任务的系统：在不进行任何微调的情况下，利用BM25检索增强的多示例翻译流水线（源自其AmericasNLP 2026系统），在英语与十一种印度东北部语言之间进行双向翻译。训练库由官方WMT26数据与Samanantar等公开语料组成，并通过网格搜索为全部22个语言方向对选择最优的检索数r和开发示例数d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向WMT26低资源印度语言翻译任务，特别是英语与印度东北部十一种语言之间资源极度稀缺的双向翻译场景，旨在验证一种无需微调、轻量且可迁移的检索增强上下文学习方法，以降低对大规模平行语料和计算资源的依赖。

Method: 采用检索增强的多示例（many-shot）翻译流水线：推理时先用BM25从每种语言专属的训练库中检索最相似的平行示例，再将这些示例作为上下文条件输入给Gemini 2.5 Flash进行翻译；全程不涉及模型微调。训练库整合官方WMT26数据与Samanantar、往年WMT共享任务等公开语料；并对检索数量r与开发示例数量d在全部22个语言方向对上做网格搜索以选取最优配置。

Result: 通过网格搜索为每个语言方向对确定了最佳检索配置；在无需微调的条件下完成了22个方向对的英-印东北部语言双向翻译，表明检索增强多示例翻译方案在低资源场景下可行且具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了基于商用LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash）与BM25检索相结合、零微调的翻译流水线可有效处理低资源印度语言翻译，其通用性和可扩展性使其易于推广到其他低资源语言对。

Abstract: This paper describes the University of Florida Gators submission to the WMT26 Low-Resource Indic Language Translation shared task. We adapt the retrieval-augmented many-shot translation pipeline from our AmericasNLP 2026 system to translate between English and eleven North-Eastern Indian languages in both directions. At inference time, BM25 retrieves the most similar parallel examples from a language-specific training bank, and Gemini 2.5 Flash translates the input conditioned on these examples. No model fine-tuning is involved. Training banks combine official WMT26 data with publicly available corpora such as Samanantar and prior WMT shared task releases. A grid search over retrieval count r and development exemplar count d across all 22 language-direction pairs selects the best configuration for each submission.

</details>


### [79] [GALA: Generation-Aware Cross-Modal Alignment for Text-to-Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13741)
*Haochen Zhang,Gengwei Zhang,Laura Yao,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GALA 提出一种“生成感知的跨模态对齐”方法，用于文本条件时间序列生成：先通过对比学习配合辅助生成损失，将预训练文本编码器与时间序列基础模型对齐到共享嵌入空间，再冻结该文本嵌入驱动流匹配生成器。在 TSFragment-600K 上，GALA 在 36 个指标列中取得 30 个第一，平均排名（1.08/1.08/1.42，长度 24/48/96）全面优于最强基线（1.92/2.00/1.75），并打破了保真度与文本一致性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件时间序列生成器要么直接使用离壳文本编码器的冻结嵌入，要么端到端微调编码器让去噪损失顺带塑造嵌入；无论哪种方式，条件表示都从未与信号模态刻意对齐，因此难以有效引导生成过程。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 通过对比学习将预训练文本编码器与时间序列基础模型耦合到共享嵌入空间，并用辅助生成损失对两个编码器同时进行面向生成的适配；(2) 冻结所得文本嵌入，用于驱动流匹配（flow-matching）生成器。

Result: 在涵盖四个领域、三种片段长度的 TSFragment-600K 上刷新 SOTA：36 个指标列中 30 个排名第一，在长度 24/48/96 下平均排名为 1.08/1.08/1.42，而最强基线为 1.92/2.00/1.75。此外，生成器内部文本编码器会在保真度与文本一致性间造成权衡，而基于对齐嵌入的条件化同时提升了 FID、CTTP 和 JFTSD；消融辅助损失会使三者同时下降。

Conclusion: 生成项是对齐的必要组成部分而非可选项；将条件表示与信号模态刻意对齐，可以同时改善时间序列生成的保真度与文本遵循度，打破原有权衡。

Abstract: Synthesizing time series from natural language is emerging as the most expressive form of controllable time series generation. However, existing text-conditioned generators either take caption embeddings frozen from off-the-shelf text encoders, or adapt the encoder end-to-end, letting the denoising loss shape the embeddings only as a by-product. In either case, the conditioning representation is never deliberately matched to the signal modality, leaving it ill-suited to guide generation. We address this by introducing GALA: Generation-Aware cross-modaL Alignment for text conditional time series generation. GALA is a two-stage approach that first contrastively couples a pretrained text encoder with a time-series foundation model into a shared embedding space with both encoders adapted to generation by an auxiliary generative loss, and then freezes the resulting caption embedding to drive a flow-matching generator. On TSFragment-600K, spanning four domains and three fragment lengths, GALA sets a new state of the art, ranking first in 30 of 36 metric columns and reaching an average rank of 1.08/1.08/1.42 at lengths 24/48/96 against 1.92/2.00/1.75 for the strongest baseline. We further find that generator-internal text encoders force a trade-off between fidelity and caption adherence, whereas conditioning on the aligned embedding breaks it: FID, CTTP, and JFTSD all improve at once. Ablating the auxiliary loss degrades FID, CTTP and JFTSD together, it indicates the generative term is a necessary component of the alignment rather than an add-on.

</details>


### [80] [Amplified Does Not Mean Predictive: Reasoning Behaviors in Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13760)
*Jean de Dieu Nyandwi,Leena Mathur,Yonatan Bisk,Robert Hawkins,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过行为提升（Behavioral Lift）指标量化推理模型中各类推理行为与正确答案的关联程度，发现推理导向训练存在"放大-提升缺口"（Amplification-Lift Gap）：思考模型大幅放大自我纠正、假设检验和不确定性承认等行为，但实际与正确性关联最强的却是置信度校准、知识对齐和自我意识。研究基于15个模型、6个基准、15,282条轨迹，涵盖文本与视觉-语言推理，揭示推理导向训练并未优先放大那些与正确性最相关的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分两类问题：哪些推理行为与正确答案相关，以及推理导向训练是否放大了这些行为。由于推理导向训练可能使推理轨迹看起来更加深思熟虑，却未必放大与正确性最相关的行为，这一区分对理解模型正确性的来源至关重要。

Method: 提出Behavioral Lift指标，度量某行为在推理轨迹中存在与不存在时模型正确率的变化量。对15个模型、6个基准（覆盖纯文本与视觉-语言推理）的15,282条轨迹进行标注，使用一个同时适用于LLM与VLM轨迹的、以核心行为构成的分类体系。

Result: 发现明显的Amplification-Lift Gap：思考模型强烈放大自我纠正、假设检验和不确定性承认，而提升度最高的行为是置信度校准、知识对齐和自我意识。置信度校准在两种模态中都是正确性最强的正向信号之一，却几乎未被放大；不确定性承认被放大3-7倍，却与正确性呈弱相关或负相关。

Conclusion: 推理导向训练并未优先放大最高提升度的行为，这一发现呼吁采用面向过程的目标函数，奖励校准且基于事实（grounded）的推理，而非仅仅奖励表面形式。

Abstract: Which reasoning behaviors are associated with correct answers in reasoning models, and does reasoning-oriented training amplify those behaviors? This distinction is important because reasoning-oriented training can make traces look more deliberative without amplifying the behaviors most tied to model correctness. We quantify this mismatch with Behavioral Lift, a metric that measures how much correctness changes when a behavior is present versus absent in a model's reasoning trace. Across 15 models and 6 benchmarks spanning text-only and vision-language reasoning, we annotate 15,282 traces with a taxonomy whose core behaviors are defined for both LLM and VLM traces. We find evidence for an Amplification-Lift Gap, in which thinking models strongly amplify self-correction, hypothesis testing, and uncertainty acknowledgment, while the highest-lift behaviors are confidence calibration, knowledge alignment, and self-awareness. Confidence calibration is among the strongest positive signals of correctness in both modalities, yet is barely amplified; uncertainty acknowledgment is amplified by 3--7$\times$, yet is weakly or negatively associated with correctness. We find that reasoning-oriented training does not preferentially amplify the highest-Lift behaviors, motivating process-level objectives that reward calibrated and grounded reasoning rather than surface form alone.

</details>


### [81] [When Lexical Change Misleads: Rethinking Dynamic Topic Model Evaluation with Traditional and LLM-Based Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13835)
*Charu Karakkaparambil James*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态主题模型在词汇变化但语义保持时传统一致性指标的失效问题。通过对 CoNTM 和 DLDA 在 NYT、DBLP、arXiv 三个数据集上的 120 个主题进行人工评估，发现传统时间一致性指标与人工判断的相关性极不稳定（ρ=-0.256 至 0.614），而基于 LLM 的语义相似度在 CoNTM 上与人工语义判断高度一致（ρ=0.502–0.721），在 DLDA 上一致性较差。作者主张采用词汇变化感知的评估框架，将传统一致性与 LLM 语义度量作为互补指标联合报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态主题模型需要捕捉随时间演化的词分布，但当词汇构成发生变化而语义含义保持稳定时，传统一致性指标可能失效，无法真实反映主题质量。现有聚合式评估掩盖了不同词汇变化程度下的表现差异，因此需要更可靠的评估方案。

Method: 从 CoNTM 和 DLDA 中提取 120 个主题，覆盖 NYT、DBLP、arXiv 三个数据集，由三位人工标注者按 Low、Medium、High 三档词汇变化程度进行分类评估；对比传统时间一致性指标与基于 LLM 的语义相似度分数，并针对不同词汇变化层级进行分层分析。

Result: 传统时间一致性指标与人工判断的相关系数波动极大（ρ=-0.256 至 0.614）；LLM 语义相似度与人工语义判断在 CoNTM 上表现强相关（NYT ρ=0.609、DBLP ρ=0.721、arXiv ρ=0.502），但在 DLDA 上一致性较弱。词汇变化分层分析揭示了聚合评估所掩盖的差异。

Conclusion: 建议采用词汇变化感知的评估方法，将传统一致性指标与基于 LLM 的语义度量作为互补而非替代的指标联合使用，以更全面、可靠地评估动态主题模型。

Abstract: Dynamic topic models capture evolving word distributions, but traditional coherence metrics may fail when vocabulary changes while semantic meaning persists. We evaluate 120 topics from CoNTM and DLDA across NYT, DBLP, and arXiv, using three human annotators and Low, Medium, and High lexical-change categories. Traditional temporal coherence shows highly variable agreement with human judgments ($ρ$=-0.256 to 0.614). In contrast, LLM-based semantic similarity agrees strongly with human semantic judgments for CoNTM on NYT ($ρ$=0.609), DBLP ($ρ$=0.721), and arXiv ($ρ$=0.502), but is less consistent for DLDA. Lexical-change stratification reveals variation hidden by aggregate evaluation. We therefore advocate lexical-change-aware evaluation, jointly reporting traditional coherence and LLM-based semantic measures as complementary rather than interchangeable signals.

</details>


### [82] [ASSERT: A Measurement Pipeline for GenAI Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13840)
*Riccardo Fogliato,Abhinav Palia,Xiawei Wang,Emily Sheng,Chad Atalla,Jean Garcia-Gathright,Nicholas Pangakis,Sharman Tan,Dan Vann,Hannah Washington,P. Alex Dow,Heba Elfardy,Hanna Wallach,Sandeep Atluri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASSERT，一个规范驱动的生成式 AI（GenAI）审计测量流水线，它将每个报告的合规率与测量选择（如对话设置、模拟用户、评判者、违规证据标准）的书面规范绑定，使审计结果差异更易归因，并在对话欺骗案例研究中表明这些测量选择会显著改变报告率乃至系统排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI 审计通常将系统行为汇总为一个报告率，用于比较系统、追踪回归和门控部署。但报告率同时反映被测系统和其背后的测量选择，导致速率变化时难以判断是系统行为变化还是测量选择变化所致，因此需要一种能将报告率与测量选择规范明确绑定的方法。

Method: ASSERT 是一个规范驱动的测量流水线：它帮助起草行为评分标准（behavioral rubric）和测试用例，然后对 GenAI 系统执行审计并返回报告率，同时将每个报告率关联到一份书面测量规格说明，从而显式记录产生该速率的所有测量选择。

Result: 在对话欺骗（conversational deception）案例研究中，报告率会随对话设置、模拟用户、评判者以及违规证据标准等因素发生显著变化，这些测量选择不仅能大幅改变报告率，还可能导致 GenAI 系统的排名发生重排。

Conclusion: 由于每个报告率都与显式规格绑定，不同审计之间的差异更容易被归因于具体测量选择，从而提升 GenAI 审计结果的透明度、可比性与可解释性。

Abstract: Audits of generative AI (GenAI) systems often summarize behavior as a reported rate: how often the audited system complies with policy. Researchers and stakeholders use that rate to compare systems, track regressions, and gate deployment. A reported rate reflects both the system under audit and the measurement choices behind it, so a change in the rate can leave it unclear whether the system or those choices moved. We introduce ASSERT, a specification-driven measurement pipeline for GenAI audits that ties each reported rate to a written specification of the measurement choices used to produce it. ASSERT helps draft a behavioral rubric and test cases, then runs the audit against a GenAI system and returns a reported rate. In a case study on conversational deception, we observe that the reported rate moves substantially with the dialogue setup, the simulated user, the judge, and the evidence bar for non-compliance. These measurement choices substantially change the reported rate and can reorder GenAI system rankings. Because each reported rate is tied to an explicit specification, differences across audits are easier to attribute and interpret.

</details>


### [83] [Bootstrapping Niche Multilingual Code Translation via Reinforcement Learning with Execution-Based Verifiable Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13854)
*Kouki Yuki,Jie Zeng,Kyoko Ogawa,Ryunosuke Ikeda,Yohei Kobashi,Takeshi Kojima,Ikuya Yamada,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多语言（many-to-many）代码翻译中因监督数据稀疏导致"看似合理但不可执行"的问题，本文提出一种由执行结果驱动的偏好强化学习训练流程：先用可验证的 Python 种子程序生成多语言可执行代码池，再用基础 LLM 生成跨语言候选并依据执行结果构造偏好，训练奖励模型，最后基于 GRPO 在 600 个有向语言对（25×24）上优化模型；同时提出执行型基准 HumanEval-X++。在 Qwen-3.5 4B/9B 上取得一致提升，其中 4B 模型在 HumanEval-X++ 上平均提升 13%，中端语言上提升 21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代码翻译长期集中于 C++、Java、Python 等少数热门语言，而真正的多对多翻译场景中并行监督数据稀疏，导致模型生成"看似合理但不具备可执行语义"的翻译结果。作者旨在填补这一小众但重要的多对多翻译空白。

Method: 提出由执行监督驱动的偏好强化学习流水线：(1) 将可验证的种子 Python 程序扩展为经过执行验证的多语言代码池；(2) 由基础 LLM 跨语言对生成翻译候选，并按执行结果为其标注偏好标签；(3) 用这些偏好训练一个能打分跨语言翻译质量的奖励模型；(4) 以奖励模型为信号，在 600 个有向语言对（25×24）上对基础 LLM 执行 GRPO 优化；同时构建执行型基准 HumanEval-X++，将 HumanEval-X 扩展到广泛的多对多语言空间以评估该能力。

Result: 在 Qwen-3.5 4B 与 9B 模型上评估，该方法在 HumanEval-X++ 及现有基准上相较未训练基线均取得一致提升。其中 4B 模型在 HumanEval-X++ 上跨语言平均提升 13%，对中端语言（mid-tier）的增益达 21%。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了由数据生成、训练到基准评测组成的可靠技术路线，验证了执行监督偏好强化学习能有效提升多对多编程语言翻译质量，为进一步"自举"（bootstrapping）改进该任务奠定了基础。

Abstract: Code translation must preserve executable behavior across many programming languages, yet neural code translation has largely focused on a few popular languages such as C++, Java, and Python. This leaves a niche, many-to-many setting where parallel supervision is sparse, producing plausible but non-executable translations. We address this setting with preference-based reinforcement learning driven by execution-based supervision. Our pipeline firstly expands verifiable seed Python programs into a multilingual pool of execution-validated codes. Using the pool, a base LLM generates translation candidates across language pairs, which we label by their execution outcomes. The resulting preferences are used to train a reward model that scores cross-language translation quality. Finally, we optimize our base LLMs with GRPO over 600 directed language pairs (25 x 24) using the reward model as a signal. To evaluate the niche translation capability, we introduce HumanEval-X++, an execution-based benchmark that extends HumanEval-X to a broad many-to-many language space. We evaluate our approach using Qwen-3.5 4B and 9B models. On HumanEval-X++ and existing benchmarks, it yields consistent gains over the untrained baselines. In particular, the 4B model achieves an average improvement of 13% across all languages on HumanEval-X++, with a gain of 21% on mid-tier languages. Our study establishes a reliable approach of data generation, training, and benchmarking, paving the way toward further bootstrapping the quality of many-to-many translation for programming languages.

</details>


### [84] [Scaling Creative Writing Beyond Story-Centric Data with Attribute-Guided Genre Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13947)
*Hwan Chang,Yongil Kim,Heuiyeen Yeen,Yireun Kim,Jinsik Lee,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种属性引导的体裁扩展框架，将主题广度与体裁形式控制分离，利用人类撰写的故事提示作为创意种子，结合人工策展的体裁属性，生成并筛选跨越13种创意体裁（共5万条示例）的高质量写作数据（Multi-Genre Collection），使微调后的大语言模型在分布外写作基准和体裁诊断上全面超越基座模型及各写作基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的高质量创意写作数据以故事为中心占据主导，限制了模型遵循多样化创意格式的结构性与功能性惯例的能力，亟需扩展故事生成之外的体裁覆盖。

Method: 采用属性引导的体裁扩展框架：将主题广度与体裁形式控制解耦，用人类撰写的故事提示作为多样化创意种子，同时利用人工策展的体裁属性强制约束特定的结构、风格与格式惯例；组合两者促使强LLM生成体裁忠实的问题-回答对，再经质量过滤，构建50K样本、覆盖13种创意体裁（故事、说唱、歌词、剧本、游戏设计、角色设计等）的语料库。

Result: 在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上，用该数据微调的模型一致超越基座模型、写作专精基线以及基于现有写作语料训练的模型；体裁数量消融实验表明，受控的体裁扩展（而非仅靠故事中心扩展）是提升鲁棒写作能力的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 受控的多样化体裁扩展比单纯的故事数据扩展更能有效提升LLM的创意写作能力，Multi-Genre Collection可作为一种可扩展、可复用的数据构建范式。

Abstract: High-quality creative writing data for large language models (LLMs) remains dominated by story-centric data, limiting models' ability to follow the structural and functional conventions of diverse creative formats. We propose an attribute-guided genre expansion framework for scaling creative writing data beyond story generation. By separating thematic breadth from genre-form control, our framework leverages human-authored story prompts as diverse creative seeds, while utilizing manually curated genre attributes to enforce distinct structural, stylistic, and formatting conventions. We combine these to prompt strong LLMs for genre-faithful query-response pairs, which are then quality-filtered. Applying this framework, we construct the Multi-Genre Collection, a 50K-example corpus spanning 13 creative genres, including story, rap, lyrics, scripts, game design, character design, and other creative formats. Experiments across out-of-distribution writing benchmarks and held-out genre diagnostics demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our data consistently surpass not only base models and writing-specialized baselines, but also models trained on existing writing corpora. Genre-count ablations further indicate that controlled genre expansion, rather than story-centric scaling alone, is a key driver of robust creative writing capability.

</details>


### [85] [Repair, Not Improvement: Decomposing Constrained Decoding in Tool-Call Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13959)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL
