TL;DR: 本文测量了约束生成文献明确排除的用例——函数调用中的工具弃权（tool abstention）：在逐字节相同的提示上，用三种条件分离文法的两个职能（确定生成何时停止、限定可发射的token），在0.6B至4B开源模型及英韩配对项目上评估。结果显示，与无约束解码器相比，先验方法在弃权指标上的对比在六个单元中四个为负（最差-29.5分，置信区间排除零），无一为正；其修复主要改善"形式"（698例修复的弃权中545例原本无可读答案，0例被评分者拒绝），而非正确性。总体上干预利弊相抵乃至为负，且两个预注册的语言假设均未成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的约束生成研究明确将函数调用排除在外：其作者发现解码器对格式约束的贡献很小，并在第7节警告勿将结论外推到"约束编码正确性要求"的情形，且点名函数调用。工具弃权正是该情形最尖锐的版本：枚举文法不改动答案措辞、只收窄答案集合，而"不调用任何工具"正是被最先丢弃的选项。本文旨在测量这个被排除的用例。

Method: 采用单一逐字节相同的提示（prompt）构造三种条件，以区分文法的两个职能：一是决定生成在何处停止（stop token），二是限定可发射的token（enum/enumeration）。在0.6B至4B的开源权重模型上，对匹配的英语和韩语项目进行项目内（within-item）语言对比评估，并以弃权（abstention）为预注册的主要指标。

Result: 与无约束解码器相比，先验工作中的对比在六个单元中有四个在弃权指标上为负（置信区间排除零），最差-29.5分，且没有单元为正（区间排除零）。两个效应的符号相反：最小模型在韩语上，停止token代价-20.0，枚举回报+19.5，两者合计-0.5。干预所"恢复"的是形式：698例被修复的弃权中，545例原本无可读答案，0例是评分者拒绝的判断。在需要工具的项目上，干预全程为正；弃权之所以是主要指标是因为它是预注册的，而合并后的数字对干预更有利，改为用它反而更糟。

Conclusion: 在被排除的函数调用/工具弃权场景中，约束生成的收益主要是形式层面的（把不可读输出变为可读），而非正确性层面的；以预注册的弃权衡量，约束解码总体为负或利弊相抵；两个预注册的语言相关假设均未得到支持，说明不应把先前关于格式约束的结论外推到此类正确性约束场景。

Abstract: Function calling is what the recent accounting of constrained generation explicitly sets aside: it finds the decoder's contribution small for format constraints, then warns in its Section 7 against extrapolating where a constraint encodes a correctness requirement, and names function calling as one. Tool abstention is that case at its sharpest: an enum leaves the wording of an answer alone and narrows the set of answers there are, and declining to call anything is the first it drops. We measure the excluded case. Three conditions over one byte-identical prompt separate a grammar's two jobs: it fixes where generation stops as well as which tokens may be emitted. We evaluate open-weight models from 0.6B to 4B on matched English and Korean items, so the language comparison is made within item. Against an unconstrained decoder, prior work's contrast is negative on abstention in four of six cells with intervals excluding zero, worst -29.5 points, and positive with an interval excluding zero in none. The total is a sum with opposite signs: on the smallest model in Korean the stop token costs -20.0, the enum returns +19.5, and the two leave -0.5. What it recovers is form: of 698 abstentions repaired, 545 had no readable answer and 0 were judgements the scorer refused. On tool-needed items it is positive throughout; abstention leads because it is the preregistered measure, and the pooled number being kinder to the intervention makes moving to it worse rather than better. Both preregistered language claims fail.

</details>


### [86] [Batch-wise Adaptive Pruning: Periodic Neuron Activation-Aware Weight Pruning for Language Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14003)
*Yongmin Kim,Shota Takashiro,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大型推理模型（LRM）在批量推理场景下现有免训练自适应剪枝方法失效的问题，提出一种专为批量推理设计的免训练自适应剪枝方法，采用周期性top-k重要性选择取代阈值选择，并引入跨更新阶段累积重要性的激活记忆机制，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上以50%稀疏度、batch size 4取得相比此前最优方法高出39.7个百分点的平均准确率，并获得1.40倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM通过延长思维链获得强性能，但推理代价高昂；生产环境必须使用批量推理以保证吞吐。然而现有免训练自适应剪枝方法在批量推理下严重退化：批量必须共享单一剪枝掩码，方法需对样本聚合激活再做阈值选择，而阈值是在未聚合激活上离线校准的，与聚合后分布不匹配，导致实际稀疏度漂移、推理任务准确率崩溃。

Method: 方法由两个组件构成：其一，用周期性的top-k选择取代基于阈值的神经元选择，作用于聚合后的重要性分数，不受聚合引起的激活分布偏移影响，且每个更新周期只执行一次选择而非每token执行，从而保留加速收益；其二，基于长推理生成过程中重要神经元会周期性重复激发的观察，引入激活记忆机制，跨更新阶段累积重要性分数，使周期性出现的神经元被持续保留。

Result: 在多个推理基准上的实验表明，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上、目标稀疏度50%、batch size 4条件下，该方法相比此前最优自适应剪枝方法在平均准确率上提升39.7个百分点；在50%稀疏度下相对稠密推理达到1.40倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了批量推理下阈值式自适应剪枝的分布漂移问题，同时通过周期化选择和激活记忆兼顾了准确率与推理加速，为LRM生产环境中的高效批量推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks through extended chain-of-thought generation, but incur substantial computational costs during inference. In production settings, batched inference is essential for high throughput, yet the existing training-free adaptive pruning methods we evaluate severely degrade in this regime. Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [87] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S2Dialog，一个面向多模态对话库的对话级语义-风格检索统一框架。它通过对话级文本检索器与对话级声学检索器分别编码文本和声学模态为对话级表示，并引入对话级文本-声学对比学习，对齐语义和风格相似的对话、区分不相关对话。在DailyTalk数据集上的大量实验表明S2Dialog有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话检索旨在从多模态对话库中检索出与目标对话在文本语义和声学会话风格上相似的对话，这对情感对话识别、口语对话系统、会话语音合成等任务至关重要。然而现有检索方法大多局限于句子级或单模态匹配，难以捕获整个对话的全局语义连贯性与风格一致性，因此需要一种对话级、多模态统一的检索框架。

Method: S2Dialog由三个核心组件构成：1) 对话级文本检索器，将对话的文本模态编码为对话级表示；2) 对话级声学检索器，将对话的声学模态编码为对话级表示；3) 对话级文本-声学对比学习，用于对齐语义与风格相似的对话并区分不相关对话，从而增强多模态检索性能。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上进行的大量实验表明，S2Dialog在对话级语义-风格检索任务上取得了显著效果，证明了其统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: S2Dialog填补了对话级多模态检索的空白，通过对话级文本/声学表示编码与跨模态对比学习，能够同时捕获对话的全局语义连贯性与声学风格一致性，为依赖外部对话示例的对话相关下游任务提供有力支撑。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [88] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HERMES，一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于从超长科学文献中提取结构化数据。该系统应用于 55 卷的《无脊椎古生物学专论》（Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology），构建了包含 32,277 个化石分类实体和 451,878 个属性的结构化数据库，并在不同化石类群间保持稳定的提取性能（实体平均 F1 约 0.90，属性约 0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学中权威的科学知识仍主要封存于传统专著和历史文献中，非结构化文本和复杂版面布局严重阻碍了计算化访问与利用，亟需一种能从超长文档中自动提取结构化数据的方法。

Method: HERMES 以协调式大语言模型（LLM）为核心，构建统一的全文档级提取流程，整合领域约束、验证规则和证据追踪，并融合解析后的文本、表格、图表及图注多模态信息，由多智能体协同完成结构化数据抽取。

Result: 系统在 55 卷专著上产出了 32,277 个分类实体与 451,878 个属性（在线发布于 https://treatise.geolex.org）；实体与属性的平均 F1 分别约为 0.90 和 0.91，跨化石类群性能稳定；与完全人工基准相比，每卷效率提升约 6 倍。此外，在不进行额外模型训练的情况下，方法可迁移至古地磁学和地球化学等不同地球科学领域。

Conclusion: 该工作为将历史科学文献转化为面向 FAIR 原则的结构化数据提供了切实可行的路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合构建了可持续的基础设施。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [89] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在具有全国代表性的新加坡受访者样本上，扩展并评估了"硅采样"（silicon sampling）的算法保真度，系统比较了"快速"与"慢速"两种模式。结果发现，硅采样仍属早期、需极度谨慎使用的方法：它能对总体均值给出中等保真度的估计，但会低估意见方差并扭曲人类意见背后的潜在情境空间。在此限制下，快速硅采样在算力与运行时间上显著更高效，且在保真度上单调优于慢速模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样有时能给出出人意料良好的总体估计，作者希望探明"高速执行"是否会削弱这种保真度，即快速模式是否仍能维持硅采样对总体的刻画能力。

Method: 在具有全国代表性的新加坡调查受访者样本上，扩展此前已有的硅采样研究工作，对"快速"与"慢速"两种硅采样模式的算法保真度进行比较评估，主要对比其在估计总体均值、意见方差及潜在情境空间等方面的表现。

Result: 当代前沿模型驱动的硅采样仍属早期发展阶段，只能对总体均值给出中等保真度的估计，且会低估意见方差并扭曲人类意见背后的潜在情境空间；条件于这些局限，快速硅采样在算力与运行时间上显著更高效，并在保真度上单调优于传统慢速硅采样。

Conclusion: 硅采样作为一种尚在早期的方法应仅被谨慎使用；在已知局限之下，快速模式是相对更优的选择——既更节省计算与时间资源，又能保持比慢速模式更好的算法保真度。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [90] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对第二届多语言对话语音语言模型（MLC-SLM）挑战赛的两个任务提出解决方案：任务1（说话人日志与识别）通过对VibeVoice-ASR-7B进行随机前导静音裁剪、一致时间戳校正和指数移动平均（EMA）训练策略微调，将tcpMER从18.30%降至16.73%；任务2（对话语音理解）通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构造合成问答对，并微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行标记式直接回答，将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战赛的两个任务均要求在完整的、未分段的多语言对话上进行评测，且不提供oracle话语边界或说话人标签；任务2甚至不提供问答训练集。这迫使参赛者必须开发无需分段先验、且能在无监督/自监督条件下构建训练数据的方法，以解决真实对话场景中说话人日志、识别与语义理解并存的问题。

Method: 任务1：在VibeVoice-ASR-7B上采用三种关键技术进行微调——随机前导静音裁剪（模拟对话开头无静音的边界情况）、一致的时间戳校正（保证ASR输出时间戳与真实边界对齐）、EMA训练策略（稳定训练并提升泛化）。任务2：先通过多模态候选生成构造合成问答对，再进行静音音频过滤以剔除无效样本，最后采用分布匹配增强使合成数据分布贴近真实评测分布；随后微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct，使其以"标记式直接回答"（tagged direct answering）的格式输出答案。

Result: 任务1评测集上：基线tcpMER为18.30%，加入裁剪后降至17.27%，再加入EMA后进一步降至16.73%，两项改进分别带来约1.0和0.5个百分点的绝对提升。任务2评测集上：联合使用分布匹配增强与标记式直接回答后，准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%，提升3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过针对对话语音特点设计的训练与推理策略（静音裁剪、EMA、合成数据增强、标记式回答），可以在不依赖oracle分段信息且缺少问答监督数据的情况下，同时显著提升说话人日志/识别精度与对话语义理解准确率，验证了所提方法在多语言完整对话场景下的有效性。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [91] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对法律领域八个检索增强生成（RAG）系统的幻觉行为进行了细粒度分析，覆盖英语GDPR和法语国家民法典两个语料库，采用声明级和答案级评估，并在142道法律专家撰写的问题上验证了发现。结果显示幻觉依然普遍存在，最佳系统约不到10%的回答受影响，而最差系统近一半回答受影响；其中包含错误假设的虚假前提问题（需被拒答）幻觉率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域RAG系统的幻觉回答可能带来严重后果，但此前缺乏对幻觉行为的细致理解。作者希望通过系统性的细粒度分析，弄清幻觉在法律RAG系统中的普遍程度、严重性以及在不同问题类型和用户角色下的分布规律，从而为改进系统提供依据。

Method: 对8个法律RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英语GDPR与法语国家民法典）上进行了细粒度幻觉分析。评估采用声明级（claim-level）和答案级（answer-level）两种粒度，测量幻觉密度（density）与严重性（severity）；按问题类别和用户画像（personas）分析性能差异，并使用142道由法律专家撰写的问题作为独立数据集进行验证，以保证结论的稳健性。

Result: 研究发现幻觉在法律RAG系统中仍然普遍存在：最佳系统的幻觉回答占比低于10%，而最差系统接近一半。特别地，虚假前提问题（包含错误假设、系统应予以拒绝的问题）在所有问题类型中产生的幻觉率最高。

Conclusion: 法律领域的RAG系统幻觉问题远未解决，且存在明显的系统间性能差异；虚假前提问题是最易诱发幻觉的挑战性场景。这提示未来的法律RAG系统需要专门针对错误前提的识别与拒答机制，并加强声明级可控性以降低幻觉密度与严重性。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [92] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM遗忘方法忽视训练数据频率的问题，提出AdaPop（自适应流行度）方法：将局部token置信度与基于外部代理（如Wikidata sitelinks、LLM-as-Judge）得到的每事实流行度指数相结合，并通过对偶上升控制器自动调节每轮次保留惩罚，从而自动平衡遗忘与保留。在三个模型族和两个基准上，AdaPop在释义查询下泄露的已遗忘内容比现有方法少约5倍，在对抗性改写下约少1.6倍，同时内部指标显示遗忘集隐状态离预遗忘模型更远而保留集表示保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对高频与低频事实一视同仁地施加相同梯度压力，但在预训练中高频（流行）事实被记忆得更深、更难被移除，稀有事实则相反。这种忽略训练数据频率的统一处理方式导致遗忘效果不佳，需要一种能感知事实流行度的自适应遗忘机制。

Method: AdaPop由两部分构成：1）将局部token置信度与每事实的流行度相关指数相结合，该指数由外部代理（如Wikidata sitelinks、LLM-as-Judge）估计；2）通过对偶上升控制器（dual-ascent controller）在每轮动态调整保留惩罚项，实现遗忘-保留权衡的自动化，无需人工调参。

Result: 在三个模型族和两个基准上，AdaPop在释义查询下泄漏的已遗忘内容比现有方法少约5倍，在对抗性改写下约少1.6倍。内部指标进一步表明：与其他方法相比，AdaPop使遗忘集的隐状态距预遗忘模型的隐藏状态更远，同时保留集的表示保持稳定（几乎没有漂移）。

Conclusion: AdaPop验证了"流行度感知（popularity-aware）"的遗忘相比于统一梯度更新的优越性，通过外部流行度代理和自适应控制器实现了更彻底的遗忘与更稳定的保留，为LLM遗忘研究提供了新的自动化、可扩展方案。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [93] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了将从长上下文推理教师模型SU-01向短上下文学生模型迁移证明推理能力的on-policy蒸馏设定，通过在共享文本空间中对齐token、引入学生参考KL损失并掩码终止token优势，解决了tokenizer不匹配、分布失配、长度爆炸与训练不稳定等问题，并在数学推理及更广泛的科学基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）是迁移强教师模型推理能力的有效途径，但将其应用于长上下文推理教师与短上下文学生模型时面临实际挑战：tokenizer不匹配、师生分布失配、响应长度爆炸以及训练不稳定，这些问题亟需解决以释放OPD在该设定下的潜力。

Method: 方法包含三个关键设计：(1) 在共享文本空间中进行OPD，仅对齐学生与教师tokenizer下占据相同文本跨度（text span）的token，从而规避tokenizer差异；(2) 引入学生参考KL损失，约束学生不过度偏离初始策略，缓解师生分布失配问题；(3) 掩码"end of response"等特殊终止token的优势，避免生成长度爆炸与频繁截断，促进长度稳定增长。

Result: 在Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4等同族与异族学生模型上均取得一致的数学推理提升，尤其在自然语言数学证明方面。Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro；并在HLE、HiPhO等科学基准上亦有提升，表明所学推理能力可泛化到更广领域。

Conclusion: 实验表明，所提出的OPD方案能有效从长上下文推理教师向短上下文学生迁移推理能力，不仅带来一致的数学推理增益，还使能力泛化到数学之外的科学任务，验证了该蒸馏框架的实用性与可推广性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [94] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Envs-FORGE 是一种将验证器奖励转化为逐种子环境合成动作的提示词策略，通过评估种子通过率、围绕目标学习前沿对六种投影方向动作评分，并用混合整数线性规划（MILP）选择动作，驱动指令、夹具、oracle 解、测试与 Docker 环境的同步重写，仅将金标准验证通过的样本送入强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向终端智能体的强化学习需要可执行且奖励可靠、难度合适的训练环境。现有的固定配方（few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对每个种子都施加相同的提示策略，即使当前策略实际上需要更难、更易或完全不同的任务，也无法实现按需的难度调节和环境多样性。

Method: Envs-FORGE 首先估计每个种子的通过率，围绕目标学习前沿对六种“投影—方向”动作进行评分，然后求解逐种子的混合整数线性规划（MILP）以选出用于约束生成的合成动作。所选动作会触发指令、夹具、oracle 解、测试与 Docker 环境的同步重写；只有经金标准验证的数据包才能进入 RL 训练。其索引化 MILP 形式还支持可选的软技能覆盖率，用于组合规划（portfolio planning）。

Result: 在 Qwen 3.5 35B 上，Envs-FORGE 相比 Base 在 tb-core 上 Pass@1 提升 9.2 个百分点（40.0%→49.2%），在 tb-2.0 上提升 6.4 个百分点（23.0%→29.4%），分别超过最强固定配方基线 2.4 和 2.1 个百分点；在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 77.1%（Base 为 73.4%）。在 4B–35B 的多种模型上，tb-core 均提升 6.8–9.2 个百分点。所有方法均产出 100 个已验证环境，消耗 2.27M–2.88M 合成 token，保证了下游训练集规模与运行成本可比。

Conclusion: Envs-FORGE 证明了依据验证器奖励自适应地选择环境合成动作可显著优于固定提示配方，且收益在多个模型规模上稳定成立，同时维持相同的训练集规模与 token 预算。所有方法在可控成本下进行公平对比，源码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [95] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究词汇多样性（数据集中独特最后一个词元/最终词元的数量）如何影响语言模型表示的内在维度（ID）估计。发现存在一个尺度依赖的转变：低词汇多样性时，独特最终词越少ID越高；高词汇多样性时顺序反转，独特词越多ID越高。作者推导出一个无参数精确公式预测反转点，与所有测试尺度下的观测一致。结果表明ID不能简单作为表示复杂度的直接指标，同时两个ID机制揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度（ID）被广泛用于探测语言模型的表示复杂度，但ID差异究竟是反映语言本身的性质，还是反映底层数据集构建方式造成的人为伪影，这一问题尚不明确。作者聚焦于词汇多样性这一具体数据集属性，探究其对ID估计的影响，以厘清ID指标的可靠性。

Method: 针对词汇多样性（数据集中独特最后词元项的数量）对数据集ID估计的影响进行系统性研究；识别出随尺度变化的两个机制（低词汇多样性区与高词汇多样性区，其中ID排序发生反转）；推导出一个精确、无参数（parameter-free）的公式来计算反转发生的临界点，并在多个尺度下验证其与观测转换点的一致性。

Result: 发现ID随词汇多样性呈尺度依赖的转变：低词汇多样性条件下，独特最终词越少ID越高；而高词汇多样性条件下顺序反转，独特词越多ID越高。推导的反转点公式为无参数精确公式，在所有测试尺度下均与观测到的转换点匹配。

Conclusion: 一方面，解释一组表示的ID时需要谨慎，它不能被直接视作表示复杂度的简单线索；另一方面，两个ID机制的发现揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般性原则，为该领域提供了新见解，深化了对LLM内部流形组织的理解。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [96] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）在体育领域应用的综合性综述，系统梳理了任务与应用、数据集与基准，并探讨了挑战与未来方向，配套开源GitHub仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育在全球范围内热情高涨，是身体健康、文化交流、社会连接与经济增长的重要力量。大模型（特别是多模态大语言模型）的快速发展展示出重塑体育理解、分析与交互的巨大潜力，但目前缺乏系统性的综述来整合该领域的研究进展。

Method: 采用综述研究的方法，从三个方面展开：（i）按不同参与群体概述体育领域的任务与应用；（ii）详细分析体育相关的数据集与基准；（iii）批判性讨论当前面临的挑战与未来研究方向。

Result: 形成了一份覆盖体育智能中任务、数据集和挑战的全面综述，并提供了开源的GitHub仓库（https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports）以支持社区共享与持续更新。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在为大模型驱动的体育智能研究与实践开发奠定基础，促进该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [97] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了摘要评估指标能否衡量摘要对具体用户的实用性（信息满足度）。在引入"读者背景/身份"（角色与专长）这一稳定信号后，发现包括强LLM-as-judge在内的多数流行指标都无法通过信息内容的扰动测试，且与传统指标、LLM指标均与专家人类评估的信息满足度判断吻合度很低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要评估工作主要关注总体质量（如ROUGE、BERTScore）或特定性质（如可读性、事实性），但这些指标无法衡量摘要对个体用户的效用。查询聚焦摘要虽部分覆盖该需求，但用户很少在查询中表达全部相关信息，短查询不足以区分研究者与医生的信息需求；而读者的背景/身份（角色与专长）跨查询相对稳定，能补回大量缺失上下文，是评估摘要是否满足读者需求的实用信号。

Method: 作者评估流行摘要指标对信息差异与身份（persona）差异的敏感性，对信息内容进行基本扰动测试；并开展专家人工评估，在给定具体人员背景和使用场景下，依据信息满足度比较摘要偏好，同时对比传统指标与LLM-as-judge指标。

Result: 许多流行指标（包括强LLM-as-judge指标）无法通过信息内容的扰动测试；在专家人工评估中，传统指标与LLM基指标都无法充分衡量信息满足度，且与人类判断的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 现有评估指标不足以衡量摘要对特定读者背景的信息满足度，说明需要新的、以用户/身份感知为中心的摘要评估方法。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [98] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 YOPO（You Only Pass Once）：训练一个小型网络以均方误差重建被 steering 覆盖前的残差流，使冻结的 Qwen2.5 骨干（1.5B/3B/7B）在单次前向传播中同时完成答题、steering 与 abstain（拒答），消除了两路研究线在同一残差流上部署时的相互干扰，三类精度相较冻结基线提升一倍以上，并首次在四域基准上提出 answer-or-abstain 评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上有两个相互耦合的弱点：一是未充分利用自身残差流中已编码的证据，二是无法检测输入信息是否充足而会臆造答案。已有两条研究线分别处理这两个问题（conditional steering probe 写入残差流提升推理精度；zero-shot sufficiency direction 读取残差流并在信息不足时弃答），但将它们放在同一次前向传播中会相互干扰（steering 写入使 sufficiency direction 读取的状态发生偏移，小模型跨域迁移损失最多 8 个 AUROC 点），而用干净的第二次前向则使推理成本翻倍。

Method: 保持 sufficiency direction 固定，训练一个小型网络以 (steered, clean) 残差对上的均方误差（无需 sufficiency 标签）重建 steering 之前的残差，然后在重建结果上读取该方向。由此 YOPO 在一次前向传播中同时完成答题、steering 与 abstain，无需额外的干净前向。

Result: 端到端三类（正确作答/错误/弃答）精度在 1.5B alphaNLI 上从冻结基线的 0.375 提升至 0.798；单次前向在每个规模上都优于两次前向的参照（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863），并在六个模型家族的十个骨干上取得一致优势。作者绘制了容量-迁移前沿，量化“abstention 不应在训练中注入”的原则；源端审计发现 alphaNLI 构造泄漏表面伪影，因此架构性结论锚定在原生标签复现（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）；在标准四域套件上贡献了据作者所知首个 answer-or-abstain 基准，其 gate 在所有域内数据集上领先，且无标签方向是唯一能在跨域迁移中存活的 gate 家族。

Conclusion: 通过在单次前向中用小型重建网络解耦 steering 写入与 sufficiency 读取，YOPO 证明冻结骨干可以在不增加推理开销的同时兼顾推理增强与可靠的拒答，且效果优于两次前向的方案；同时强调对基准构造进行源端审计、并建立 answer-or-abstain 评测基准以严谨衡量此类系统的必要性。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出洛伦兹傅里叶神经算子（L-FNO），一种面向随机事件动力学的随机神经算子，用于对现代运维系统中罕见、突发、自激发的点过程事件进行建模与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代运维系统即使在常规条件下也面临不确定性，罕见、突发且自激发的事件同时受外生协变量和内生事件动力学的驱动。标准神经算子通常被训练为回归式函数到函数的映射，而非条件强度估计器，因此在稀疏事件场景下适应性不足。

Method: L-FNO是一种随机神经算子，包含三个关键组件：基于FNO风格的协变量路径、用于刻画历史依赖激发的洛伦兹谱核，以及基于似然函数的训练目标，从而将神经算子与点过程条件强度估计相结合。

Result: 在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集（涵盖疾病爆发预测和半导体故障/缺陷检测）上进行评估，L-FNO在事件似然、校准诊断和罕见事件检测方面均优于基于回归和基于似然的神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结构化谱记忆与基于似然的学习为神经算子建模随机事件动力学提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [100] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批评了现有编码基准（如SWE-bench、LiveCodeBench）被过度当作通用编码能力的证据，并通过自建的Django案例基准套件证明：针对这些基准进行优化只会得到任务特定性能，基准排名无法泛化，SWE-bench优化在其他任务上几乎没有迁移收益，导致"测得的分数"与"宣称的通用能力"之间存在意义鸿沟。作者呼吁采用差异化评估、构建能力分类体系并持续维护基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文、模型卡和博客常把少量编码基准上的分数当作广泛编码能力的证据，但针对基准优化会使得分数只反映任务特定表现，与"通用编码能力"的宣称之间存在严重脱节（meaning gap），因此需要系统性地检验这种脱节并给出更可靠的评估标准。

Method: 作者自建了一套基于Django的案例研究基准套件，对经过SWE-bench轨迹后训练的基座模型和检查点进行评测，比较其在自建Django任务、LiveCodeBench等基准上的表现，并检验不同Django模态微调之间的迁移情况，以考察跨任务迁移与排名泛化性。

Result: 实验发现：基准排名经常无法泛化；后训练检查点几乎不存在跨任务迁移；SWE-bench优化在自建任务和LiveCodeBench上收益有限甚至为零；针对单一Django模态的微调同样无法迁移到其他模态。

Conclusion: 少量基准不足以在基准优化压力下评估多样化模型。作者建议采用差异化评估：对前沿模型做整体性评估、对研究场景用多任务套件、对窄任务应用用人在环路研究；同时呼吁构建能力分类体系并持续维护基准，而非一次性发布基准，否则用户只能依赖不充分的证据做决策。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [101] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了XGBoost的鲁棒性，指出其性能会受到垂直离群点和杠杆点的影响；通过引入基于M、S和τ估计的稳健回归损失函数，提出了一种名为MM-XGBoost的两步方法，在鲁棒性与预测精度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost作为一种广受欢迎且强大的预测方法，其标准损失函数为二次损失，也可使用Huber损失，但在垂直离群点和杠杆点存在时，其性能会显著下降，因此需要探索更鲁棒的损失函数以应对数据中的异常值。

Method: 在XGBoost框架下系统比较了多种替代损失函数，包括来自稳健回归的M估计、S估计和τ估计，并据此提出一个两阶段的MM-XGBoost流程，通过先稳健初始化再迭代优化的方式来兼顾鲁棒性与拟合能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的MM-XGBoost方法在鲁棒性和预测准确性之间提供了最佳权衡，优于使用标准二次损失或Huber损失的XGBoost以及其他稳健损失变体。

Conclusion: XGBoost确实易受异常值影响，但通过引入稳健回归的MM两步估计损失函数可以有效增强其鲁棒性，为在实际含噪数据上应用XGBoost提供了更可靠的选择。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [Forecast Collapse in Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14106)
*Shu Wan,Miles Ma,Hank Zhu,Guangqi Liu,Stephen Wang,Qingsong Wen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对1,000只美国股票的每小时收益率预测任务，本文发现了一个意外现象——"预测坍缩"（forecast collapse）：预测结果近乎平坦，股票排序能力（以横截面相关性衡量）很差。该现象在预测交易量时基本消失。研究者跨时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）、12种深度学习模型及97个公开基准配置进行系统调查，发现该现象与目标可预测性密切相关。他们识别出两个原因：低可预测性限制了校准点预测的振幅；逐序列目标函数无法识别跨序列结构。进一步发现校准-排序权衡：优化平方误差导致平坦预测，而直接优化横截面相关性虽改善排序，却可能使预测振幅膨胀一个数量级以上。为此提出CalibRank目标，在Finance1K上将横截面相关性提升近三倍，同时保持振幅接近真实目标，并在所有测试模型上改善相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在金融场景（如股票收益率预测）中常按逐序列指标评估。本文发现按此评估表现良好的模型，其预测在横截面上坍缩为近似平坦值，丧失股票间排序能力。这一盲区暴露了传统逐序列评估指标的不足，亟需理解坍缩成因并提出兼顾校准与排序的新方法。

Method: 该研究首先系统地在多元设置下复现并量化预测坍缩现象，对T时间序列基础模型、12种深度学习模型及97个公开基准配置进行大规模实证调查。分析表明坍缩与目标可预测性紧密相关，并归结为两个机制：(1) 低可预测性使校准后的点预测振幅被压缩；(2) 逐序列的训练目标无法挖掘跨序列共享结构。基于该理解，作者提出CalibRank——一种同时平衡校准（平方误差）与排序（横截面相关性）的复合目标函数。

Result: 在Finance1K基准上，CalibRank将横截面相关性提升近三倍，同时使预测振幅保持在接近真实目标的水平；该改进在所有测试的模型上均一致出现。相较之下，纯平方误差目标产生平坦预测，纯排序目标虽提升相关性却使振幅膨胀超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文揭示出传统时间序列评估的一个盲点：逐序列指标可能掩盖跨序列结构的失效。预测坍缩的发现与CalibRank目标表明，在金融等横截面结构至关重要的场景中，评估与优化都必须同时考虑序列内校准与序列间排序，才能获得既准确又具区分度的预测。

Abstract: When forecasting hourly returns for 1,000 US equities, we observe an unexpected phenomenon: predictions become nearly flat and show poor stock ranking, as measured by cross-sectional correlation. We call this forecast collapse. Surprisingly, the phenomenon largely disappears when forecasting trading volume under the same setting. We investigate forecast collapse across time-series foundation models (TSFMs), twelve deep-learning forecasting models, and 97 public benchmark configurations, and find that it is closely tied to target predictability. We identify two distinct reasons behind it: low predictability limits the amplitude of calibrated point forecasts, while per-series objectives leave cross-series structure unidentified. These findings reveal a calibration-ranking tradeoff: optimizing squared error leads to flat predictions, whereas directly optimizing cross-sectional correlation improves ranking but can inflate forecast amplitude by more than an order of magnitude. To address this tradeoff, we introduce CalibRank, a simple objective that balances calibration and ranking. On Finance1K, CalibRank nearly triples cross-sectional correlation while keeping amplitude close to the target, and improves correlation on all tested models. Our results reveal a blind spot in conventional time-series evaluation: per-series metrics can hide failures in cross-series structure needed by downstream decisions.

</details>


### [103] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨尺度异构模型融合，提出 Activation-Prune-Merge (APM) 框架：在不依赖神经元级语义对齐的前提下，通过任务条件激活图指导对强供体模型进行剪枝（层、隐藏维度、注意力头、MLP 神经元），将其剪裁为受体架构并用微插值系数注入原始 3B 受体模型。在 16 个基准上，整体平均准确率从 55.5% 提升至 60.6%，并验证了激活引导提取可获得更高质量的供体切片且保持小比例融合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构模型融合旨在合并任务、初始化、架构或规模不同的模型，但跨尺度融合（即用更强的供体模型改善较小的受体模型）尚未被充分探索。本文要回答的核心问题是：在存在显著架构失配、且不做显式神经元级语义对齐的情况下，能否实现有效的能力迁移。

Method: APM 方法基于观察：将大模型截断为较小架构并以极小的混合权重注入即可改善受体。具体地，APM 在供体上构建任务条件激活图，据此筛选显著层、隐藏维度、注意力头和 MLP 神经元，将供体剪枝为受体架构，再将所得供体切片以微插值系数注入原始受体。该方法将供体视为“浓缩功能组件”的来源，而非要求精确的结构移植。

Result: 在 16 个基准（涵盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令跟随和分类）上，相比原始 3B 受体，整体平均准确率由 55.5% 提升至 60.6%。其中 RTE 从 64.3% 提升至 82.3%，QNLI 从 52.3% 提升至 65.7%，BoolQ 从 70.8% 提升至 79.2%。对注入比例和顺序式多阶段融合的分析表明，激活引导提取能提升可迁移供体切片的质量，同时保持小比例融合机制。

Conclusion: 实验证据表明，当供体贡献足够“浓缩”时，无需显式语义对齐，跨尺度异构融合即可成功。该方法弥合了小受体与强供体之间的架构鸿沟，为异构模型融合提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [104] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模配对实验比较了基于间隔的不确定性采样与随机采样在干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）及有界难度依赖噪声下的表现，发现不确定性采样总体节省标注成本，但“对噪声稳健”这一结论取决于数据集、预算、噪声结构以及所用的性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过挑选信息量大的样本降低标注成本，但最不确定的样本往往也最难标注正确。已有观点认为不确定性采样会因获得更多错误标签而失效，但这一假设缺乏严格检验。作者希望区分两种可能的失败机制：一是不确定性采样采集了更多被污染的标签（标签损坏量的“暴露”效应），二是错误集中在难例区域（结构化误差位置）本身对学习尤其有害。

Method: 在三个公开二分类表格数据集上，将基于间隔的不确定性采样与随机采样进行对比，涉及三种标签场景：干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）和有界难度依赖噪声。实验设计采用100个配对随机种子、9种期望噪声率（0到0.30）、标注预算20至120，并在每个预算下用交叉验证重新选择逻辑回归的正则化参数。设置“暴露匹配”的RCN对照以对齐最终采集的污染样本均值，另设干净标签扩展实验将预算推至400。以归一化平衡准确率的学习曲线下面积为评价指标。

Result: 在干净标签下，不确定性采样在所有数据集上将归一化平衡准确率曲线下面积提升1.09至1.77个百分点。难度依赖噪声在乳腺肿瘤数据集（Breast Cancer Wisconsin）上于8个噪声率中的6个比RCN更削弱该优势，但在另外两个数据集的所有测试噪声率下均未表现出更强的削弱作用。暴露匹配分析未发现“结构化误差位置造成额外普遍惩罚”的修正后证据。在干净的MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样虽提升平衡准确率，却降低了平均精度以及固定假阳性率下的真阳性率。因此其“稳健性”是依赖数据集、预算、噪声结构和评价指标的。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样在干净标签下确实具有标签效率优势，但其看似稳健的表现并不能被归结为单一的通用机制：标签污染增加与难例区域误差集中所产生的危害均随数据、预算与噪声结构而变化。研究者应结合暴露匹配对照与多种指标来评估主动学习在含噪标注下的收益，避免得出过泛的结论。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [105] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于核风险敏感均值p次方准则的随机向量功能链接网络（KRPRVFL），通过用KRP损失替代最小二乘目标并结合协作学习机制，同时引入核诱导特征映射，在保持RVFL高效性的同时提升对噪声标签、离群点和不平衡数据的鲁棒性，在UCI和KEEL基准上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL网络训练高效、泛化能力强，但对噪声标签、离群点和类别不平衡数据敏感，限制了其在真实场景中的性能表现，因此需要一种兼具RVFL计算效率与强鲁棒性的改进模型。

Method: 1) 用核风险敏感均值p次方（KRP）准则损失替代标准最小二乘目标，自适应降低被污染或不可靠样本的影响力；2) 引入协作学习机制，使模型各组件自适应交互，增强复杂噪声环境下的鲁棒性；3) 借助核诱导特征映射捕捉非线性关系，无需显式隐层选择，兼顾效率与可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面一致优于基线模型，验证了其作为快速、可扩展且可靠的解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL成功将RVFL的计算效率与KRP准则的鲁棒性相结合，为面临噪声、离群点和数据不平衡挑战的分类任务提供了一种高效、可扩展且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [106] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA 是一种两阶段的事件级异常检测框架，先通过监督对比学习在多种物理过程上构建嵌入空间，再在该空间中运行标准自编码器生成异常分数。与基线自编码器相比，ORCA 在高亮度大型强子对撞机模拟数据上显著提升了新物理信号的灵敏度广度与深度，并通过最大似然模板拟合使异常样本可解释，能准确恢复注入信号产额并刻画模板库以外的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对对撞机物理中通用事件级异常检测的两个持续存在的问题：(1) 异常分数难以解释；(2) 异常分数与能量标度和事例多重数强相关，从而降低灵敏度并掩盖物理信息。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 在多种物理过程上利用监督对比学习训练嵌入空间，使已知过程在空间中占据不同区域且具有语义组织；(2) 在该嵌入空间中运行标准自编码器，以重构误差生成事件级异常分数；随后使用最大似然模板拟合嵌入分布，归属异常样本到模板物理过程并给出量化不确定性。

Result: 在与高亮度大型强子对撞机条件一致的模拟数据集上，ORCA 相比基线自编码器架构在灵敏度的广度（可探测的信号类型范围）与深度（对给定信号的敏感程度）上均取得显著提升；模板拟合能准确恢复注入的信号产额（包括在嵌入训练中未出现的信号），并能通过最相似的已知过程刻画模板库中不存在的信号。

Conclusion: ORCA 为对撞机上可解释的基于异常检测的搜索提供了可行路径：嵌入几何承载比标准一维输出拟合更高维的物理信息，使异常检测不仅能标记异常事件，还能揭示其物理来源，从而增强搜索的可信度与可解释性。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [107] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对线性注意力在长上下文下因状态混叠导致检索退化的问题，本文提出 Query 派生的擦除方向（QED）：在 Delta 规则模型的键派生擦除向量之外，额外加入一个与键正交、由查询派生的擦除方向，从而消除键方向编辑无法触及的读取干扰，使模型能利用可编辑部分去抵消沿查询方向测得的旧状态内容。实验表明 QED 在超过训练窗口的所有长度上都能改善检索，并在 S-NIAH-1 上使可用上下文长度约翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力维护固定大小的状态，在长上下文下大量存储项共享该状态，彼此之间的干扰会破坏检索精度。现有 Delta 规则模型（包括 GDN-2）的擦除向量都来自当前 token 的键，但读取时的干扰是通过查询来度量的，因此基于键的擦除步骤无法触及这部分干扰。这一不匹配构成了现有方法在长上下文检索上的根本局限。

Method: 提出 QED（Query-derived Erase Direction）：在键派生的原始擦除方向之外，增加第二个由查询派生且与键正交的擦除方向。在快速权重（fast-weight）视角下，键方向的 Delta 编辑无法改变读取中与键正交的分量；QED 正是利用可编辑的部分来消除状态中沿查询方向测得的旧内容，从而与读取度量方式对齐。

Result: 在超过训练窗口的每一条长度上，QED 都能改善检索性能；在 S-NIAH-1 基准上，它使可用上下文长度大约翻倍。

Conclusion: 将擦除方向从仅依赖键扩展为键与查询的联合正交方向，能够消除键方向编辑无法触及的查询侧干扰，显著提升线性注意力模型在超长度上的检索能力，并延长有效上下文范围。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [108] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了2018年10月至2026年7月间AI模型的进展：从BERT等简单系统演进为能解决复杂数学与编写软件的智能体，自2024年末起真实编码问题解决能力每年提升近六倍，同时推理成本大幅下降；性能顶端分散在擅长不同任务的专用模型上，并验证了高级采样与置信度排序工具在数学评测中带来的显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪并量化AI模型在编码、数学推理与成本效益方面的快速演进趋势，揭示顶级能力从单一通用模型向专业分工模型转移的格局，并评估采样策略与置信度排序工具对下游任务的实际提升作用。

Method: 采用纵向对比方法：统计2018至2026年多代模型在真实编码问题上的解决率变化；对比不同价位模型（如GPT-5.6 Luna）与旗舰模型的成本-性能关系；在多领域任务（前端编码、仓库级编码、终端任务）中评测专用模型；并在Qwen 2.5上的小学算术测试中比较基础方法、高级采样方法及置信度排序工具的效果。

Result: 真实编码问题解决能力自2024年末起每年提升近六倍；GPT-5.6 Luna以每百万token 1至6美元的价格达到旗舰级能力；Claude Opus 5、Claude Fable 5与GPT-5.6 Sol分别在各自任务领域领先；在100道小学算术题中，基础方法解答58题，高级采样提升至79题；置信度排序工具在前50个候选中正确识别47个正确答案。

Conclusion: AI能力呈指数级增长且成本急剧下降，性能已形成按任务专业化的多极化格局；高级采样与置信度排序工具能显著提升小模型在数学任务中的表现，验证了此类技术在任务排序与筛选场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [109] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-PRISM 是一种通用的事后可解释性方法，利用线性变换与反向传播规则，将任意 EEG 基础模型的时-通道归因分数映射到生理相关的频域与源（空间）域，无需修改或重训底层模型。实验表明该方法在模拟数据上实现近完美的频谱恢复与 69.2% 的空间精度，并在癫痫与自闭症数据上正确定位事件与临床生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 基础模型是人工智能用于脑电分析的下一代进展，但现有可解释 AI 技术仅在时-通道输入空间给出归因分数，与临床医生对 EEG 的直觉（频率与脑区/源）不匹配，因此亟需一种通用方法，可将任意基础模型的可解释性扩展到替代且生理相关的领域。

Method: EEG-PRISM 借助线性变换与既有的反向传播规则，将时-通道归因分数映射到替代领域：通过可逆 DFT 映射到频域，通过近似可逆的 EEG 生成模型映射到源域。评估使用了五个基础模型与四种 AI 解释器，在模拟数据和真实数据上检验跨域恢复真实现象的能力。

Result: 模拟实验中，EEG-PRISM 实现近完美的频谱恢复与 69.2% 的空间准确率。在癫痫数据中，正确判定 delta-theta 活动最为显著并以 50% 的准确率定位发作起始区；在自闭症数据中，将具有预测意义的 delta-alpha 生物标志物定位到额叶和颞叶区域，与既往研究一致。

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM 是一种理论扎实的事后归因方法，能够准确映射到频谱与空间域，支持对瞬态事件（如癫痫发作）的窗口级分析以及临床相关生物标志物（如自闭症）的群体级识别，从而推进可解释的 EEG 基础模型，并为临床提供事件定位等有意义的信息。

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [110] [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702)
*Kiran Nair,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一种针对Transformer类脉冲神经网络（SNN）的不确定性调制代理梯度机制。它利用归一化自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，在训练期间动态调整代理梯度的斜率，推理模型保持不变，从而在不增加部署成本的前提下提升训练优化灵活性。在CIFAR-10/100上相对于固定代理梯度基线取得了1-2%的稳定精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN虽然通过稀疏事件驱动计算提供高能效，但其训练挑战较大，因为不可微的脉冲函数需要代理梯度，而固定形状的代理梯度在各层和各训练阶段可能并非最优，限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出SAGE机制：从归一化自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，利用该信号在训练期间自适应调整代理梯度的斜率，仅改变训练时的代理参数，推理时模型架构和部署成本均保持不变。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100数据集上，SAGE相比固定代理梯度基线取得了更高的精度，在多个仿真时间步上实现了一致的1-2%精度提升。

Conclusion: 注意力派生的不确定性可作为轻量级训练信号，用于Transformer类SNN的自适应代理梯度学习，在不改变推理成本的前提下提升训练效果，具有广阔潜力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient alternative to conventional deep neural networks by exploiting sparse event-driven computation, but their training remains challenging because the non-differentiable spike function requires surrogate gradients whose fixed shape may be suboptimal across layers and training stages. In this work, we introduce SAGE, an uncertainty-modulated surrogate-gradient mechanism for Transformer-based SNNs. SAGE estimates block-level uncertainty from normalized self-attention entropy and uses this signal to adapt the surrogate-gradient slope during training while leaving the inference model unchanged. By modulating only the training-time surrogate parameter, the proposed method preserves the original architecture and deployment cost while improving optimization flexibility. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 demonstrate that SAGE achieves improved accuracy over fixed-surrogate baselines, with results up to 1-2\% consistent gains across multiple simulation time steps. These results highlight the potential of attention-derived uncertainty as a lightweight training signal for adaptive surrogate-gradient learning in transformer-based SNNs.

</details>


### [111] [Sequence prediction under a lying oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14102)
*Puspabeethi Samanta,Nikhil Karamchandani,Jayakrishnan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究m元序列的在线序贯预测问题：每轮环境中生成一个符号，学习者在不知情的情况下输出一个概率分布，并根据实际符号对应的概率承担代价。该代价函数刻画了借助“说谎预言机”的成对比较查询进行预测的复杂度。作者针对随机环境和对抗环境分别提出算法，并证明二者均达到对数级别的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测框架中的损失函数通常直接度量预测误差的量化损失，而现实场景中（如通过查询从不可靠/说谎的预言机获取比较信息）预测的代价可能来自查询过程的复杂度。因此需要一种新的代价模型，将预测复杂度与成对比较查询到说谎预言机的场景联系起来，并在随机与对抗两类环境下给出可证明的算法。

Method: 形式化地建立“m元符号序贯预测 + 说谎预言机比较查询”的损失模型；将环境划分为随机（i.i.d.）与对抗两种设定；针对每种设定分别设计预测算法（据摘要“propose algorithms for both settings”），并通过分析算法在两类环境中的累积遗憾，证明其上界。

Result: 在随机环境与对抗环境下，所提出的算法均获得了关于累积遗憾的O(log)（对数）型上界，表明总预测代价随轮次增长仅呈对数增长。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使在需要通过比较查询一个不可靠（说谎）预言机来预测并承担查询复杂度代价的困难设置下，随机与对抗环境中的预测问题仍可被高效求解，遗憾至多为对数级。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sequential prediction of an $m$-ary sequence, where at each epoch, (i) the environment selects an outcome from an $m$-ary alphabet, (ii) the learner selects a probability distribution over the same alphabet (unaware of the outcome generated by the environment), and finally, (iii) the learner incurs a cost that depends on the probability assigned to the outcome. The cost function we consider captures the complexity of predicting the outcome generated by the environment, in a scenario where the aforementioned prediction is performed via comparative queries to a lying oracle. We consider both stochastic and adversarial environments, propose algorithms for both settings, and establish logarithmic upper bounds on their regret.

</details>


### [112] [Capacity-Dependent Effects of Data Selection for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13721)
*Cuong Dang,Hoang Anh Just,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了推理任务监督微调中"高似然数据更有效"的直觉，发现似然驱动的数据选择效果取决于模型容量和训练时长：高似然数据带来快速、稳定的早期提升（尤其对小模型），而低似然数据在长时间训练下对大模型更有利，呈现容量依赖的"Fast-Fit / Slow-Gain"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于似然的数据选择方法认为，与学生当前分布更接近的响应能提供更有效的监督，暗示高似然响应普遍更优。作者希望检验这一直觉是否在所有场景都成立，并揭示其适用范围。

Method: 在数学推理任务上进行受控实验，采用1.5B至8B参数的学生模型，由更强教师模型生成监督数据；结合学习动力学分析与容量约束下的蒸馏理论视角，研究数据难度、数据跨度与学生容量如何联合决定知识迁移。

Result: 观察到清晰的容量依赖型"Fast-Fit / Slow-Gain"模式：高似然数据对小模型带来更快更稳定的早期提升；但随着训练时间延长，低似然数据对大模型越来越有益。小模型难以吸收低似然监督，易陷入浅层或重复行为；大模型则能更好地向教师分布移动。

Conclusion: 推理任务的数据选择不应基于单一的高似然偏好，而应同时考虑模型容量与计算预算；数据难度、数据跨度与学生容量共同决定蒸馏迁移效果。

Abstract: In reasoning supervised fine-tuning, candidate responses for the same instruction can differ substantially in how well they match the student's current distribution. Recent likelihood-based response selection methods suggest that responses closer to the student distribution provide more effective supervision, motivating the hypothesis that high-likelihood responses may generally be preferable for fine-tuning. In this paper, we revisit this intuition and show that the value of likelihood-based data selection depends critically on model capacity and training duration. Through controlled experiments on mathematical reasoning, using students ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters and supervision generated by stronger teacher models, we observe a clear \emph{capacity-dependent} ``{\color{SMALLCOLOR}\textbf{Fast-Fit}} / {\color{LARGECOLOR}\textbf{Slow-Gain}}'' pattern. High-likelihood data provides faster and more stable early improvements, especially for smaller models, but low-likelihood data becomes increasingly beneficial for larger models when training is allowed to continue longer. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze learning dynamics, showing that small models often fail to absorb low-likelihood supervision and instead fall into shallow or repetitive behaviors, while larger models are better able to move toward the teacher distribution under such data. We further provide a capacity-constrained theoretical view of distillation that clarifies how data difficulty, data span, and student capacity jointly govern transfer. Overall, our findings show that effective data selection for reasoning should be aware of model capacity and computing budget rather than based on a single universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.

</details>


### [113] [The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13756)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战“实现同一 scaled INT8 GEMM 接口的 GPU 内核可互换”这一假设。作者在 vLLM 中仅替换 INT8 线性内核（CUTLASS 与 Triton），固定 checkpoint、提示词、硬件、推理引擎、解码与量化配置，发现每个分支自身跨冷启动可逐位复现，但两分支在所有端到端对比中（0/8、0/16、0/64 序列）没有任何序列一致。借助“整数 alibi”（INT32 点积在无溢出界下精确且与顺序无关，累加器不可能是差异来源），作者证明两内核在真实权重尺度下逐层输入恒定时输出最多相差一个 bfloat16 间距，从而把分歧定位到精确累加器之后的尺度缩放与输出舍入；将该干预作为探针 checkpoint 可恢复端到端逐位一致（8/8、16/16）。FP8 GEMM 呈现不同特征：差异的普遍性与幅度随归约深度增长，而 INT8 的差异率在 K 变化 64 倍范围内维持在百万分之一量级且不超过一个间距。教师强制重放将层与 token 关联，翻转集中在 logit 边际小的位置，ROC-AUC 达 0.94（16,384 个位置）。作者将发布预注册、逐层预测、含内核选择证据的清单以及一致性检查流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个实现同一 scaled INT8 GEMM 接口的 GPU 内核（CUTLASS 与 Triton）通常被视为可互换，这一假设未被严格检验。作者旨在测试：在完全固定 checkpoint、提示词、硬件、引擎、解码与量化配置的前提下，仅交换 INT8 线性内核是否会导致端到端输出的分歧，并试图解释这种分歧的根源，进而将其从“基准差异”提升为可定位、可预测、可复现的科学发现，同时提供可操作的一致性核验方法。

Method: 在 vLLM 中仅替换 INT8 线性内核（CUTLASS 对 Triton）进行对照实验；对 Qwen3-1.7B 与 8B 的全部线性层（196 层与 252 层）向两个内核喂入相同操作数，比较输出。利用“整数 alibi”：在验证过的无溢出界下 INT32 点积精确且与顺序无关，从而排除累加器差异，将分歧定位到尺度缩放与舍入。通过改变 scale 应用方式（作为探针 checkpoint）验证端到端逐位一致性；并对比 FP8 GEMM 作为不同特征基线。最后用教师强制重放把层与 token 关联，分析 logit 边际预测翻转风险，并使用 ROC-AUC 量化。

Result: 每个分支自身跨冷重启逐位复现，但两分支在端到端对比中无任何序列一致（0/8、0/16、0/64）。在 power-of-two 尺度下，两内核在全部 196/196 与 252/252 层输出逐位一致（1.7B 为预注册，8B 为固定但非盲）；在真实尺度下差异最多一个 bfloat16 间距。通过探针干预可恢复端到端逐位一致（8/8、16/16）。FP8 GEMM 的差异随归约深度增长，而 INT8 差异率在 K 变化 64 倍内保持约百万分之一且不超过一个间距。翻转集中在 logit 边际小处，预测翻转风险的 ROC-AUC 为 0.94（16,384 个位置）。

Conclusion: 即使实现同一接口的内核，在输出舍入与尺度应用上的微小差异也足以在端到端推理中产生完全的分歧（序列级零一致），尽管累加器在数学上是精确且顺序无关的。作者将分歧精确定位到精确 INT32 累加器之后的 scale 应用与 bfloat16 输出舍入，并证明该差异可被探针干预消除、可被 logit 边际预测（AUC 0.94）。这表明内核选择并非可忽略的实现细节，需要统一的一致性核验程序；论文将以预注册、逐层预测、含内核选择证据的清单及一致性检查流程的形式发布这些控制手段。

Abstract: Two GPU kernels implementing the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface are usually treated as interchangeable. We test that assumption: holding the checkpoint, prompts, hardware, inference engine, decoding, and quantization configuration fixed, we swap only the INT8 linear kernel (CUTLASS versus Triton) inside vLLM. At 1.7B each arm reproduces itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts, yet the arms agree on no sequence in any end-to-end comparison we ran (0/8, 0/16, and 0/64). What makes this more than a benchmark discrepancy is an integer alibi: for shared INT8 operands under a verified no-overflow bound, the INT32 dot product is exact and order-independent, so the accumulator cannot be the source of any difference. Feeding both kernels identical operands from every linear layer of Qwen3-1.7B and 8B (196 and 252 layers), we find bit-identical outputs under power-of-two scales, confirming a pinned prediction list 196/196 and 252/252 (pre-registered at 1.7B, pinned but not blind at 8B), and observed differences of at most one bfloat16 spacing under the checkpoints' real scales. This localizes the divergence to scale application and output rounding after the exact accumulator. Applied as a probe checkpoint, the same intervention restores end-to-end bitwise agreement (8/8 and 16/16 sequences). Cross-implementation FP8 GEMM shows a different signature: both the prevalence and the magnitude of differences grow with reduction depth, while the INT8 fraction stays at parts per million and within one spacing over a 64x range of K. Teacher-forced replay ties layers to tokens: flips concentrate at small logit margins, which predict flip risk with ROC-AUC 0.94 on 16,384 positions. We will release the pre-registration, per-layer predictions, manifests with kernel-selection evidence, and a conformance procedure that turns these controls into a concrete check for kernel interchangeability.

</details>


### [114] [CutClean: Neural Network Pruning for Privacy-Preserving Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13773)
*Leonardo Magliolo,Vito Paolo Pastore,Giuseppe Valenzise,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CutClean，一种隐私感知的剪枝方法，通过在网络的每个块上放置辅助线性隐私头来量化隐私信息泄漏，并施加递增的稀疏化以移除敏感属性泄漏，同时提高网络的稀疏性，在合成和真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络被越来越多地部署在高风险应用中，隐私泄漏问题日益突出。现有研究通常将隐私泄漏归因于数据集或表示层面的不平衡（即传统数据集偏差），但本文表明，即使不存在此类表示不平衡，隐私泄漏依然可能发生，这对处理敏感属性的模型部署构成重大隐私风险，因此亟需不依赖数据集偏差假设的隐私防护手段。

Method: CutClean 是一种隐私感知的剪枝方法。具体而言，它在网络的每个块（block）处附加辅助线性隐私头，用以量化各层的信息泄漏程度；随后施加递增的稀疏化级别，以最后一块所接隐私头精度作为隐私泄漏的度量，逐步移除与私有属性相关的信息流，在降低隐私泄漏的同时提高模型稀疏性。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，CutClean 能够有效最小化私有信息流动，同时实现较高的稀疏率并保持分类性能。

Conclusion: CutClean 证明了在不依赖传统数据集偏差的隐私泄漏场景下，通过隐私感知剪枝能够同时兼顾隐私保护（降低敏感属性泄漏）和模型效率（高稀疏率），为高风险应用中的隐私安全模型部署提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications with growing privacy leakage concerns. We show that this privacy leakage can occur even in the absence of representation imbalances that lead to traditional dataset biases. This poses significant privacy risks when deploying models that process sensitive attributes. In this context, we propose CutClean, a privacy-aware pruning method that allows to reduce privacy information flow through the network, while increasing its sparsity. Our approach employs auxiliary linear privacy heads placed at each network's block to quantify information leakage, and further applies increasing levels of sparsity to remove the private attribute leakage, measured in terms of the accuracy of the privacy head attached to the last block. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively minimizes private information flow while achieving high sparsity rates and preserving classification target accuracy.

</details>


### [115] [PPAPlace: Differentiable Cross-Stage Objectives for Chip Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13790)
*Ruogu Chen,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PPAPlace，一个面向后布线性能/功耗/面积（PPA）的时序驱动可微代理模型，用于芯片宏单元布局。它由双流预测器（图注意力+空间卷积）构成，训练标签来自全局布线后的时序指标，并将预测的 WNS/TNS 梯度端到端回传到单元坐标，既可作为解析布局器的共目标（CoOpt），也可作为宏单元位置后置优化（Refine）。在 5 个未见测试电路上，平均 WNS 和 TNS 相对层级基线分别改善 22% 和 51%，同时保持功耗与可布线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统布局方法以半周长线长（HPWL）为主要优化目标，但近年基准测试显示 HPWL 与布线后时序指标（WNS/TNS）相关性近乎为零，导致六种 AI 布局器相比层级基线的 PPA 反而退化。现有跨阶段预测器仅用宏单元表示、并以布线前指标为训练标签，保真度不足。作者通过对十个电路在四个设计流程阶段的标签保真度研究，发现全局布线后阶段在时序保真度与标签生成成本间取得最佳平衡，从而需要一种能直接预测并优化布线后 PPA 的方法。

Method: PPAPlace 包含一个双流时序驱动代理模型：一条流对芯片网表做图注意力，另一条流对布局网格做空间卷积，联合预测布线后 WNS/TNS。模型使用全局布线后标签训练，预测梯度端到端回传至单元坐标。梯度以两种方式利用：一是作为共目标注入解析布局器的优化循环（PPAPlace-CoOpt）；二是作为后置精修步骤，通过投影梯度下降调整宏单元位置（PPAPlace-Refine）。评估中采用同一代理模型且不对测试电路重训。

Result: 在五个从训练中排除的 ChiPBench 测试电路上，PPAPlace 相比层级基线平均改善 WNS 22%、TNS 51%，同时保持功耗和可布线性。研究还给出标签保真度分析：HPWL 与布线前时序对最终布线后时序排名的反映较差，而全局布线后标签最合适。

Conclusion: 布线后时序指标（WNS/TNS）可作为比 HPWL/布线前指标更可靠的布局优化目标；以全局布线后标签训练的跨阶段可微代理模型能够端到端改进布局 PPA。PPAPlace 无需对测试电路重训即可推广，表明时序驱动可微代理是缩小 AI 布局器与层级基线差距的有效途径。

Abstract: Macro placement significantly affects a chip's post-route performance, power, and area (PPA). Most placement methods optimize half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) as the primary objective. However, recent benchmarking shows a near-zero correlation between HPWL and post-route timing metrics such as the worst negative slack (WNS) and total negative slack (TNS). As a result, all six evaluated artificial intelligence (AI) placers degraded PPA relative to the hierarchical baseline. Recent efforts have tried to train cross-stage predictors to close this gap. However, existing methods focus on macro-only representations and use pre-route metrics as training labels. A label fidelity study of ten circuits at four design flow stages reveals that HPWL and pre-route timing poorly reflect final post-route timing rankings. In contrast, post-global-routing achieves the best balance between final timing fidelity and label generation cost-effectiveness. Based on this finding, PPAPlace is a timing-driven differentiable surrogate predicting post-route PPA from macro and standard-cell placements. The surrogate is a dual-stream predictor that combines graph attention over the chip netlist with spatial convolution over the placement grid. It is trained on post-global-routing labels. The predicted WNS and TNS gradients flow end-to-end back to cell coordinates. PPAPlace exploits these gradients in two ways: as a co-objective injected into an analytical placer's optimization loop (PPAPlace-CoOpt), and as a post-placement refinement step that adjusts macro positions via projected gradient descent (PPAPlace-Refine). On five ChiPBench test circuits excluded from training, PPAPlace improves average WNS and TNS by 22\% and 51\% over the hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability, using the same predictor without test-circuit retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/ValleyC/PPAPlace.

</details>


### [116] [Recent Advances in Deep Learning-Based Drug-Target Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13797)
*Jafin Khan,Md Hossain Shuvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对近年来基于机器学习的药物-靶点结合亲和力预测方法进行了全面综述与比较分析，覆盖代表性深度学习架构、七种常用基准数据集及评价指标，指出了现有方法在数据集偏差、评估设置有限、冷启动场景下泛化不足等问题，并提出了数据集设计、评估标准化、冷启动处理及多模态融合等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-靶点结合亲和力预测是药物发现计算流程中的关键子问题，尽管近年来深度学习方法取得进展，准确预测仍是一个开放研究领域；现有方法在标准基准上表现良好，但其有效性常受数据集偏差和有限评估设置影响，缺乏系统性的优劣势与研究空白梳理。

Method: 采用综述与比较分析的方法：回顾采用常见基准数据集和评价指标的代表性深度学习方法，涵盖多种神经网络架构和表征策略；系统分析七个广泛使用的基准数据集和常用的评价指标；从数据集偏差、冷启动场景、评估设置等维度比较各方法表现并归纳其局限。

Result: 分析表明，许多方法在标准基准上报告强性能，但其有效性常受数据集偏差和有限评估设置的影响；大多数方法在冷启动场景下性能下降，凸显泛化挑战。识别出的局限包括数据集不平衡、缺乏标准化评估、真实世界适用性有限以及冷启动难题。

Conclusion: 本文指出了当前药物-靶点结合亲和力预测研究的关键空白，并建议未来研究应关注更好的数据集设计、更稳健的评估方法、改进冷启动问题处理以及多模态表征的整合，以推动该领域的实际应用。

Abstract: Computational approaches to drug discovery involve multiple sub-problems, and among them, drug-target binding affinity prediction plays an important role. Despite recent advances, accurately predicting binding affinity remains an open research area. The major objective of our paper is to perform a comprehensive review and comparative analysis of recent machine learning methods for drug-target binding affinity prediction, with a focus on identifying strengths, limitations, and research gaps. We review representative recent deep learning approaches that use common benchmark datasets and evaluation metrics, covering a range of neural network architectures and representation strategies. In addition, we analyze seven widely used benchmark datasets and commonly adopted evaluation metrics for drug-target binding affinity prediction. Our analysis indicates that although many methods report strong performance on standard benchmarks, their effectiveness is often influenced by dataset bias and limited evaluation settings. Furthermore, most methods exhibit reduced performance in cold-start scenarios, highlighting challenges in generalization. We identify several limitations of current approaches, including dataset imbalance, the lack of standardized evaluation, limited real-world applicability, and challenges in cold-start scenarios. We also discuss future research directions, including better dataset design, more robust evaluation methods, improved handling of cold-start problems, and the integration of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing with Online Requests: Event-Driven Transformer--DRL and Rolling-Horizon Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13799)
*Faezeh Ardali,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向动态多车场车辆路径问题（渐进式请求揭示、车辆状态动态演化）的事件驱动学习与基准测试框架。训练了带掩码的 MLP 与 Transformer 策略（行为克隆 + PPO），并用确定性可行性掩码避免非法指派、用固定前缀/灵活后缀承诺保护已完成的、进行中的和近期决策。实验表明：最近可行启发式在综合目标上最优，学习策略具备毫秒级决策且可零重训迁移到 80 请求实例，但未超越最强启发式；没有任何单一方法在所有指标上全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态多车场车辆路径问题存在请求按序揭示、车辆状态实时演化、调度需在线决策等特点，传统优化方法（如滚动时域优化）计算代价高，启发式方法简单但可能缺乏适应性；而学习方法的可行性与稳定性、以及与经典方法的系统对比尚缺乏统一框架。作者旨在构建一个公平、统一的事件驱动学习与基准测试协议，量化学习策略与启发式、滚动时域优化在多维指标（路由质量、响应性、稳定性、计算开销）上的差异。

Method: 采用事件驱动框架：为动态多车场 VRP 设计统一协议，训练 Masked MLP 和 Transformer 两种策略，训练算法为行为克隆与近端策略优化（PPO）。通过确定性可行性掩码保证不产生非法车辆-请求指派；采用固定前缀/灵活后缀的路径承诺机制分别度量车辆重指派与重排序行为。对照方法为动态插入启发式与限时滚动时域优化，并在 20 场景策略基准、5 次独立训练运行及泛化到 80 请求实例上做系统评估。

Result: 在 20 场景基准中所有方法均完成全部请求且无非法动作，但最近可行启发式取得最低平均目标，并在路由质量、等待时间、稳定性、完工时间与运行时间上均优于学习策略。五次独立训练中，PPO 对 MLP 平均影响很小，对 Transformer 平均有改进但种子方差更大。统一协议下，最近可行获得最低的组合目标与路径扰动，滚动时域获得最低等待时间与完工时间但计算成本显著更高。学习策略保持毫秒级决策，可无需重训迁移至最多 80 请求实例，但未超越最强启发式。

Conclusion: 在路由效率、服务响应性、稳定性与在线计算开销之间不存在单一最优方法：学习策略的优势在于极低推理延迟与泛化能力，启发式在质量与稳定性上占优，滚动时域在响应性（等待、完工时间）上占优但计算昂贵。该框架为动态多车场 VRP 提供了可复现的公平比较基准，并指出学习策略在路由质量上仍有待提升。

Abstract: This paper presents an event-driven learning and benchmarking framework for the Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problem with progressively revealed requests and evolving vehicle states. Masked MLP and Transformer policies are trained through behavior cloning and proximal policy optimization. Deterministic feasibility masking prevents invalid vehicle--request assignments, while fixed-prefix/flexible-suffix route commitments protect completed, active, and near-term decisions and separately measure vehicle reassignment and resequencing. The learned policies are compared with dynamic insertion heuristics and time-limited rolling-horizon optimization. In a 20-scenario policy benchmark, all methods completed every request without invalid actions, but nearest feasible achieved the lowest mean objective and outperformed the learned policies in routing quality, waiting time, stability, makespan, and runtime. Across five independent training runs, PPO had little average effect on the MLP and improved the Transformer on average, although with greater seed variability. Under the common protocol, nearest feasible achieved the lowest combined objective and route disruption, whereas rolling horizon achieved the lowest waiting times and makespan at substantially higher computational cost. The learned policies retained millisecond-level decisions and transferred to instances with up to 80 requests without retraining, but did not outperform the strongest heuristic. No single method was best across routing efficiency, service responsiveness, stability, and online computation.

</details>


### [118] [Stochastic Control Policies for Robust Molecular Transition Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13800)
*Jingqian Liu,Yu-Hsiang Wang,Yanru Qu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于展开式轨迹（rollout）的过渡路径采样（TPS）控制方法存在的不稳定和强种子依赖问题，提出将这类方法重新理解为学习路径空间上的提议分布，并通过在控制策略中引入随机性（随机策略）来提升探索能力与优化鲁棒性。作者设计了两种随机策略：FS-TPS（直接参数化状态依赖高斯分布）与LaS-TPS（采样紧凑隐变量并解码为跨原子相关力变化），在丙氨酸二肽、chignolin和快速折叠蛋白BBL三个系统上的多种子实验表明，随机策略一致提升了过渡成功率和路径质量，并显著降低了对随机初始化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡路径采样（TPS）对理解生物分子机制至关重要，机器学习已成为前沿TPS的核心手段。基于展开式轨迹的控制方法通过保留底层分子动力学，能产生比端点条件生成器更物理合理的路径，但存在训练不稳定、性能对随机种子高度敏感的问题。作者希望改善这类方法的探索能力和优化鲁棒性。

Method: 作者将基于展开式轨迹的控制重构为学习路径空间上的提议分布，并将随机性的放置位置作为设计选择。具体提出两种随机策略：FS-TPS直接对控制策略输出参数化一个状态依赖的高斯分布；LaS-TPS则采样一个紧凑的隐潜变量，再将其解码为具有跨原子相关结构的力扰动。两种方法均在保持分子动力学展开形式的基础上引入随机探索。
