TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家的过去与未来——即能够自动化科学研究的机器。这类系统能提出假设、推导后果、设计并执行实验、解释结果并修正信念。文章回顾了最早做出新颖科学发现的机器Adam，以及建立现代自驱动实验室架构的Eve，指出当前的核心问题已不再是单个组件的自动化，而是集成：AI科学家必须将神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人和正式科学记录相结合，并有望变革科学，使其更快、更便宜、更系统、更可复现。文章还提到Nobel Turing Challenge（2050年前实现自动化诺贝尔级发现）的目标，并认为进展已超前于计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学的各个组件（假设生成、实验执行、数据分析等）已可分别自动化，真正阻碍在于将这些组件整合为统一的科学智能体。作者希望通过综述展示AI科学家从早期系统（Adam、Eve）到基于基础模型与机器人技术的现代系统的演进，强调集成是当前和未来的核心挑战，并指出其变革科学、实现大规模协作科研乃至自动化诺贝尔级发现的巨大潜力。

Method: 本文为一篇综述性文章，采用历史脉络梳理与前瞻分析相结合的方法：先回顾Adam（通过假设形成与物理实验循环做出新颖发现的首台机器）和Eve（确立现代自驱动实验室架构）的里程碑式贡献，再论述基础模型、自主智能体与实验室机器人如何使远超Adam和Eve的通用系统成为可能，最后讨论实现这类集成系统所需的能力组合（神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人、正式科学记录）。

Result: 文章指出：科学自动化已从"单个组件能否自动化"转向"如何集成"的问题；AI科学家有望使科学更快、更便宜、更系统、更可复现，并能研究过于复杂而不适合人类单独开展的系统，还可实现数千个AI科学家协作解决单个问题。此外，Nobel Turing Challenge设定的2050年实现自动化诺贝尔级发现的目标，当前进展已超前于计划。

Conclusion: 作者认为，构建通用AI科学家的关键瓶颈在于集成而非个别组件。当这一目标实现时，将创造一种新的科学形态并彻底改变世界；且按照当前进展判断，该目标有望在2050年前提前或如期达成。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [249] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 optstop，一种基于精度（precision）的自适应停止框架，将 LLM 评估视为顺序测量问题：在不确定性高的地方继续采样，在估计足够精确或稳定时停止，从而用不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配评估计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 评估通常使用固定的采样预算，即使在估计已足够精确时仍对每个测试项进行相同次数的测试，造成大量不必要的计算开销。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推断构建框架：支持二元、有序和连续结果；保持所有基准项可被采样，无需经过校准的项目库；可在线运行也可事后（回顾性）运行；并包含一个安全机制——当测量性能趋近于零（稀有成功至关重要）时更谨慎地采样。

Result: 在一个 200 项、10 轮（epoch）的示例评估中，optstop 在九种验证设置下移除了 57%-97% 的计划试验，同时总体结论与完整运行结果一致。

Conclusion: LLM 评估中的计算资源可以按不确定性而非固定重复计数来分配，节省的幅度取决于评估设计的具体设置。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [250] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI参与自身研发所构成的反馈回路是否可能引发"智能爆炸"，并通过数学建模分析最剧烈增长情形的动力学条件。核心结论是：向垂直渐近线趋近的奇异增长比近期经济学启发式模型所暗示的更难实现，且存在一类被忽视的"超指数但无垂直渐近线"的增长速率；此外，"代际时间"（完成一次反馈回路所需的时间）是被忽视的关键参数，只有当代际时间迅速趋近于零时，奇异增长才可能发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正越来越多地被用于辅助AI本身的研发，这种自我改进的反馈回路在特定条件下可能产生智能爆炸（能力快速升级）。作者希望透过数学分析理解这类爆炸性过程的内在驱动因素，澄清现有经济学启发式建模在预测爆炸可能性上的局限，并识别被忽略的动力学参数。

Method: 采用数学建模与动力学分析方法，考察智能爆炸反馈回路中最剧烈的（"最具爆炸性"的）增长可能性；对增长率的分类进行严格数学刻画，比较奇异增长（垂直渐近线）与超指数增长的区别；引入"代际时间"这一参数，分析其取值与变化对整体系统行为（是否趋向奇异增长）的影响。

Result: 发现：1）奇异增长（指向垂直渐近线）的实现条件比近期经济学启发式模型预测的更为苛刻；2）存在一类重要的、此前被忽视的增长速率——快于指数但不会导致垂直渐近线；3）代际时间是决定智能爆炸行为的关键被忽略参数，奇异性增长要求代际时间快速趋近于零。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸能否以最剧烈的奇异形式发生，取决于代际时间能否快速缩短这一通常未受重视的条件；即便达不到奇异增长，仍可能出现超指数但不发散的增长形态。这为理解AI自我改进的极限与风险提供了更精细的数学框架，也提示应注意反馈回路周期在爆炸动力学中的核心地位。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [251] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE-Bench是一个基于物理仿真器的基准测试，包含144个源到目标环境适配任务，横跨六个物理领域，用于评估自进化智能体在环境条件变化后的恢复能力。实验表明该基准远未饱和：最优方法（Reflexion+Qwen3-14B）仅解决35.9%的任务，即使提供完全物理变化信息也未突破性能上限，说明核心瓶颈在于机制重设计而非参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体的评估通常在固定执行条件下进行，不测试环境条件变化后的恢复能力，这与真实场景中环境会突变的情况不符，因此需要一个能模拟环境突变并要求智能体迭代适应的评测基准。

Method: 构建PACE-Bench：144对源-目标适配任务，跨六个物理领域，每对由源环境与突变后的目标环境构成，目标与接口保持不变。设计上保证在源环境成功的代码在目标环境必然失败，智能体需在有限的尝试预算内利用诊断沙箱反馈迭代改进。比较十个来自四种范式的自进化方法，并分析不同预算上限和是否揭示物理变化条件下的表现。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion+Qwen3-14B在完整基准上仅成功35.9%；GPT-5.5在全部预算下可解决Statics子集的66.7%。实验显示基于仿真器反馈的反思比未经验证的自我修订更可靠；记忆机制会把智能体锚定在早期设计上；宽泛的树搜索容易探索但不收敛。即便揭示精确的物理变化，性能上限也未被提高。

Conclusion: 研究指出机制重设计而非参数推断是当前自进化智能体的核心瓶颈；PACE-Bench为未来研究提供了可复现的评测平台，强调在非固定条件下评估智能体恢复能力的重要性。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [252] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Wyvern，一个多智能体框架，用于自动生成有据可依（grounded）的多模态技术报告，整合图像、表格、文本与引用，并通过声明自动修订阶段提升内容可追溯性。人类评估显示其图表信息量在87%情况下优于基线，报告有用性在63%-100%的案例中优于三种对比方法；自动评估中引用召回率最高提升2.3倍、引用精确率最高提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动创新的时代，知识增长迅速，人们难以跟上；尽管生成模型被广泛用于内容合成，但往往缺乏信息依据（grounding）。作者旨在解决生成内容缺乏可追溯、可验证来源的问题，并满足技术报告需融合多模态信息的需求。

Method: Wyvern 采用多智能体框架，能够自动生成包含图像、表格和文本的统一技术报告，并为内容附上支持性参考文献；框架特别强调内容依据，实现了一个声明自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段，用于核查和修正报告中的论断以增强其可追溯性。

Result: 人类评估显示，Wyvern 生成的图表信息量在87%的案例中被认为优于近期基线；其报告在63%至100%的实例中被评为比三种替代方法更有用。自动评估显示，相较基线，Wyvern 的引用召回率最高提升2.3倍，引用精确率最高提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern 在生成有据可依的多模态技术报告方面表现优越，兼顾内容丰富度（多模态）与内容可靠性（引用依据），其方法与评估结果为自动化科学文献综述和知识综合提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [253] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SheetCompass，一种面向电子表格推理与自动化的图引导、记忆驱动的智能体框架，通过显式建模工作簿内及跨工作表的结构关系，并维护任务相关信息，使大语言模型能够像人类专家一样利用全局空间上下文进行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格广泛用于组织、分析和操作半结构化数据，但大语言模型（LLM）对其自动化推理仍具挑战。真实工作簿常包含隐式的跨表关联、细粒度的列依赖和复杂的空间布局；现有方法往往将多维结构展平为顺序字符串，丢失了表内边界和表间语义，导致 LLM 无法利用人类专家自然依赖的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass 采用图引导且记忆驱动的智能体框架：显式建模工作表内部及工作表之间的结构关系（如图结构表示），同时在记忆中维护与任务相关的信息，使智能体能够在复杂工作簿上更有效地进行推理与自动化操作。

Result: 通过显式保留电子表格的多维结构语义并借助图引导与记忆机制，SheetCompass 在复杂工作簿上的推理与自动化任务中相较展平序列策略的现有方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 图引导与记忆驱动的结构建模能够弥补 LLM 在电子表格推理中缺失的空间语义，是提升复杂工作簿自动化推理能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [254] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“移位感知双编码器迁移框架”（shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework），用于短时程PM2.5预报。该框架融合源域（美国10个监测站）预训练知识与目标域（台湾77个站点）专用表示学习。结果表明：在冻结源编码器时，双编码器模型在四个基线中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725），较TL-v1和TL-v2的MSE分别降低约7.1%和4.1%；允许源编码器自适应的配置取得最优结果（MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739）。消融实验显示移除台湾专用分支导致性能下降最大，SHAP分析表明预测主要由近期PM2.5观测及与污染物输送/扩散相关的气象变量驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标域观测样本有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异的情况下，PM2.5短时程预报十分困难：仅用局部数据训练的模型难以捕捉复杂的时序动态，而直接迁移学习则可能产生负迁移。因此需要一种既能利用源域知识又能保留目标域特有信息的迁移框架来应对域间分布偏移。

Method: 构建移位感知双编码器迁移框架：源编码器使用美国10个监测站的逐小时观测进行预训练，随后在台湾77个站点两年的逐小时观测数据上，采用按时间顺序划分的训练-验证-测试协议进行适配与评估。模型包含源域知识分支与目标域专用表示分支，并考察冻结源编码器与允许源编码器自适应两种设置；通过消融实验（移除台湾专用分支等）及SHAP可解释性分析进行验证。

Result: 在四个主要基线中，冻结源的dual-encoder模型表现最佳（MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725），MSE较TL-v1降低约7.1%、较TL-v2降低约4.1%。消融分析显示移除台湾专用分支造成最大性能下降；允许源编码器自适应取得总体最优结果（MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析显示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测以及涉及污染物输送与扩散的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特有信息并允许迁移表示在监督下自适应时最为有效；该框架通过显式建模域间分布差异、兼顾源域通用知识与目标域局部动态，能够缓解负迁移并提升短时程PM2.5预报精度。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [255] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"测试时世界模型推理"(Twin)系统：由前沿编码模型编写可执行的世界模型，仅凭模拟与交互来自动推断ARC-AGI-3等持续学习游戏隐藏的规则与目标，并采用"孪生世界模型"进行回放验证与反例修复，最终在183个关卡中通过179个（97.8%），将基础模型得分从7.8%提升至93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个任务手工设计世界模型，成本高昂且无法泛化。每个游戏都隐藏规则与目标，作者希望仅通过模拟和交互自动构建可用的世界模型，解决持续学习场景中的自动推断问题。

Method: 系统赋予编码智能体对网格游戏较强的归纳先验，使其能近乎完全恢复真实转移与目标；通过孪生世界模型进行回放验证，即程序必须复现此前所有已观测到的游戏转移后才允许执行动作；每次预测与实际结果的不匹配都会作为反例用于修复世界模型；目标在无奖励时先被推断，剩余关卡则通过搜索自动发现。

Result: Twin在183个关卡中通过179个（97.8%），其中158个（88.3%）比人类更高效；156个关卡（87.2%）在获得任何奖励前就推断出目标，其余关卡通过搜索发现目标。基准按0-100对完成度与动作效率打分：基础模型仅7.8%，现成harness提升至61.1%，而孪生世界模型将同一基础模型提升至93.3%，清通25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 构建可用的世界模型比预期更简单，真正困难的问题在于推断正确的目标。该结果表明测试时世界模型推理能显著提升编码模型在持续学习任务上的表现。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [256] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对道德AI偏好 elicitation 管线的三个关键阶段（特征选择、投票者抽样、问题措辞）进行实证检验，发现每个阶段都会实质性地影响最终生成的偏好画像。研究基于两个阶段共809名被试、三个部署场景（AI肾脏分配、模拟缺勤工人的AI代理、生成式AI再现逝者），表明基于投票的对齐无法仅靠聚合实现公平或透明的AI，各阶段需要审计与披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德偏好 elicitation 被广泛用于让AI在道德负荷决策中代表公众意见，即通过民意投票聚合结果训练AI策略。但开发者在投票前做出的三个关键选择（特征范围、投票者抽样、问题框架）往往被视为技术细节，缺乏透明度与规范性审视。作者旨在证明这些看似中立的工程决策实际上具有规范性影响。

Method: 研究设计采用两阶段实证调查（N=809），在三个部署情境（AI肾脏分配、AI代理模拟缺勤工人、生成式AI再现逝者）中系统操纵并检验道德AI elicitation 管线的三个阶段：1) 比较不同情境下道德相关特征集的迁移性；2) 考察政治意识形态对特征偏好的影响及其方向；3) 改变 elicitation 问题的措辞，测量其对意识形态分歧及道德基础关联的调节效应。

Result: 主要发现包括：道德相关特征在不同情境间发生迁移，特征schema不能跨部署领域直接套用；约三分之一的特征上偏好因政治意识形态而异，且部分差异方向相反，投票者池的意识形态构成会改变聚合偏好画像；问题措辞可将意识形态分歧扩大或缩小至整整一个量表点，且框架条件会改变道德基础与参与者判断之间的关联方式。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，投票式对齐无法仅靠聚合本身达至公平或透明的AI。道德AI elicitation 管线的每一个阶段都应被审计并公开披露，因为从特征界定到谁投票、再到如何提问，每一环节都塑造着最终被AI采纳的道德偏好。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [257] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大语言模型的应用中“会话交接”（session handover）的方法论与理论性质：当上下文达到模型输入上限、应用重启或交由另一智能体继续任务时，需要决定将上一会话的哪些信息传递给新会话。作者将交接形式化为任务相关的上下文学习（ICL）状态的迁移，区分“精确恢复早期材料”与“保留目标分布”两种目标，并在外生性条件下用预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接及其定长比特需求，进而给出三类记录构成的具体方案及高斯线性回归与非参数回归下的记忆-误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM应用中，任务常因上下文长度限制、应用重启或换agent等原因在多个会话间延续，此时必须决定传递哪些历史信息。已有工作缺乏对“什么信息必须保留”以及“记忆需求如何随任务变化”的系统性理论，因此需要一套方法论的、理论化的交接框架。

Method: 将交接形式化为任务相关ICL状态的转移，并区分精确恢复与分布保留两种判据；引入外生性条件，以预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接，导出定长比特需求。分析分离了记忆约束、写入者与续写流程三者的作用，并量化“在下游查询未知前先写入”的代价。提出三部分记录：精确保存决策与约束、用任务正当统计量压缩重复证据、保留未被统计量捕获的原始观察。对高斯线性回归给出精确有限维交接与有限比特扰动界，对非参数回归给出联系记忆量与平方预测误差的上下界。

Result: 得到如下结果：在预测等价性与外生性条件下，可构造最粗的确定性充分交接并给出定长比特需求；三部分记录方案能兼顾精确保存与统计压缩；高斯线性回归场景存在精确的有限维交接和有限比特扰动界；非参数回归下记忆量与平方预测误差之间存在上下界关系，刻画了记忆约束对预测质量的直接影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为“交接必须保留什么”提供了理论与方法，阐明了交接的记忆需求如何依赖于续写任务的性质，并显式刻画了先写入（下游查询未知）与记忆受限带来的代价，为LLM多会话应用的交接设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [SN-ASMO: Satellite-Navigation Array Spatial-Manifold Precise Observation Theory A Mathematical Foundation for Observation Formation, Unified U(1) Geometry, Intrinsic Information, and Preservation of the RTK Integer Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13611)
*Xianwei Meng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出 SN-ASMO 框架，在压制干扰、接收平台高动态运动和载波相位 RTK 场景下，将观测形成过程统一建模，严格分离"传播引起的变化"与"观测者引起的变化"，从而保证接收机主动参与观测形成（阵列几何、通道状态、权重、信号处理规则）时，载波观测仍能连续表征同一客观传播过程并保持整周模糊度整数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当接收机通过阵列几何、通道状态、权重和信号处理规则主动参与观测形成时，载波观测量如何在保持相位连续性与整周模糊度整数结构的前提下，继续代表同一客观传播过程？物理世界不因观测者重构而改变，但其在观测世界的复杂表征会改变。若不严格区分传播引起与观测者引起的变化，抗干扰、高动态与精密载波相位定位将在观测形成层面被结构性耦合。

Method: SN-ASMO 将观测形成、参考协方差、精确干扰商（exact nuisance quotients）、同源传输（same-source transport）、内在统计信息以及 RTK 整数结构保持统一在一个数学封闭且物理可检验的框架内；并给出静态干扰、动态旋转、支路间定时失配、近零响应、射频重放和 RTK 整数风险验证等可复现、可证伪的测试协议。

Result: 形成了数学闭环、物理可检验的统一框架，能够在接收机主动重构观测形成的条件下解耦"传播引起的变化"与"观测者引起的变化"，保持载波相位连续并维持整周模糊度的整数结构；通过多类可复现、可证伪的协议对框架进行验证。

Conclusion: SN-ASMO 的作用域化创新在于：在同一个数学封闭且物理可检验的框架内，统一了观测形成、参考协方差、精确干扰商、同源传输、内在统计信息以及 RTK 整数结构的保持，从而在观测形成层面彻底解耦抗干扰、高动态与精密载波相位定位之间的结构性耦合。

Abstract: Suppressive interference, high-dynamic motion of the receiving platform, and carrier-phase RTK lead satellite navigation to one fundamental question: when the receiver actively participates in observation formation through its array geometry, channel states, weights, and signal-processing rules, how can the resulting carrier observation continue to represent the same objective propagation process while preserving continuous phase and the integer-ambiguity structure? The physical world is not changed by a reconfiguration of the observer, but its complex representation in the observation world is. Without a strict separation between propagation-induced and observer-induced variations, anti-jamming, high dynamics, and precise carrier-phase positioning become structurally coupled at the observation-formation level.
  Finally, reproducible and falsifiable protocols are provided for static interference, dynamic rotation, inter-branch timing mismatch, near-null response, RF replay, and RTK integer-risk validation. The scoped novelty of SN-ASMO is the unification of observation formation, reference covariance, exact nuisance quotients, same-source transport, intrinsic statistical information, and preservation of the RTK integer structure in one mathematically closed and physically testable framework.

</details>


### [259] [A Survey of Typical-Cell Volume Distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay Tessellations: Analytical Theory, High-Dimensional Limits, and Wireless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13615)
*Minghua Xia,Tian Shi,Wenkunn Wen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了由泊松点过程诱导的泊松- Voronoi 与泊松-Delaunay 两类典型随机空间剖分中的典型胞体积分布：对比了二者的解析可处理性（Voronoi 缺乏无条件闭式分布，Delaunay 则具有显式 PDF/CDF/矩公式），总结了主要分析方法、精确与近似结果、高维极限，并讨论了无线网络应用与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松-Voronoi 与泊松-Delaunay 剖分是随机几何中的典型对偶结构，其典型胞体积分布对覆盖、负载、连通性等系统特征建模至关重要。然而文献对此二者的分析呈现明显不对称：Delaunay 单纯形体积具有维度显式的闭式分布，而 Voronoi 胞体积缺乏可处理的元条件闭式分布，实际建模多依赖仿真与经验近似。作者受此对比驱动，旨在系统梳理、综合并指出开放问题。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法：回顾主要解析技术（Mellin 变换、Meijer G 函数、尺度-形状分解、条件 Gamma 结构），综合精确与近似结果，归纳新兴的高维极限结论，并结合无线网络应用场景（负载建模、协作传输、三维架构）进行讨论，最后列出开放问题。

Result: 指出泊松-Voronoi 胞体积在平面情形存在精确积分表示，且近期有含条件 Gamma 结构的一般维度精确表示，但其归一化形状律与无界面数混合仍是隐式的，且无可处理的元条件闭式分布；与之对比，泊松-Delaunay 单纯形体积拥有基于 Mellin 变换与 Meijer G 函数的维度显式 PDF、CDF 与矩公式。此外汇总了高维极限结果以及在无线网络中的具体应用。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统性综述弥合了两类剖分在体积分布分析上的不对称性认知，确认 Delaunay 情形解析结果完备而 Voronoi 情形仍主要依赖近似的现状，并明确指出未来研究方向：元条件泊松-Voronoi 分布、非泊松空间模型、数据驱动的几何推断以及维度感知的无线网络建模。

Abstract: Random spatial tessellations generated by point processes provide fundamental models for proximity, space partitioning, and local geometry in stochastic systems. Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations induced by homogeneous Poisson point processes form a canonical dual pair used in stochastic geometry, computational geometry, spatial statistics, and wireless-network analysis. Their typical-cell volume distributions provide important geometric inputs for modeling coverage, traffic load, clustering, connectivity, and other system characteristics. Despite extensive study, the literature remains analytically asymmetric. For Poisson--Voronoi cell volumes, exact integral representations exist in certain planar settings, while a recent scale--shape factorization provides an exact general-dimensional representation with conditional Gamma structure. However, the normalized shape laws and unbounded facet-count mixture remain implicit, and tractable unconditional closed-form distributions are unavailable. Practical modeling therefore relies largely on simulation, moment characterizations, and empirical approximations. By contrast, Poisson--Delaunay simplex volumes admit dimension-explicit PDFs, CDFs, and moment formulas derived through Mellin-transform analysis and Meijer's \(G\)-function representations. Motivated by this contrast, this paper surveys typical-cell volume distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations. We review the main analytical methods, synthesize exact and approximate results, summarize emerging high-dimensional limits, and discuss wireless-network applications, including load modeling, cooperative transmission, and three-dimensional architectures. We also identify open problems concerning unconditional Poisson--Voronoi distributions, non-Poisson spatial models, data-driven geometric inference, and dimension-aware network modeling.

</details>


### [260] [Energy-Aware Compression-Computation Co-Adaptation for Latency Minimization in Multi-User Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13632)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Yumin Park,Avi Deb Raha,Huy Q. Le,Zhu Han,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对DeepJSCC语义通信中用户异构性（信道质量、QoS期望、本地能量差异）带来的挑战，提出能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应（CoCo）框架。基站通过发送更长信号或任务卸载来满足QoS，用户需消耗能量进行信号去噪以恢复高保真潜在特征。作者将问题分解为参数优化与资源分配两个子问题，提出无需重训练即可适应多样压缩率与信道噪声的鲁棒编解码器，并采用贪心子载波分配降低通信时延。仿真表明CoCo相比仅速率自适应或仅去噪方案能降低总时延并满足各用户需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeepJSCC语义通信虽在低信道带宽比下能实现高感知质量，但难以适应不同用户在信道质量、预期QoS目标和可用本地能量方面的异构性，缺乏统一考虑这些异构因素的解决方案。

Method: 1) 显式建模用户设备在预期QoS、信道条件和本地能量上的异构性并数学化描述问题；2) 提出能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应（CoCo）框架，融合传输信号长度调整与任务卸载两条QoS满足路径，用户端以能量代价换取去噪后的高保真潜在特征；3) 将原问题分解为参数优化和资源分配两个子问题；4) 设计无需重训练即可适应不同压缩率和信道噪声的鲁棒编解码器；5) 采用贪心子载波分配算法降低通信时间。

Result: 在加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）信道和标准图像数据集上的仿真验证了CoCo框架的有效性：相比仅速率自适应的DeepJSCC或仅去噪的方案，CoCo可降低总时延，同时确保每个个体用户的QoS需求得到满足。

Conclusion: 通过压缩与计算两方面的联合自适应，CoCo能有效调和用户异构性带来的QoS满足与能量开销之间的权衡，为异构无线网络中DeepJSCC语义通信的实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep joint source-channel coding-enabled (DeepJSCC) semantic communication (SemCom) has excelled at delivering high perceptual quality at low channel-bandwidth ratios, which positions it as a pillar for next-generation wireless networks. However, the existing works have difficulty accommodating user heterogeneity in terms of communication channel quality, expected quality-of-service (QoS) targets, and the available local energy. Therefore, in this paper, we explicitly reflect the heterogeneity of user devices in terms of the differences in expected QoS, channel condition, and local energy, and then mathematically formulate the problem. Next, we propose an energy-aware compression-computation co-adaptation (CoCo) framework, in which the base station can meet the expected user QoS by transmitting a longer signal or offloading the task to a local device. The user has to dedicate energy to denoising the signal to recover higher-fidelity latent features before feeding it to the semantic decoder. To solve the formulated problem, we first decompose it into two sub-problems: parameter optimization and resource allocation problems. Specifically, we propose a robust codec that effectively works under a diversity of compression rates and channel noise without re-training, while the greedy sub-carrier allocation lowers the communication time. Finally, we present simulation results on standard image datasets over additive white Gaussian noise to demonstrate the effectiveness of CoCo, which reduces total latency relative to rate-only adaptive DeepJSCC or denoising-only, thereby ensuring the demands of each individual user are met.

</details>


### [261] [Optimal Power Allocation and AI Receiver Design for Superimposed DMRS and Data Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13809)
*Sha Hu,Zhongwang Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究基于OFDM的MIMO系统中，将解调参考符号（DMRS）与数据叠加（SI-DMRS）传输的方案。作者首先推导了一个解析框架，用以刻画迭代信道估计（CE）与MIMO检测（MD）过程中二者均方误差（MSE）的迭代演化行为，并据此优化DMRS与数据符号间的功率分配和导频图案；其次，设计了一种基于Transformer编码器、内嵌迭代CE与检测（ICED）结构的AI接收机。仿真结果表明，所提AI-ICED接收机结合SI-DMRS相比采用非重叠DMRS的传统系统能有效提升频谱效率（SE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIMO-OFDM系统中，DMRS与数据符号在时频资源上非重叠复用，导频开销占用大量资源，导致频谱效率受限。若将DMRS叠加在数据符号上（SI-DMRS），可消除导频开销、提高频谱效率，但叠加会引入导频与数据之间的相互干扰，使信道估计与检测性能耦合恶化。因此需要深入理解迭代CE与MD之间的交互行为，并设计能有效抑制干扰的接收机，这正是本文的出发点。

Method: 本文方法分两步：(1) 推导解析框架，刻画ICED过程中信道估计MSE与MIMO检测MSE之间的迭代演化关系，并基于该框架联合优化DMRS与数据之间的功率分配以及导频（参考符号）图案；(2) 提出基于Transformer编码器、融合ICED结构的AI接收机（AI-ICED），利用深度学习的表示能力联合处理叠加导频场景下的信道估计与信号检测，以适配SI-DMRS传输。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的AI-ICED接收机在SI-DMRS传输下，相比采用非重叠DMRS和数据的传统系统，能够有效提升频谱效率（SE）；同时，基于解析框架优化的功率分配和导频图案设计进一步增强了系统性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了SI-DMRS结合AI-ICED接收机是提升MIMO-OFDM系统频谱效率的有效途径：解析优化为资源（功率与导频图案）分配提供理论指导，Transformer编码器为核心的AI接收机则具备处理叠加干扰下复杂CE/MD联合问题的能力，二者结合可在消除导频开销的同时获得显著的SE增益。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider transmissions with superimposed (SI) demodulation-reference-symbol (DMRS) and data in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) based multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. First, we derive an analytical framework to characterize the iterative behavior between the mean-square errors (MSEs) of channel estimation (CE) and MIMO detection (MD) within an iterative CE and detection (ICED) process. This framework is subsequently utilized to optimize power allocation and pilot patterns between the DMRS and data symbols for SI-DMRS transmission. Second, we design an artificial intelligence (AI) based receiver built upon Transformer encoders for SI-DMRS transmissions, which incorporates an iterative CE and detection (ICED) structure. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AI-ICED receiver, combined with SI-DMRS, effectively increases spectral efficiency (SE) compared to conventional systems using non-overlapped DMRS and data symbols.

</details>


### [262] [The Capacity Region of the Multiple Access Channel with Non-Signaling Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13860)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了在非信令（NS）辅助下K发送者离散无记忆多址接入信道（MAC）的容量区域，证明其与经典容量区域形式相同，唯一区别是允许各发送者输入分布任意相关。同时构造例子表明NS辅助带来的容量乘性增益可任意接近K，结合先前的上界，确立了K是所有K发送者MAC中NS辅助乘性增益的极值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多址接入信道（MAC）的容量区域在经典信息论中已被完全刻画，但经典假设各发送者的输入相互独立。非信令（NS）资源作为一种超量子关联形式，可放宽这一独立性约束。此前Fawzi和Fermé仅对K=2发送者情形建立了外界的界，因此亟需将该结果推广到一般K发送者情形，并探究NS辅助所能带来的容量增益究竟有多大。

Method: 本文将Fawzi和Fermé针对K=2发送者的NS辅助外界界自然推广到一般的K发送者情形，证明NS辅助下的容量区域与经典容量区域具有相同的形式，仅将独立输入分布替换为允许任意相关的联合输入分布，从而给出该容量区域的完整刻画。此外，作者构造了具体的K发送者MAC实例，分析NS辅助容量相对经典容量的乘性增益。

Result: 证明了K发送者MAC在NS辅助下的容量区域可由经典容量区域形式完全刻画（仅输入分布改为任意相关）；给出了该结果与K=2情形的Fawzi-Fermé外界界相一致的说明；构造了乘性增益任意接近K的示例，结合先前的上界，确立了最大乘性增益的极值恰好为K。

Conclusion: 该工作将NS辅助MAC的容量区域刻画从2发送者推广到任意K发送者，并确立了NS辅助乘性增益的极值上界K是可达到的，从而完整解决了K发送者情形下NS辅助MAC容量增益的极值问题。

Abstract: The capacity region of the $K$-sender discrete memoryless multiple access channel (MAC) is fully characterized when non-signaling (NS) assistance is available to all $K$ transmitters and the receiver. It is shown to have the same form as the classical capacity region of the MAC, except that the input distribution is allowed to be arbitrarily dependent across the senders. In particular, the NS-assisted capacity region matches the natural generalization to $K$ senders of an outer bound that was previously established by Fawzi and Fermé for $K=2$ senders. Additionally, we provide examples of $K$-sender MACs where the multiplicative gain in capacity from NS-assistance is arbitrarily close to $K$. Combined with an upper bound from prior work, this establishes $K$ as the extremal value of the multiplicative gain from NS-assistance across all $K$-sender MAC settings.

</details>


### [263] [New lower bounds for constant-weight codes via seeded bit-swap tabu search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13906)
*William Echols*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用带种子初始化的比特交换禁忌搜索算法，为二元等重码的最大尺寸 A(n,d,w) 找到了 124 个新的构造，改进了现有下界；并借此改进了 k=32、33、34、37 时的亲吻数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元等重码 A(n,d,w) 的最大尺寸确定是组合编码理论中的经典难题，现有下界仍有提升空间；同时 A(n,8,8) 的更强下界可间接推导出高维亲吻数的改进，因此构造更好的码具有重要意义。

Method: 采用带种子（seeded）初始化的比特交换（bit-swap）禁忌搜索（tabu search）元启发式算法，通过局部搜索与禁忌机制在候选码空间中寻找并验证能突破现有下界的二元等重码构造。

Result: 共找到 124 个新的构造，改进了 A(n,d,w) 的现有下界；特别地，通过增强 A(n,8,8)（n∈{32,33,34,37}）的下界，进一步改进了亲吻数 τ_32、τ_33、τ_34 和 τ_37 的下界。

Conclusion: 研究表明，种子初始化与比特交换禁忌搜索相结合是一种有效发现更优等重码的方法，并表明改进 A(n,8,8) 等特定下界能带动亲吻数等其他组合问题下界的同步提升。

Abstract: A binary constant-weight code is a set of binary words of length $n$ such that each word has exactly weight $w$ and is at least Hamming distance $d$ from every other word in the set. $A(n,d,w)$ denotes the maximum size of a binary constant-weight code with parameters $(n,d,w)$. Using seeded initialization with bit-swap tabu search, we found 124 new constructions that improve existing lower bounds for $A(n,d,w)$. As a corollary of stronger bounds on $A(n,8,8)$ for $n \in \{ 32,33,34,37 \}$, we also improve lower bounds on kissing numbers $τ_{32}$, $τ_{33}$, $τ_{34}$, and $τ_{37}$.

</details>


### [264] [A Structural Characterization of Entropy Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13917)
*Daniel Lazarev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在测度论框架下系统刻画了“可容许”的熵泛函：要求当输入测度关于参考测度绝对连续时，输入熵不超过参考熵。在广义均值复合假设下，作者完整刻画了所有此类熵泛函，得到一个由均值生成元、熵标度与可加性假设逐级决定的四层层级；其核心判据是 g(1/t) 的严格凸/凹性，该判据等价于 Csiszár f-散度生成元的严格凸性，从而推出带精确等号条件的 Markov 核数据处理不等式，并回答了 Rényi 于 1961 年提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon、Rényi、Tsallis 乃至更一般的熵族在最大熵推断、最小散度估计和拟合优度检验等统计方法中被广泛使用，但选择哪种熵往往出于惯例而非结构原则，缺乏一个判定“何类熵是合理/可容许的”的公理化依据，因而亟需一个统一的测度论框架来给出结构性的选择准则。

Method: 作者引入测度论框架，将可容许性定义为：凡输入测度关于参考测度绝对连续，则前者的熵必有界于后者之上。在广义均值复合（generalized mean-value composition）的约束下，利用凸分析工具对熵泛函进行表征，并按均值生成元、熵标度和可加性假设（乘积可加、内部可加）逐级建立四层层级；同时将该判据与 Csiszár f-散度的凸性及数据处理不等式相关联。

Result: 刻画得出：连续严格单调的生成元 g 可容许当且仅当对递增 g，t↦g(1/t) 严格凸；对递减 g，则严格凹。该判据同时等价于相应 Csiszár f-散度生成元的严格凸性，从而在 Markov 核下获得带精确等号条件的数据处理性质。层级中，乘积可加性唯一确定 Rényi 族；内部可加性（或乘积可加性加上算术均值复合）唯一确定 Shannon 熵；此外还构造出新的可容许熵族与散度族。

Conclusion: 该研究把熵的选择从惯例提升为可判定的结构原则，解决了 Rényi 关于哪些广义均值可替代其公理化中算术均值的问题，并建立了熵可容许性、f-散度凸性与数据处理不等式之间统一的等价刻画，为熵族的选择与构造提供了构造性的新工具。

Abstract: Entropy functionals and their associated divergences underlie many statistical methods, including maximum entropy inference, minimum divergence estimation, and goodness-of-fit testing, yet choosing among Shannon, Rényi, Tsallis, and more general entropies is often a matter of convention rather than structural principle. We introduce a measure theoretic framework in which admissibility requires the entropy of an input measure to be bounded above by that of its reference measure whenever the former is absolutely continuous with respect to the latter. Under generalized mean-value composition, we characterize all such entropy functionals and obtain a four-level hierarchy determined successively by the mean generator, entropy scale, and additivity assumptions. A continuous strictly monotone generator $g$ is admissible exactly when $t\mapsto g(1/t)$ is strictly convex for increasing $g$, or strictly concave for decreasing $g$. This resolves a question posed by Rényi (Proc. 4th Berkeley Sympos. Math. Statist. Prob., 1961) concerning which generalized means may replace the arithmetic mean in his entropy axiomatization. The same criterion is equivalent to strict convexity of an associated Csiszár $f$-divergence generator and therefore yields data processing under Markov kernels with an exact equality condition. Within this hierarchy, product additivity singles out the Rényi family, while internal additivity, or product additivity together with arithmetic mean-value composition, singles out Shannon entropy. The characterization is constructive and yields new admissible entropy and divergence families, including integral-transform examples.

</details>


### [265] [Integrated Information in the Active Inference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14165)
*Carlotta Langer,Jesse van Oostrum,Nihat Ay*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合主动推理（Active Inference）与整合信息理论（IIT）的研究方法：在主动推理智能体的生成模型上附加结构假设来定义整合信息度量，并通过仿真实验发现该度量与智能体的自由能之间存在随生成模型规模增大而增强的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推理框架以统一方式处理感知与行动选择，为建模智能行为提供了原理性方法；而整合信息理论旨在通过系统整合信息的能力来量化意识水平。作者希望将两者结合，从而能够从整合信息的视角分析主动推理智能体，探究模型层面意识相关度量与智能体行为（自由能）之间的内在联系。

Method: 作者以主动推理智能体为基础，建立关于世界相关动态的内部生成模型，用于推断未来观测、内部状态并选择行动；同时引入整合信息理论的分析视角，通过对生成模型施加额外的结构假设，为其定义了一个新的整合信息度量；随后利用模拟智能体开展实验，考察该度量与主动推理智能体自由能之间的相关性。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所定义的整合信息度量与主动推理智能体的自由能之间存在相关性，且该相关性随生成模型规模的增大而增强。

Conclusion: 该项工作表明生成模型的整合信息度量能够与主动推理的核心量（自由能）建立可观测的联系，为从意识（整合信息）角度理解和分析主动推理智能体提供了新的分析工具与经验证据，尤其随模型规模增大这种联系更为显著。

Abstract: The active inference framework provides a principled approach to modeling sentient behavior. In this framework perception and action selection are treated in a unified way. The resulting agents form an internal generative model of the relevant dynamics of the world in order to infer their future observations, their internal states and to select actions. We combine this modeling framework with the Integrated Information Theory of consciousness and are therefore able to analyze the active inference agents from the perspective of integrated information. The Integrated Information Theory aims at quantifying the level of consciousness of a system by assessing its capability to integrate information. Here, we define a measure of integrated information for the generative model by making an additional structural assumption. Experiments with simulated agents reveal a correlation between integrated information measures and the free energy of the active inference agents that increases with the size of the generative model.

</details>


### [266] [Positioning with Flexible Reflectors: Solution and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14184)
*Jiajun He,Han Yu,Danyan Lin,Gaofeng Pan,Hing Cheung So,Stefano Buzzi,Hien Quoc Ngo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对非视距（无LoS）场景，利用柔性反射面（FR）构建虚拟视距路径实现目标定位；提出低复杂度高精度估计器，推导克拉美罗下界（CRLB）并用于优化FR的位置与朝向，同时分析随机部署下CRLB的统计分布，仿真验证所提方案接近CRLB并给出部署指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性反射面（FR）是低成本、高能效的电磁传播环境重塑方案，但现有工作较少研究其在无LoS路径下的目标定位能力，且缺少对定位精度极限与FR部署策略的系统性分析。

Method: 利用FR产生的虚拟视距路径，设计简单而准确的定位估计器，消除LoS缺失的影响；推导CRLB以刻画性能极限，并以CRLB为准则优化FR的位置与朝向；进一步引入随机FR部署模型，推导CRLB的概率分布，分析网络配置对定位精度的影响。

Result: 仿真表明所提估计方案性能逼近CRLB；解析结果揭示了不同网络配置（如FR部署方式）对定位精度的作用规律，为FR部署提供实用指导。

Conclusion: FR辅助的虚拟LoS机制可在严重遮挡条件下实现高精度目标定位，理论CRLB分析与优化方法为FR群辅助定位系统的设计与部署提供了依据。

Abstract: Flexible reflectors (FRs) have emerged as a low-cost and energy-efficient solution for reshaping electromagnetic propagation environments across a wide range of applications. This paper investigates FR-swarm-assisted target localization in scenarios where line-of-sight (LoS) paths are unavailable. By leveraging the virtual LoS paths created by the FRs, a simple yet accurate estimator is proposed for localization under severe blockage conditions. To characterize the performance limits of the proposed scheme, we derive the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) and use it to optimize the positions and orientations of the FRs. Furthermore, by accounting for random FR deployment, we characterize the CRLB distribution and reveal how different network configurations affect localization accuracy. Simulation results demonstrate that the developed scheme closely approaches the CRLB performance, while the derived analytical results provide useful guidelines for FR deployment and network design.

</details>


### [267] [Lossy Compression via Sparse Regression Codes: Generalized Construction and Finite-length Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14494)
*Galen Reeves,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏回归码（SPARCs）在有损压缩场景下的简单贪心编码规则（基于相关性与基于距离两种），并将SPARC构造推广为一类"加性正交"回归码（标准SPARC为其特例）。通过跟踪各阶段编码残差的演变，导出了关于平方误差失真的非渐近界，揭示了功率分配对失真控制的决定性作用，并据此优化功率分配，从而改善SPARC的有限长度压缩性能，同时为低复杂度变体（如含符号SPARC和K稀疏SPARC）提供失真保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有损压缩中的稀疏回归码通常在编码复杂度与理论性能之间存在权衡。标准SPARC的构造及其性能分析在有限长度下尚需更精确的失真刻画，而简单贪心编码规则（如相关型和距离型）虽然在实践中高效，但其数学分析不够完备。作者希望统一并推广SPARC构造，建立非渐近的失真界，并借此理解功率分配如何影响编码性能，从而同时提升有限长度表现并覆盖低复杂度变体。

Method: 作者提出了一类更一般的"加性正交"回归码（additive orthogonal regression codes），其中标准SPARC是特例。围绕两种简单贪心编码规则（基于相关性的预测与基于距离的选取），通过逐阶段跟踪编码残差的演化过程，利用数学递推分析推导平方误差失真的非渐近上界。基于该界中功率分配所起的作用，将失真界对码参数（功率分配）进行优化，得到更优的有限长度压缩性能，并将分析方法扩展到含符号SPARC、K稀疏SPARC等低复杂度变体。

Result: 得到了加性正交回归码在贪心编码下的非渐近平方误差失真界；证明功率分配是控制失真的关键参数，并给出基于码参数的最优分配策略。优化的功率分配显著改善了SPARC的有限长度压缩性能；同时文中提供的界对含符号SPARC和K稀疏SPARC等低复杂度编码变体给出了明确的失真保证。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过对功率分配的系统优化，贪心编码的SPARC类编码能够在较低复杂度下获得有保证且更优的有限长度有损压缩性能。这一框架统一了标准SPARC及其变体，为实际系统中采用低复杂度稀疏回归编码提供了理论依据和可操作的功率分配准则。

Abstract: We study sparse regression codes (SPARCs) for lossy compression under simple greedy encoding rules, including both correlation-based and distance-based methods. We generalize the SPARC construction, and consider the class of \emph{additive orthogonal} regression codes, of which standard SPARCs are a special case. For this class of codes, we derive nonasymptotic bounds on the squared-error distortion by tracking the evolution of the encoding residual across stages. Our results highlight the role of power allocation in controlling the distortion, allowing us to optimize the allocation based on the parameters of the code. The optimized allocation improves the finite-length compression performance of SPARCs, and our bounds provide distortion guarantees for lower complexity variants of SPARCs, like signed SPARCs and $K$-sparse SPARCs.

</details>


### [268] [Learning-to-Transition for Large-scale and High-Order MIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14511)
*Yubo Zhang,Yiyao Liu,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出学习式转移（L2T）框架，将高阶MIMO检测建模为完整向量转移的随机序列：硬输出检测通过残差到BER的课程式训练递归应用转移网络；软输出检测则将训练好的硬策略参数级克隆到解耦的软输入软输出IDD接收机每一层，实现硬到软的迁移，并利用贝叶斯先验和有监督多阶段训练生成LLR供LDPC译码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶MIMO检测需要在巨大离散符号空间中高效搜索，同时为信道译码提供可靠的软信息，而传统方法在大规模高阶场景下难以兼顾搜索效率与软输出质量。

Method: (1) 将检测形式化为完整向量转移的随机序列，每个转移由信道耦合Transformer同时更新实例嵌入与采样策略，并用逐块自回归分解刻画流间依赖以控制序列复杂度。(2) 硬输出阶段：递归应用转移网络，采用"残差到BER"课程学习，先用精确残差度量学习MIMO搜索几何，再与发送比特精度对齐。(3) 软输出阶段：将硬策略在参数层面克隆进每一层解耦的软输入软输出迭代检测译码（IDD）接收机（tied-to-untied转移），保留零先验搜索动力学并允许逐层/逐轮特化。(4) IDD每轮中，译码先验按贝叶斯法则倾斜候选生成，对似然加权的终止假设生成后验与外部LLR供LDPC译码。(5) 采用多阶段训练，逐步让接收机接触合成与环内译码反馈以稳定硬到软迁移。

Result: 摘要未给出量化实验数据，但在机理上表明：硬策略经参数级克隆迁移后，在IDD中既保留零先验搜索动力学，又能依据译码反馈进行层/轮特化，并通过贝叶斯先验倾斜与似然加权假设生成高质量软信息，从而支撑LDPC迭代译码的收敛。

Conclusion: L2T框架以统一的"学习到转移"视角同时解决高阶MIMO的硬检测与软输出问题，课程学习保证硬策略可训练性，tied-to-untied参数迁移保证软接收机稳定收敛，为大规模MIMO高性能迭代检测提供新范式。

Abstract: High-order multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) detection requires efficient search over a large discrete symbol space while producing reliable soft information for channel decoding. This paper develops a learning-to-transition (L2T) framework that formulates MIMO detection as a stochastic sequence of complete-vector transitions. At each transition, a channel-coupled Transformer updates both the instance embedding and the sampling policy, while a blockwise autoregressive factorization captures inter-stream dependence with moderate sequential complexity. For hard-output detection, a transition network is applied recursively and trained through a residual-to-BER curriculum, which first learns the MIMO search geometry from the exact residual metric and then aligns the policy with transmitted-bit accuracy. For soft-output reception, the well-trained hard policy is cloned at the parameter level into every layer of an untied soft-input soft-output iterative detection and decoding (IDD) receiver. This tied-to-untied transfer preserves the learned zero-prior search dynamics while enabling layer- and round-specific specialization under decoder feedback. Within each IDD round, decoder priors tilt candidate generation according to Bayes' rule, and likelihood-weighted terminal hypotheses produce posterior and extrinsic log-likelihood ratios for LDPC decoding. A multi-stage training strategy further stabilizes the hard-to-soft transfer by progressively exposing the receiver to synthetic and in-loop decoder-generated priors.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [AlphaSeek: Trajectory-Level Self-Iterative Factor Mining Framework for Multi-Source Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13913)
*Qilu Zhu,Zijun Lu,Jianmin Zhu,Ning Chen,Shuo Yin,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaSeek，一个面向量化投资的大语言模型驱动端到端因子挖掘框架，通过自动化方向发现、轨迹级因子进化挖掘与自迭代组合优化，解决了现有方法在方向设计主观、多源信息融合不足、语义漂移、因子冗余以及缺乏端到端反馈闭环等方面的局限，并在CSI300上取得策略级与因子级指标的领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的量化因子挖掘方法存在五方面缺陷：1) 挖掘方向依赖人工主观设计；2) 对最新多源信息的整合有限；3) 存在语义漂移问题；4) 因子冗余严重；5) 缺少从因子发现到组合回测的端到端反馈闭环。这些局限制约了因子挖掘的自动化程度与有效性，亟需一个系统性框架加以解决。

Method: AlphaSeek包含三个核心模块：(1) 自动方向发现：收集并总结多源金融信息以识别有前景的挖掘方向；(2) 轨迹级因子进化挖掘：将优化单元从单一因子表达式扩展为完整研究轨迹（涵盖假设生成、因子构建、验证、回测与反馈），并引入三种进化算子——并行方向扩展、变异和交叉——以提升搜索多样性、优化质量与因子稳健性；(3) 自迭代因子组合：在冗余感知约束下，使新发现的因子与现有SOTA因子库交互，构建自迭代因子投资组合，形成从因子发现到组合回测的端到端反馈闭环。

Result: 在CSI300上的实验表明，AlphaSeek取得最优的整体策略级表现：年化收益率(ARR)为8.28%，信息比率(IR)为1.29，最大回撤(MDD)为6.28%；因子的预测性指标IC为0.0454，保持竞争力。此外，在CSI300上挖掘的因子迁移至CSI500时仍取得强时间序列收益表现，优于其他模型，显示出良好的跨市场可迁移性。

Conclusion: AlphaSeek通过端到端框架将自动化方向发现、轨迹级因子进化与自迭代组合优化有机结合，有效克服了传统LLM因子挖掘的多个局限，在策略绩效、因子预测力和跨市场泛化能力上均取得显著效果，为LLM驱动的量化因子研究提供了更系统、可迁移的范式。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models, LLM-driven quantitative factor mining has become an increasingly active research area. However, existing methods still suffer from subjective direction design, limited integration of up-to-date multi-source information, semantic drift, factor redundancy, and the absence of an end-to-end feedback loop from factor discovery to portfolio backtesting. To address these limitations, we propose AlphaSeek, an end-to-end factor mining framework for quantitative investment that integrates automated direction discovery, trajectory-level factor evolution mining and self-iterative portfolio optimization. AlphaSeek first collects and summarizes multi-source financial information to identify promising mining directions. It then performs trajectory-level factor mining by extending the optimization unit from a single factor expression to a complete research trajectory covering hypothesis generation, factor construction, validation, backtesting, and feedback. Based on this design, we introduce evolution operators - parallel direction expansion, mutation and crossover - to improve search diversity, refinement quality and factor robustness. Finally, AlphaSeek constructs a self-iterative factor portfolio, allowing newly discovered factors to interact with an existing state-of-the-art(SOTA) factor library under redundancy-aware constraints. Experiments on CSI300 show that AlphaSeek achieves the strongest overall strategy-level performance on CSI300 with ARR of 8.28%, IR of 1.29 and MDD of 6.28%, while remaining competitive on factor predictive metrics with IC of 0.0454, while factors mined on CSI300 also achieve strong time-series return performance on CSI500 than other models, suggesting promising cross-market transferability under a zero-shot setting.

</details>


### [270] [MMDynOpt-Agent: Dynamic Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Model Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14026)
*Wenjin Liu,Haoran Luo,Fayuan Ke,Zhenghong Lin,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: MMDynOpt-Agent 将多模态推理的动态优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过端到端强化学习训练一个轻量级多模态智能体，以自适应地生成多轮动态优化提示来引导目标多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的推理过程，并在奖励机制中同时考虑格式合规、答案正确性和预算控制，以实现准确且高效的多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将多模态输入的视觉线索与问题的语义高效转化为有效的推理条件，从而限制了多模态大语言模型的推理性能。

Method: 将多模态推理的动态优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并通过端到端强化学习进行优化；轻量级多模态智能体作为决策策略，将目标 MLLM 视为环境，通过多轮动态优化提示自适应地引导其推理；设计结合格式合规、答案正确性与预算感知的奖励机制，兼顾推理准确性与效率。该方法具有可迁移性，可在单个目标 MLLM 上训练并在推理时迁移到其他模型。

Result: 在十五个公开数据集上的实验表明，MMDynOpt-Agent 表现出强劲的性能，并优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MMDynOpt-Agent 通过强化学习驱动的自适应提示优化，有效提升了多模态推理的准确性与效率，且具备跨模型的可迁移性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong potential in visual understanding and complex reasoning tasks. However, existing methods often struggle to efficiently transform visual cues from multimodal inputs and the semantics of the question into effective reasoning conditions, thereby limiting the reasoning performance of multimodal large language models. To address this challenge, we propose MMDynOpt-Agent, which models the dynamic optimization of multimodal reasoning as a Markov decision process via end-to-end reinforcement learning. Specifically, a lightweight multimodal agent serves as the decision policy and interacts with the target MLLM as the environment, adaptively steering its reasoning through multi-turn dynamic optimization prompts. Furthermore, to reduce the cost of multimodal reasoning, a reward mechanism that combines format compliance, answer correctness, and budget awareness is designed to jointly ensure reasoning accuracy and efficiency. MMDynOpt-Agent is transferable and generalizable, enabling training with one target MLLM and inference-time transfer to others. Experimental results on fifteen public datasets show MMDynOpt-Agent achieves strong performance and outperforms baselines. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/MMDynOpt-Agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [What preferences can - and cannot - predict in multi-agent online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13810)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究博弈中基于序数偏好的解概念（偏好图）与无遗憾学习动力学（如FTRL）长期行为之间的关系。作者证明动力学稳定集必须蕴含偏好稳定，并发现二者仅在子博弈情形下等价；通过一个三人博弈反例说明此等价性在一般情形下失效，随后提出"聚合偏离下的弹性"这一易检验的收益条件，用以弥补该差距并保证任意纯策略组合集合（span）的渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究博弈的组合结构数据（偏好图）在多大程度上能决定无遗憾学习动力学（如FTRL）的长期结果，即检验"偏好式"的静态解概念能否作为动力学稳定性的判定准则。由于偏好图易于检验而动力学稳定性难以直接分析，建立二者之间的联系具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 采用双边研究路径：一方面证明"动力学稳定集的骨架（所含纯策略组合）必定偏好稳定（即对有利偏离封闭）"这一正向蕴含关系；另一方面研究其逆命题，先是证明在子博弈（通过限制玩家行动集得到的纯策略组合子集）情形下偏好可以刻画渐近稳定性，再构造一个三人博弈反例，展示在一般情形下偏好稳定集的span可以动力学不稳定，从而证明等价性失效；最后引入"聚合偏离下的弹性"这一基于收益、易于检验的条件，作为保证任意纯策略组合span渐近稳定的充分条件。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 每个动力学稳定集的骨架必须偏好稳定；(2) 在子博弈情形下，偏好能够刻画渐近稳定性；(3) 在一般情形下此等价性崩溃——构建了一个三人博弈，其中存在偏好稳定但其span动力学不稳定的集合；(4) "聚合偏离下的弹性"这一易检验的收益条件可以桥接这一差距，保证任意纯策略组合span的渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 结论表明偏好数据虽然能提供动力学稳定性的必要条件和某些特殊情形下的充分条件，但在一般博弈中不足以作为稳定性的判据；需要补充"聚合偏离下的弹性"这类收益信息才能系统地刻画无遗憾学习动力学的长期稳定行为。

Abstract: We examine the interplay between ordinal, preference-based solution concepts in games and the long-run behavior of game dynamics, asking in particular to what extent the combinatorial data of a game -- its preference graph -- determine the outcomes of no-regret learning dynamics -- such as follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In one direction, we show that the skeleton of every dynamically stable set (i.e. the set of pure profiles it contains) must also be preferentially stable, that is, it must be closed under profitable deviations. We then ask the converse question: when do preferences determine the long-run behavior of the players' learning dynamics? We begin by showing that preferences characterize asymptotic stability in the case of subgames -- i.e. subsets of pure profiles obtained by restricting players' action sets. Beyond this case however, the equivalence between dynamic and preferential stability collapses: concretely, we construct a three-player game with a preferentially stable set whose span is dynamically unstable, showing in this way that preferences do not suffice as a criterion of dynamic stability. We then bridge this gap via the notion of resilience under aggregate deviations, an easy-to-check payoff-based condition that guarantees asymptotic stability of arbitrary spans of pure strategies.

</details>


### [272] [Equilibrium Pricing in Oligopolistic Data Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14018)
*Bhaskar Ray Chaudhury,Jugal Garg,Eklavya Sharma,Jiaxin Song*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究寡头数据市场中的均衡定价问题，其中买方（如购买数据以提升模型精度的机器学习公司）有预算约束，卖方是策略性数据卖家。作者证明：由于数据的非竞争性（non-rivalry），在统一定价下，精确的纳什均衡（NE）可能不存在，甚至 1.363-近似 NE 也可能不存在；但当卖家采用分段线性凸定价函数（超越统一定价的更一般定价）时，可保证存在一个定价组合使得任何卖家通过偏离到任意统一价格最多只能提升两倍收益（即针对统一偏离的 2-近似 NE）。仿真实验表明快速收敛且经验近似比优于最坏情况界 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据在市场中的交易日益重要（如机器学习公司购买数据提升模型精度），但现有竞争均衡理论主要针对竞争性（rivalrous）商品，其均衡存在性保证并不适用于数据这类非竞争性商品。因此需要重新审视寡头数据市场中均衡定价的存在性与松弛均衡概念。

Method: 将卖家间的定价竞争建模为定价博弈，分析纯纳什均衡的存在性；通过构造反例证明在统一定价下精确 NE 和 1.363-近似 NE 都可能不存在；然后引入分段线性凸定价函数，证明其能在常数因子内保证近似稳定性（2-近似 NE 针对统一价格偏离）；最后通过仿真验证收敛性与经验近似效果。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）统一定价下精确 NE 不一定存在；（2）更强地，1.363-近似 NE 在统一定价下也可能不存在；（3）允许分段线性凸定价函数时，存在一个定价组合，使得任何卖家偏离到统一价格最多增收两倍，即 2-近似 NE（针对统一偏离）；（4）仿真显示快速收敛且经验近似比优于最坏情况界 2。

Conclusion: 数据的非竞争性从根本上改变了对均衡定价的理解：竞争商品所保证的均衡存在性在数据市场中失效，因此需要松弛均衡概念或更灵活的（超越统一的）定价方案来恢复稳定性。分段线性凸定价提供了常数因子的近似稳定性保证，且在实践中表现优于理论最坏情况界。

Abstract: We study equilibrium pricing in oligopolistic data markets with budget-constrained buyers (e.g., machine learning companies purchasing data to improve model accuracy) and strategic data sellers. Sellers compete by setting prices for their datasets, giving rise to a pricing game whose pure Nash equilibria correspond to equilibrium prices. While equilibrium prices are guaranteed for rivalrous goods via competitive equilibrium, we show that the non-rivalry of data fundamentally alters this picture: an exact Nash equilibrium (NE) need not exist, and in fact, 1.363-approximate NE may also not exist under uniform pricing. We therefore investigate relaxed equilibrium notions. Allowing sellers to use beyond-uniform pricing---specifically, piecewise-linear convex pricing functions---guarantees approximate stability within a constant factor: there exists a pricing profile in which no seller can improve revenue by a factor of two by deviating to any uniform price (a 2-approximate NE against uniform deviations). Finally, our simulations demonstrate fast convergence and empirical approximation guarantees that outperform the worst-case bound of 2.

</details>


### [273] [Optimal Pricing and Charging Strategy Design for Non-cooperative Battery Swapping Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14167)
*Huanyu Yan,Chenxi Sun,Huanxin Liao,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究竞争性市场中由同一聚合商协调的多个换电站（BSS）的最优定价与电池充电策略。作者构建了双层博弈模型：第一阶段BSS在日前市场确定换电价格，第二阶段在实时市场确定最优充电策略，并严格证明了子博弈完美纳什均衡（SPNE）的存在性与唯一性。基于唯一均衡提出最优定价与充电策略，并设计了应对换电需求波动的预测误差处理方法。基于西安真实数据的12个BSS仿真表明，所提策略可使单个BSS利润至少提升18.1%，且最优充电策略自然实现削峰填谷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换电是电动汽车快速补能的重要方式。随着越来越多主体参与建设换电站（BSS），需要研究非合作BSS如何在竞争性市场中最大化自身利润。现有研究对竞争环境下多个BSS的定价与充电协同优化关注不足。

Method: 构建由同一聚合商协调的竞争性BSS分层博弈模型：第一阶段在日前市场决策换电价格，第二阶段在实时市场决策电池充电策略；严格证明SPNE的存在性与唯一性；基于唯一均衡提出每座BSS的利润最大化定价与充电策略；并提出应对换电需求意外波动的预测误差处理方法。

Result: 基于中国西安真实数据的12-BSS系统仿真表明：所提定价与充电策略使单个BSS利润至少提升18.1%，同时最优充电策略自然实现了电网削峰填谷。

Conclusion: 唯一SPNE为竞争环境下的最优策略提供了理论支撑；所提方法在提升BSS个体利润的同时带来削峰填谷的电网效益，为聚合商协调下的竞争性换电市场运营提供可行方案。

Abstract: Battery swapping is a rapid way to recharge electric vehicles (EVs). As more and more entities are involved in building Battery Swapping Stations (BSSs), how non-cooperative BSSs maximize their profit in a competitive market needs further investigation. In this paper, we focus on a practical scenario where competitive BSSs are coordinated by the same aggregator. To study the optimal pricing and battery charging, we formulate a hierarchical game-theoretic model, where BSSs determine the swapping price in the day-ahead market in the first stage, and then determine the optimal battery charging strategy in the real-time market in the second stage. We rigorously prove the existence and uniqueness of the Subgame Perfect Nash Equilibrium (SPNE). In particular, the uniqueness property provides theoretical support that the strategy under equilibrium is optimal in the competitive environment. Based on the unique SPNE, we propose an optimal pricing and charging strategy for each BSS to maximize profit in the competitive market. A prediction error handling method is also proposed to deal with unexpected fluctuations in swapping demand. Our simulation with a 12-BSS system based on real-life data from Xi'an, China shows that our pricing and charging strategy increases the individual BSS profit by at least 18.1\%, while the optimal charging strategy naturally achieves peak shaving for the power grid.

</details>


### [274] [Individual Rationality in Constrained Hedonic Games: Friends, Enemies, and Neutrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14461)
*Šimon Schierrreich,Ildikó Schlotter*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究在朋友/敌人/中立关系诱导的hedonic博弈中，受规模约束的联盟形成问题：要求找到满足个体理性（IR）且恰好有k个非空联盟、每个联盟大小落在给定上下界内的划分。在敌人导向偏好下问题等价于尺寸受限的图着色，复杂度与之相应；在朋友导向偏好下问题则复杂得多，由敌意结构主导，并受规模约束严格程度及关系对称性影响，出现对称情形可解而非对称情形不可解的多种情况。作者从经典复杂性和参数化复杂性两个维度完整刻画了这一边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体理性（IR）单独在任意hedonic博弈中都很容易满足，但加上联盟数量与规模上下界的约束后，判定是否存在可行划分变得计算上困难。作者希望系统性地理解这种困难来自何处，尤其是朋友导向与敌人导向两种偏好细化下问题的本质差异，以及对称/非对称关系、规模约束严格程度等因素如何塑造复杂性。

Method: 将两类偏好模型分别归约到已知的组合结构：敌人导向偏好对应尺寸受限的图着色问题（沿用其复杂度结论）；朋友导向偏好则转化为由敌意结构支配的判定问题。作者结合经典复杂度与参数化复杂度分析，并考察关系对称与不对称两种设置，系统地为每种情形建立可解性与不可解性结果。

Result: 两类模型呈现截然不同的复杂度图景：敌人导向情形完全归结为尺寸受限图着色；朋友导向情形则更精细，复杂度由敌意关系（而非友谊）决定，并进一步取决于规模约束的严格程度与关系的对称性——多个情形在对称设置下可解，而引入不对称后变为不可解。作者给出了完整的复杂度边界刻画。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了带规模约束的IR联盟形成问题在朋友导向与敌人导向偏好下的经典及参数化复杂度边界，指出两者"截然不同"的故事：敌人导向由图着色驱动，朋友导向由敌意结构驱动，且对称性与规模约束的严格性是决定可解性的关键因素。

Abstract: We study constrained coalition formation in games induced by friends, enemies, and neutrals, under the two standard refinements of additively separable preferences: friend-oriented and enemy-oriented. We ask for partitions that are individually rational (IR), while additionally requiring exactly $k$ non-empty coalitions, each satisfying a prescribed lower and upper bound on its size. Although IR alone is trivial to satisfy for any hedonic game, the size constraints make it computationally intractable to decide whether a feasible partition exists.
  The two models tell strikingly different stories. Under enemy-oriented preferences, the problem collapses to size-constrained graph coloring, and its complexity follows accordingly. Under friend-oriented preferences, however, the picture is far more intricate, and is governed by the enmity structure rather than the friendships. The complexity is further shaped by two factors: how strict the imposed size requirements are, and whether relationships are symmetric or asymmetric, with several cases turning out tractable in the symmetric setting but intractable once asymmetry is allowed. Charting this boundary in terms of both classical and parameterized complexity, we provide a complete understanding of which properties of the friend/enemy structure are responsible for hardness.

</details>


### [275] [Ex-ante versus Ex-post: Egalitarian Facility Location Mechanism Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14499)
*Zohar Barak,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究设施选址机制设计问题：n个策略性智能体在欧氏空间报告位置，机制输出单一设施位置，目标是策略性下（strategyproof）最小化平等主义成本（最大智能体成本）。确定性最优近似比为2（独裁机制达到）。作者研究随机化"期望策略无偏"（strategyproof-in-expectation）机制，区分"事后"（ex-post，期望最大成本）与"事前"（ex-ante，最大期望成本）两种评价。主要结果：（1）低维中二者严格分离——在R上给出去近似比1的简单策略无偏机制；在R^2上设计"随机旋转角点"机制达ex-ante近似比至多1.598，突破确定性下界2，而ex-post目标下证明下界1.605；（2）高维中不可能——对d>>1，任何E-SP机制相比独裁机制改进仅为o_d(1)，因此RRCWM机制对平等主义目标在高维也是最优，其对任意d≥1在两类目标下均达近似比2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设施选址机制设计中的基本目标是构造策略无偏且公平的机制。确定性情形下最优近似比为2（由独裁机制实现），因此自然的问题是：随机化能否在保持策略无偏的前提下改善公平性（平等主义目标）。已有工作仅研究"事后"评价（期望最大成本，即对机制随机性取期望后看社会成本），但这一目标与期望策略无偏性在数学形式上并不自然对齐。作者提出改用"事前"评价（最大期望成本，即每个智能体在机制随机性下的期望成本取最大），因为它与"期望策略无偏"（strategyproof-in-expectation）在结构上自然匹配。此外，高维情形下是否存在能超越独裁机制的随机机制也是开放问题，作者希望给出完整答案。

Method: 作者从机制设计理论出发，同时采用构造与不可能性证明两种方法。（1）在R上给出一个简单的期望策略无偏机制，证明其ex-ante近似比为1（最优）。（2）在R^2上设计"Random Rotated Corner"机制：先对坐标做随机旋转再选取相应的角点作为设施位置，通过几何分析证明其ex-ante近似比至多1.598，从而突破确定性下界2；同时对ex-post目标构造下界1.605，证明两种评价标准在R^2中严格分离。（3）在高维（d>>1）中，通过对随机化机制的信息论/对称性论证建立不可能性，说明任何策略无偏机制的改进都不能超过o_d(1)。（4）分析RRCWM（Random Rotation Coordinate-Wise Median）机制，证明它对任意d≥1在ex-post与ex-ante两种目标下近似比均为2，因此是平等主义目标在高维下的最优。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）R上存在ex-ante近似比为1的简单策略无偏机制，达到最优；（2）R^2上"Random Rotated Corner"机制实现ex-ante近似比至多1.598，优于确定性最优的2；同时ex-post下界为1.605，表明两种评价标准严格分离；（3）高维（d>>1）中任何策略无偏机制相对独裁机制的改进至多o_d(1)，即没有随机机制能本质改善公平性；（4）RRCWM机制对任意d≥1在ex-post和ex-ante目标下均为近似比2，构成高维下平等主义目标的最优。

Conclusion: 作者的结论是：随机化对平等主义设施选址的帮助取决于维度——在低维（R、R^2）中，ex-ante评价框架下随机机制能显著突破确定性下界（R上达到1，R^2上达到1.598），且ex-ante与ex-post两种评价存在严格分离；但在高维中，随机化不再有任何实质收益，任何策略无偏机制都无法超越确定性独裁机制，因此RRCWM（对功利目标最优的机制）对平等主义目标在高维也是最优的。这一结果明确了随机策略无偏机制在公平性目标下的能力边界。

Abstract: We study the facility location mechanism design problem where $n$ strategic agents report locations in Euclidean space and the mechanism outputs a single facility location. Each agent's cost is its distance from the facility, and our objective is to minimize the egalitarian cost, i.e., the maximum agent cost, in a strategyproof way.
  The optimal deterministic approximation ratio is $2$, achieved by any dictator mechanism. We study the power of randomized strategyproof-in-expectation mechanisms. Prior work has focused on ex-post evaluation, defined as the expected maximum agent cost. We instead study ex-ante evaluation, defined as the maximum expected agent cost, which is naturally aligned with strategyproofness in expectation.
  We establish the following results:
  (1) Low dimensions: Strict ex-ante vs. ex-post separation. In $\mathbb{R}$, we give a simple strategyproof mechanism achieving the optimal ex-ante approximation ratio of $1$. In $\mathbb{R}^2$, we design the "Random Rotated Corner" mechanism, with ex-ante approximation ratio at most $1.598$, breaking the deterministic barrier. For the ex-post objective, we prove a lower bound of $1.605$, yielding a strict separation in $\mathbb{R}^2$.
  (2) High dimensions: Impossibility. In $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d \gg 1$, we show that no strategyproof-in-expectation mechanism improves on the deterministic dictator mechanism beyond $o_d(1)$. Thus neither ex-post nor ex-ante evaluation yields improved fairness guarantees in high dimensions. An implication is that the "Random Rotation Coordinate-Wise Median" (RRCWM), currently the best known mechanism for the utilitarian objective, is also best possible for the egalitarian objective in high dimension: we show it achieves an approximation ratio of $2$ for both ex-post and ex-ante objectives in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for every $d \ge 1$.

</details>


### [276] [Forging Self-Funded Marketplaces among Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14548)
*Yuan Deng,Vasilis Gkatzelis,Xizhi Tan,Grigoris Velegkas,Song Zuo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究"自筹资金市场"的机制设计问题：即设计激励策略性自利代理人为自建市场投入努力的机制，其中每位代理人的努力产生价值，但成本信息私有，且总支付不得超过总收入（预算平衡）。该问题将经典"预算可行机制设计"（总支付受固定预算B约束）推广为内生预算约束的情形。作者证明在最优第一基准下，任何诚实拍卖都无法达到有界近似比，即使在受限设置中也无法优于对数近似；随后提出一类序贯拍卖，其子博弈完美均衡可保证对数近似比。针对第一基准过强的问题，作者引入更能刻画市场厚度的最大化分额（MMS）基准，并给出一种序贯拍卖，其子博弈完美均衡可实现常数近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算可行机制设计假设存在一个预先给定的外部预算B，但在许多现实场景（如众包、去中心化平台、社区驱动的市场）中，市场本身由参与者的贡献（收入）自筹资金，没有外部预算。此时需要保证机制是自筹资金的，即总支付不超过参与者产生的总收入，同时每个代理人愿意付出努力。由于成本c_i是私有信息且可能大于或小于收入r_i，机制设计者面临激励兼容与预算平衡的双重挑战，因此需要新的机制设计框架与性能基准。

Method: 形式上，代理i选择努力x_i∈[0,1]，付出成本x_i·c_i（c_i对设计者未知），产生收入x_i·r_i；努力向量x产生价值v(x)。目标是最大化价值，同时满足个体理性（p_i≥x_i·c_i）与预算平衡（∑p_i≤∑x_i·r_i）。作者首先以无私有信息情形下的最优价值（第一最优基准）为参照，分析其近似极限并构造下界；随后设计一类序贯拍卖机制，并分析其子博弈完美均衡的近似保证。针对第一最优基准过强而导致的负结果，作者引入最大化分额（MMS）作为新基准，用以刻画市场"厚度"（即可被分配的总盈余规模），并基于此设计具有常数近似保证的拍卖机制。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）不存在能够以有界近似比逼近第一最优基准的诚实（truthful）拍卖；（2）即使在受限设置下，任何拍卖的近似比也不可能优于对数级别；（3）所提出的序贯拍卖类在子博弈完美均衡下能实现该第一最优基准的对数近似保证；（4）在最大化分额（MMS）基准下，作者给出的拍卖机制其子博弈完美均衡可实现常数近似比，从而在更贴近市场厚度现实的基准下获得显著更强的保障。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自筹资金市场中第一最优基准的极端严苛性：在不依赖预先给定预算的情况下，诚实机制在最优基准下至多只能达到对数级保证，这源于成本私有信息与预算平衡的内在张力。通过引入最大化分额（MMS）这一更能捕捉市场厚度的替代基准，作者展示了序贯拍卖机制可以实现常数近似这一强得多的理论保证，为设计实用的自筹资金市场机制提供了可行的方向与理论依据。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of designing mechanisms that incentivize strategic agents to form self-funded marketplaces. In our model, if agent $i$ exerts effort $x_i\in [0,1]$, they incur a cost of $x_i\cdot c_i$ (where $c_i$ is unknown to the mechanism designer) and they generate revenue $x_i\cdot r_i$; crucially, $c_i$ can be greater or smaller than $r_i$. Each effort profile $\mathbf{x}$ yields value $v(\mathbf{x})$ and the objective is to choose an effort vector that maximizes the value while ensuring that every agent $i$ receives a payment $p_i\geq x_i\cdot c_i$ and that $\mathbf{x}$ is budget-balanced, i.e., $\sum_{i} p_i \leq \sum_{i} x_i\cdot r_i$. This problem generalizes the well-studied budget-feasible mechanism design problem, where the requirement is that $\sum_{i} p_i \leq B$ for some predetermined budget $B$.
  To evaluate the performance of such mechanisms, we first consider the first-best benchmark (the optimal value achievable in the absence of any private information) and show that no truthful auction can achieve a bounded approximation of this benchmark. Also, even in restricted settings, no auction can achieve better than a logarithmic approximation. We complement these results by proposing a class of sequential auctions whose subgame perfect equilibria guarantee a logarithmic approximation of this benchmark. We then introduce an alternative benchmark, the maximin share (MMS), that better captures the thickness of the market and we provide an auction whose subgame perfect equilibria achieve a constant approximation of this benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

### [277] [Strand-based Hairstyle Generation via Large Reconstruction and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13679)
*Conghui Hao,Tao Huang,Yuefan Shen,Tongtong Wang,Zhongtian Zheng,Kui Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需任务专属训练数据、融合大重建模型(LRM)、大多模态模型(LMM)与传统几何处理的自动化流水线，可从单视角图像生成生产级、基于发丝的头发几何，在数分钟内重建出视觉上令人信服的直发、卷发、短发、长发以及发髻和马尾等复杂发型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产管线中高质量的发丝级发型制作严重依赖熟练美术师和耗时的手工建模，成本高且难以规模化。已有的基于学习的方法虽推动了图像驱动发丝重建，但通常需要大规模多样化的训练数据，难以泛化到发髻、马尾等复杂结构发型，且其表征往往与基于发丝的建模、编辑和仿真流程不直接兼容。

Method: 提出一种自动化的组合式流水线，将三类技术协同使用：大重建模型(LRMs)用于三维几何重建、大多模态模型(LMMs)提供语义理解与先验，以及经典几何处理方法用于细化与规整，从而从单视图图像生成高质量的发丝级几何；整个过程无需针对特定任务的训练或数据采集。

Result: 该方法能够覆盖直发、卷发、短发、长发以及马尾、发髻等具有挑战性的结构化发型，在数分钟内生成视觉上富有说服力且生产可用的发丝级重建结果，展示了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需特定任务训练的前提下兼顾了质量、速度与通用性，能够很好地集成到现代生产流程中，为单视图发丝重建提供了一条实用且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Creating high-quality strand-based hairstyles in current production pipelines remains heavily dependent on skilled artists and time-consuming manual authoring, making it costly and difficult to scale. Existing learning-based methods have advanced image-driven hair reconstruction, but typically require large, diverse training datasets, struggle to generalize to complex styles such as buns and ponytails, and often operate in representations that are not directly compatible with strand-based modeling, editing, and simulation. We present a novel automatic pipeline that combines the capabilities of Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs), Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), and classical geometry processing to generate high-quality strand-based hairstyles from single-view images. Our approach produces detailed, production-ready strand geometry without task-specific training or data collection and can handle a wide variety of hairstyles, including straight and curly hair, short and long styles, and challenging structured configurations such as ponytails and buns. Across this diverse set of examples, our method generates visually compelling strand-level reconstructions within only a few minutes, making it well-suited for integration into modern digital human workflows.

</details>


### [278] [Uncertainty-Aware Jacobi Set Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14409)
*Daniel Klötzl,Daniel Weiskopf*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向不确定性的Jacobi集计算方法。Jacobi集是描述两个标量场梯度对齐关系的拓扑描述符，常用于多场可视化。作者在现有基于三角网格边的Jacobi集识别方法基础上，引入基于多元正态分布的空间相干不确定性模型，通过解析传播不确定性，将原始Jacobi集计算推广到不确定输入，并设计了叠加式可视化映射以同时展示Jacobi集及其多种不确定性信息。方法在解析数据集上通过蒙特卡洛方法验证，并应用于流体动力学和气象集合两个用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际科学数据（如流体动力学、气象数据）往往带有不确定性，而传统Jacobi集计算假设输入为确定值，无法反映这种不确定性；同时现有方法在不确定输入下的可靠性缺乏保证，因此需要一种能感知不确定性并传播到拓扑描述符的计算方法。

Method: 采用并重构现有基于三角剖分边识别的Jacobi集边计算方法；构建基于多元正态分布、空间相干的标量场对不确定性模型；对Jacobi集计算过程进行解析不确定性传播；设计专用的叠加式视觉映射，将Jacobi集与不同维度的不确定性信息（如位置不确定性、拓扑存在概率等）联合展示。

Result: 通过解析数据集上的蒙特卡洛模拟验证了不确定性模型和不确定感知方法的正确性；并在流体动力学和气象集合两个实际用例中展示了方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够将原始Jacobi集计算提升到不确定输入层面，为多场可视化中的拓扑特征分析提供了确定性的可靠性度量，兼顾解析计算的效率与蒙特卡洛验证的准确性。

Abstract: We present an uncertainty-aware Jacobi set computation method. In general, Jacobi sets are topological descriptors that capture the gradient alignments of two scalar fields, as, e.g., used for multi-field visualization. We adopt and reformulate an existing computational approach that relies on an edge-based identification of Jacobi set edges on a given triangulation. Our extension to uncertainty visualization builds upon a versatile, spatially coherent uncertainty model for pairs of scalar fields based on multivariate normal distributions. We propagate the uncertainty analytically, thereby lifting the original Jacobi set computation to uncertain inputs. Furthermore, we present an overlay of visual mappings specifically designed to show the Jacobi sets along with different facets of uncertainty information. Both the uncertainty model and uncertainty-aware method are validated against a Monte Carlo approach on an analytic dataset and applied to two use cases from fluid dynamics and weather ensembles.

</details>
